+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07a section 6, Gate W0 (the census agent; new in 07a): with the parent's
+AdaptiveLevin/levin_quadrature.py installed in tree h, is the code that executes the parent's? Read only.
+
+1. Every file that differs between the parent dadd63a0 and h (4bf50d34) outside docs/ and prompts/ is listed
+   (git diff --name-only), with what it is.
+2. LiouvilleGreen/bessel_phase.py and LiouvilleGreen/three_bessel_integrals.py: the module at dadd63a0 (git show) and
+   h's file are parsed with ast, every docstring removed (the first statement of the module and of each class,
+   function and async function body, when it is a string constant; a body left empty gets ``pass``), and ast.dump of
+   the two compared. Equal means the two compile to the same code apart from ``__doc__``. The removed docstrings that
+   differ are listed by their node.
+3. Whether any code in h reads ``__doc__`` (a grep over the tree's .py files outside tests, docs and prompts), since a
+   docstring then could reach a computed value.
+
+Writes OUT_TXT (provenance header, then the findings). Exits 1 unless both modules are AST-equal.
+
+Usage: python -B ast_lg_check_07a.py OUT_TXT
+"""
+
+import ast
+import subprocess
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+from prov import H_SHA, H_TREE, PARENT_SHA_07A, header, sha256  # noqa: E402
+
+MODULES = ("LiouvilleGreen/bessel_phase.py", "LiouvilleGreen/three_bessel_integrals.py")
+
+
+def git(*args):
+    return subprocess.run(["git", "--no-optional-locks", "-C", str(H_TREE), *args], capture_output=True, text=True,
+                          check=True).stdout
+
+
+def strip_docstrings(tree):
+    removed = []
+    for node in ast.walk(tree):
+        if isinstance(node, (ast.Module, ast.ClassDef, ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
+            body = node.body
+            if body and isinstance(body[0], ast.Expr) and isinstance(body[0].value, ast.Constant) and isinstance(
+                    body[0].value.value, str):
+                name = getattr(node, "name", "<module>")
+                removed.append((type(node).__name__, name, body[0].value.value))
+                node.body = body[1:] or [ast.Pass()]
+    return tree, removed
+
+
+def main():
+    out_p = Path(sys.argv[1])
+    lines = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), "levin-phase-floor 07a: Gate W0, the parent's code in tree h (AST check)",
+                   [H_TREE / m for m in MODULES])
+    ok = True
+    names = git("diff", "--name-only", PARENT_SHA_07A, H_SHA, "--", ".", ":!docs", ":!prompts").split()
+    lines.append(f"# files differing between {PARENT_SHA_07A[:8]} and {H_SHA[:8]} outside docs/ and prompts/: {len(names)}")
+    for n in names:
+        role = ("replaced in the K0 run by the parent's file (variant_loader)" if n == "AdaptiveLevin/levin_quadrature.py"
+                else "a test module (not imported by the harness)" if "/tests/" in n
+                else "docstrings only: AST checked below" if n in MODULES else "UNEXPECTED")
+        ok &= role != "UNEXPECTED"
+        lines.append(f"  {n}: {role}")
+    for m in MODULES:
+        src_p = git("show", f"{PARENT_SHA_07A}:{m}")
+        src_h = (H_TREE / m).read_text()
+        tp, rp = strip_docstrings(ast.parse(src_p))
+        th, rh = strip_docstrings(ast.parse(src_h))
+        eq = ast.dump(tp, include_attributes=False) == ast.dump(th, include_attributes=False)
+        ok &= eq
+        lines.append(f"# {m}: sha256 at {PARENT_SHA_07A[:8]} (git show) "
+                     f"{__import__('hashlib').sha256(src_p.encode()).hexdigest()}, in h {sha256(H_TREE / m)}; "
+                     f"text equal {src_p == src_h}; AST without docstrings equal: {eq}")
+        dp = {(k, n): s for k, n, s in rp}
+        dh = {(k, n): s for k, n, s in rh}
+        diff = sorted(k for k in set(dp) | set(dh) if dp.get(k) != dh.get(k))
+        lines.append(f"  docstrings removed: {len(rp)} at the parent, {len(rh)} in h; differing: {diff}")
+    grep = subprocess.run(["grep", "-rn", "--include=*.py", "__doc__", "."], cwd=str(H_TREE), capture_output=True,
+                          text=True).stdout.splitlines()
+    grep = [g for g in grep if not g.startswith(("./docs/", "./prompts/")) and "/tests/" not in g]
+    lines.append(f"# '__doc__' read in h's .py files outside docs/, prompts/ and tests: {len(grep)} lines")
+    lines += [f"  {g}" for g in grep[:40]]
+    lines.append(f"# verdict: {'HOLDS' if ok else 'FAILS'}")
+    out_p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    out_p.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
+    print("\n".join(lines))
+    print(f"wrote {out_p} sha256 {sha256(out_p)}")
+    return 0 if ok else 1
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    sys.exit(main())

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07a section 6: the census's cost projection, from 07's census and from the K0 units' timings
+at h and at the parent. New in 07a, after 07's census_projection_07.py (a record, not edited), whose model it keeps with
+07's census in 06m's place. Read only.
+
+- **A unit's time** is what its worker spends on it outside the producer rebuild: ``wall_s`` plus every post-timed
+  record's seconds (``scan_s``, ``scan_07_s``, ``w3.seconds``, ``deep_lines.seconds``, ``cc_floor_s``,
+  ``declared_06h_s``, ``checked_06g_s``, ``analytic_06h.seconds``), each missing one counted 0. ``rebuild_s`` is left out
+  of the ratio (a K0 worker runs a few of its k's units, a census worker 1,160) and 07's census rebuild time is added
+  back unchanged.
+- **The ratio** rho(class) = sum over the class's K0 units of the time at h / the same sum in 07's census, per class
+  (rtol, in the 720 or not; the 720 from 06k's K0 list's ``in_720``, checked against 07's census scan at 1e-7).
+- **The projection:** P = 07's census wall (22,708 s, six workers) x (sum over 07's 18,560 units of rho(class) x its
+  time, plus 07's rebuild time) / (sum of 07's times, plus 07's rebuild time); the same with the parent's K0 timings (the
+  harness's added diagnostics at 07's code); and 07's own K0 stage wall at its tree g (359 s) against this run's K0 stage
+  walls.
+
+Writes OUT_JSON and prints it. Usage: python -B census_projection_07a.py K0_H_DIR K0_PARENT_DIR OUT_JSON
+"""
+
+import json
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+from prov import CHECKPOINT07_SHA256, H_TREE, RUN07, header, sha256  # noqa: E402
+
+WALL_07 = 22708.0  # 07's census wall (docs/levin-phase-floor/07/census/log.md)
+K0_STAGE_07_G = 359.0820007324219  # 07's 61_k0_run_g (docs/levin-phase-floor/07/census/out/k0_results.json)
+K0_UNITS = H_TREE / "docs" / "levin-phase-floor" / "06l" / "out" / "k0" / "units.json"
+
+
+def t_unit(d):
+    t = d.get("wall_s") or 0.0
+    for k in ("scan_s", "scan_07_s", "cc_floor_s", "declared_06h_s", "checked_06g_s"):
+        t += d.get(k) or 0.0
+    for k in ("w3", "deep_lines", "analytic_06h"):
+        t += (d.get(k) or {}).get("seconds") or 0.0
+    return t
+
+
+def main():
+    h_dir, p_dir, out_p = Path(sys.argv[1]), Path(sys.argv[2]), Path(sys.argv[3])
+    ck = RUN07 / "checkpoint.jsonl"
+    hdr = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), "levin-phase-floor 07a: the census's cost projection",
+                 [ck, K0_UNITS, h_dir / "units.jsonl", p_dir / "units.jsonl"])
+    if sha256(ck) != CHECKPOINT07_SHA256:
+        raise SystemExit("07's checkpoint has another SHA-256")
+    k0 = {u["uid"]: u for u in json.loads(K0_UNITS.read_text())["units"]}
+    rows720 = {u["row"] for u in k0.values() if u["in_720"]}
+    t07 = {}
+    sp_rows = set()
+    with open(ck) as f:
+        for line in f:
+            r = json.loads(line)
+            d = r["data"]
+            t07[r["unit"]] = (t_unit(d), d["rtol"], d["row"], d["wall_s"], d.get("rebuild_s") or 0.0)
+            if d["rtol"] == "1e-7" and any(s and s["n_sign_changes"] > 0 for s in d["scan"] or []):
+                sp_rows.add(d["row"])
+    if not rows720 <= sp_rows:
+        raise SystemExit("a K0 unit marked in_720 is not a row of the 720")
+
+    def load(dirp):
+        return {"g-" + json.loads(l)["unit"].split("-", 1)[1]: json.loads(l)["data"] for l in open(dirp / "units.jsonl")}
+
+    H, P = load(h_dir), load(p_dir)
+
+    def cls(uid):
+        _t, rt, row, _w, _rb = t07[uid]
+        return (rt, row in sp_rows)
+
+    out = {"header": hdr, "wall_07_s": WALL_07, "n_rows_720": len(sp_rows), "classes": {}}
+    for name, X in (("h", H), ("parent", P)):
+        rho, num = {}, {}
+        for uid, d in X.items():
+            c = cls(uid)
+            a = num.setdefault(c, [0.0, 0.0, 0, 0.0, 0.0])
+            a[0] += t_unit(d)
+            a[1] += t07[uid][0]
+            a[2] += 1
+            a[3] += d["wall_s"]
+            a[4] += t07[uid][3]
+        for c, (tx, t7, n, wx, w7) in num.items():
+            rho[c] = tx / t7
+            out["classes"].setdefault(f"{c[0]}|720={c[1]}", {})[name] = {"n": n, "time_k0": tx, "time_07": t7,
+                                                                       "rho_time": tx / t7, "rho_wall_s": wx / w7}
+        rb7 = sum(v[4] for v in t07.values())
+        tot7 = sum(v[0] for v in t07.values()) + rb7
+        totx = sum(v[0] * rho[cls(u)] for u, v in t07.items()) + rb7
+        out[f"projection_{name}_s"] = WALL_07 * totx / tot7
+        out[f"ratio_{name}"] = totx / tot7
+    res = json.loads((HERE.parent / "out" / "k0_results.json").read_text())["results"]
+    walls = {r["name"]: r["wall_s"] for r in res}
+    out["k0_stage_walls_s"] = {"07_k0_g": K0_STAGE_07_G, "parent": walls.get("41_k0_run_parent"), "h": walls.get("61_k0_run_h")}
+    out["projection_from_k0_stage_wall_h_s"] = WALL_07 * walls["61_k0_run_h"] / K0_STAGE_07_G
+    out["sum_time_07_s"] = sum(v[0] for v in t07.values())
+    out["sum_rebuild_07_s"] = sum(v[4] for v in t07.values())
+    out_p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    out_p.write_text(json.dumps(out, indent=1))
+    print(json.dumps({k: v for k, v in out.items() if k != "header"}, indent=1))
+    print(f"wrote {out_p} sha256 {sha256(out_p)}")
+    return 0
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    sys.exit(main())

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07a section 6, the user's references: 07's census check_captures.py (a record, not edited),
+changed only where marked "prompt 07a": the census is named on the command line, AGAINST ``h`` (07a's census, the
+run directory RUN_H, units h-...) or ``07`` (07's census, prov.RUN07, units g-...; for the captures made with the
+parent's module); the call record's keys ``route_07a`` and ``route_07a_post`` (07a's diagnostics, absent from 07's
+records) are not compared, and the harness check also allows the files 07a changes (prov.py, worker.py,
+run_census.py, variant_loader.py), each one's two hashes recorded; the title names 07a.
+Usage: PYTHONPATH=<tree h>:<scripts> python -B check_captures_07a.py CAPTURE_JSON SELECTION_JSON OUT_JSON h|07 [RUN_H]
+07's text follows.
+
+levin-phase-floor prompt 07 section 6, V4: 06m's census copy of 06l's check_captures.py (a record), changed only
+where marked "prompt 07": the census the captures are checked against is 07's census g (refs_common.CENSUS07, its
+checkpoint SHA-256 checked) in place of 06g's, and the title names 07. Every call-record key but ``elapsed`` (and the
+route keys) is compared, 07's ``guard_07`` included; every region-array key, 07's flags included. 06l's text follows.
+
+levin-phase-floor prompt 06l: copied from 06k's scripts/check_captures.py (a record; its copy is in
+state/scripts_06k_reference/), changed only where marked "prompt 06l": the call record's new key ``route_06l`` is
+not compared, as ``route_06k`` is not; and (after run levin-phase-floor-06l-k0partL-20261105T091942, where it
+failed every capture on this alone) the harness check also allows ``run_census.py``, which worker.py hashes in SCRIPTS
+but does not import, and which 06k changed (``--variant`` and the backstop) after its own captures ran; its two
+hashes are recorded with the others'. 06k's text follows.
+
+levin-phase-floor prompt 06k: copied from 06j's scripts/check_captures.py (a record, not edited; its copy is in
+state/scripts_06j_reference/), changed only where marked "prompt 06k": the harness check allows the SCRIPTS files
+06k changes or adds (prov.py, its tree and paths; worker.py, the variant loading and the route record;
+variant_loader.py, new), each one's two hashes recorded; and the call record's new key ``route_06k`` (None when no
+variant is loaded, as in the captures) is not compared. 06j's text follows.
+
+levin-phase-floor prompt 06j: copied from 06g's references scripts/check_captures.py (a record, not edited; its
+copy is in state/scripts_06g_reference/), changed only where marked "prompt 06j": the harness check compares
+worker.py's SCRIPTS hashes file by file, every one equal to the census record's except prov.py, which 06j
+changes in its tree and its paths (out/scripts_diff_against_06g.txt); prov.py's two hashes are recorded.
+06g's text follows.
+
+levin-phase-floor prompt 06g section 5, the references: every capture against the g census, bit for
+bit, beyond x1_capture.py's tuple. New in 06g.
+
+x1_capture.py already compares each captured call's value and abserr (hex), num_regions, max_depth,
+converged and label with the g census record (``capture_matches_census``). This script adds the
+regions, as the brief asks ("value, abserr and regions"):
+- **the region arrays.** The capture's worker re-ran the whole unit and wrote the unit's region
+  record (``regions_<worker>.pkl``, at the offset its emitted unit data gives, SHA-256 checked); the
+  census wrote the same record (the run directory's ``regions_*.pkl``, at the offset its checkpoint
+  record gives, SHA-256 checked). For the captured call (``regions`` for a group call,
+  ``other_regions`` for an analytic block, matched by index) every key is compared: arrays by
+  dtype, shape and bytes (so NaNs compare by bit pattern), anything else by equality;
+- **the harness.** The capture worker's ``scripts_sha256`` (worker.py's SCRIPTS) must equal the
+  census record's: the capture ran the census's harness byte for byte;
+- **the checkpoint call record.** The capture's emitted record of the call against the census's,
+  every key; ``elapsed`` (a wall time) is volatile and reported but not compared. Any other
+  difference is reported.
+A call whose value, abserr or regions differ is a stop (the brief: "A capture that does not
+reproduce its census record is a stop"); the script exits 1 if any does. Reads only.
+
+Usage: PYTHONPATH=<tree g>:<this directory> python -B check_captures.py CAPTURE_JSON SELECTION_JSON OUT_JSON
+"""
+
+import hashlib
+import json
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import numpy as np
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+import refs_common as RC  # noqa: E402
+from prov import header  # noqa: E402
+from prov import CHECKPOINT07_SHA256, RUN07  # noqa: E402  prompt 07a
+
+VOLATILE_CALL_KEYS = {"elapsed", "route_06k", "route_06l", "route_07a", "route_07a_post"}  # prompt 07a: route_07a*  # prompt 06k: route_06k, the new key; prompt 06l: route_06l
+
+
+def load_blob(path, offset, length, sha):
+    with open(path, "rb") as f:
+        f.seek(offset)
+        blob = f.read(length)
+    if hashlib.sha256(blob).hexdigest() != sha:
+        raise RuntimeError(f"{path}@{offset}: sha256 differs")
+    import ray.cloudpickle as cp  # the records were written by harness._GroupPickler
+
+    return cp.loads(blob)
+
+
+def same(a, b):
+    if isinstance(a, np.ndarray) or isinstance(b, np.ndarray):
+        a, b = np.asarray(a), np.asarray(b)
+        return a.dtype == b.dtype and a.shape == b.shape and a.tobytes() == b.tobytes()
+    if isinstance(a, dict) and isinstance(b, dict):
+        return a.keys() == b.keys() and all(same(a[k], b[k]) for k in a)
+    if isinstance(a, (list, tuple)) and isinstance(b, (list, tuple)):
+        return len(a) == len(b) and all(same(x, y) for x, y in zip(a, b))
+    if isinstance(a, float) and isinstance(b, float):
+        return a.hex() == b.hex() if a == a or b == b else repr(a) == repr(b)
+    return a == b
+
+
+def census_units_data(checkpoint, units):
+    """{unit: [its ``done`` records' data]} for ``units``, in one pass over the census checkpoint."""
+    out = {u: [] for u in units}
+    with open(checkpoint) as f:
+        for line in f:
+            head = line[:300]
+            if not any(f'"{u}"' in head for u in units):
+                continue
+            r = json.loads(line)
+            if r.get("unit") in out and r["data"].get("status") == "done":
+                out[r["unit"]].append(r["data"])
+    return out
+
+
+def capture_unit_data(wdir, uid):
+    for line in open(Path(wdir) / "emit.jsonl"):
+        m = json.loads(line)
+        if m["kind"] == "unit" and m["data"]["unit"] == uid:
+            return m["data"]
+    return None
+
+
+def region_entry(rec, kind, index):
+    key = "regions" if kind == "group" else "other_regions"
+    hits = [e for e in rec[key] if e["index"] == index]
+    return hits[0] if len(hits) == 1 else None
+
+
+def call_entry(data, kind, index):
+    key = "calls" if kind == "group" else "other_calls"
+    hits = [c for c in data[key] if c["index"] == index]
+    return hits[0] if len(hits) == 1 else None
+
+
+def main():
+    cap_p, sel_p, out_p = (Path(a) for a in sys.argv[1:4])
+    against = sys.argv[4]  # prompt 07a
+    if against == "07":
+        run06g = RUN07
+        ck = run06g / "checkpoint.jsonl"
+        if RC.sha256(ck) != CHECKPOINT07_SHA256:
+            raise SystemExit("07's census checkpoint has another SHA-256")
+        ukey = "g_unit"
+    elif against == "h":
+        run06g = Path(sys.argv[5])
+        ck = run06g / "checkpoint.jsonl"
+        ukey = "unit"
+    else:
+        raise SystemExit("AGAINST is h or 07")
+    for l in header(
+        Path(__file__).resolve(),
+        f"levin-phase-floor 07a: the reference captures against the census {against}, bit for bit (regions)",  # prompt 07a: the title
+        [cap_p, sel_p, ck],
+    ):
+        print(l)
+    sel = {(e["unit"], e["index"]): e for e in json.loads(sel_p.read_text())}
+    caps = json.loads(cap_p.read_text())
+    gdata = census_units_data(ck, {e[ukey] for e in sel.values()})  # prompt 07a
+    results = []
+    n_stop = 0
+    for c in caps:
+        e = sel[(c["unit"], c["index"])]
+        row = {
+            "sid": e["sid"],
+            "unit": c["unit"],
+            "index": c["index"],
+            "kind": e["kind"],
+        }
+        problems = []
+        if not c.get("capture_matches_census"):
+            problems.append(
+                "x1_capture: value/abserr/num_regions/max_depth/converged/label differ, or no capture"
+            )
+        cu = capture_unit_data(c["dir"], c["unit"]) if c.get("dir") else None
+        gu = gdata[e[ukey]]  # prompt 07a
+        if cu is None or len(gu) != 1:
+            problems.append(
+                f"unit data: capture {'absent' if cu is None else 'present'}, census done records {len(gu)}"
+            )
+        else:
+            gu = gu[0]
+            # the harness the capture ran is the census's, byte for byte (worker.py's SCRIPTS hashes)
+            # prompt 06j: every SCRIPTS file equal except prov.py (06j's tree and paths)
+            cs_, gs_ = cu.get("scripts_sha256") or {}, gu.get("scripts_sha256") or {}
+            row["scripts_sha256_differing"] = sorted(
+                k for k in set(cs_) | set(gs_) if cs_.get(k) != gs_.get(k)
+            )
+            row["prov_py_sha256"] = [cs_.get("prov.py"), gs_.get("prov.py")]
+            row["scripts_sha256_equal"] = set(row["scripts_sha256_differing"]) <= {"prov.py", "worker.py", "variant_loader.py", "run_census.py"}  # prompt 06k; prompt 06l: run_census.py
+            row["changed_scripts_sha256_06k"] = {k: [cs_.get(k), gs_.get(k)] for k in row["scripts_sha256_differing"]}  # prompt 06k
+            if not row["scripts_sha256_equal"]:
+                problems.append(
+                    "the capture's harness scripts differ from the census's (scripts_sha256)"
+                )
+            crec = load_blob(
+                Path(c["dir"]) / cu["pickle"],
+                cu["pickle_offset"],
+                cu["pickle_length"],
+                cu["pickle_record_sha256"],
+            )
+            grec = load_blob(
+                run06g / gu["pickle"],
+                gu["pickle_offset"],
+                gu["pickle_length"],
+                gu["pickle_record_sha256"],
+            )
+            cr = region_entry(crec, e["kind"], c["index"])
+            gr = region_entry(grec, e["kind"], c["index"])
+            if cr is None or gr is None:
+                problems.append("region entry missing")
+            else:
+                diff = sorted(
+                    k
+                    for k in set(cr) | set(gr)
+                    if k not in cr or k not in gr or not same(cr[k], gr[k])
+                )
+                row["region_keys_compared"] = sorted(set(cr) | set(gr))
+                row["region_keys_differing"] = diff
+                row["n_regions"] = int(len(gr["start"]))
+                if diff:
+                    problems.append(f"region arrays differ: {diff}")
+            cc = call_entry(cu, e["kind"], c["index"])
+            gc = call_entry(gu, e["kind"], c["index"])
+            if cc is None or gc is None:
+                problems.append("call record missing")
+            else:
+                diff = sorted(
+                    k
+                    for k in set(cc) | set(gc)
+                    if k not in VOLATILE_CALL_KEYS
+                    and (k not in cc or k not in gc or not same(cc[k], gc[k]))
+                )
+                row["call_keys_differing"] = diff
+                row["call_elapsed"] = [cc.get("elapsed"), gc.get("elapsed")]
+                if diff:
+                    row["call_differences"] = {k: [cc.get(k), gc.get(k)] for k in diff}
+        row["problems"] = problems
+        stop = bool(problems)
+        n_stop += stop
+        row["reproduces_census"] = not stop
+        results.append(row)
+        print(
+            f"{row['sid']} {row['unit']} #{row['index']} {row['kind']}: "
+            + (
+                "REPRODUCES (value, abserr, label, converged, regions bit for bit)"
+                if not stop
+                else "DOES NOT REPRODUCE: " + "; ".join(problems)
+            )
+            + (
+                f"; other call-record keys differing: {row.get('call_keys_differing')}"
+                if row.get("call_keys_differing")
+                else ""
+            )
+        )
+    Path(out_p).write_text(json.dumps(results, indent=1, default=str))
+    print(
+        f"{len(results) - n_stop} of {len(results)} captures reproduce the g census bit for bit; wrote {out_p} sha256 {RC.sha256(out_p)}"
+    )
+    return 0 if n_stop == 0 and len(results) == len(sel) else 1
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    sys.exit(main())

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07a section 6: units run by this harness against 07's census
+(levin-phase-floor-07-census-20261108T171137, checkpoint 03dfe786...), bit for bit, by 07's comparison with only 07a's
+new keys set aside. New in 07a, after 07's compare_07.py (a record, not edited), whose matching, evidence and diff it
+follows; the diff is compare_k0.diff (06l's, unchanged), and compare_k0's ``EXCLUDED`` (timing and provenance, 07's set;
+every key ending ``_s`` likewise) is extended at import by NEW_KEYS_07A below. 07's own new keys (``guard_07`` and the
+rest) are in both records and are compared. Read only.
+
+**The keys set aside, and why each** (NEW_KEYS_07A): every one is a key 07a's harness adds (worker.py, "prompt 07a");
+none occurs in 07's records (checked on every unit compared: ``new_keys_in_07_records`` must be 0).
+- ``route_07a`` (each call's summary; the pickle's per-call list): section 6's diagnostics;
+- ``route_07a_post`` (each call's summary; the pickle): the tally of this harness's own re-solves after the timed
+  section; ``route_07a_post_unit`` (unit): the same for 06h's analytic counterfactual;
+- ``instrument_07a`` (unit): whether the diagnostics ran.
+``phase_span`` (prompt 07a section 6: "the comparison with 07's census, which sets phase_span aside") is not a key of any
+record of 07's harness, at 07 or at h; every unit compared counts its occurrences as a dict key in both records
+(``phase_span_keys``: must be 0), so that setting it aside is shown to be vacuous rather than assumed.
+**Normalised, not excluded** (modes ``whole``, ``calls``): a unit run at h (``h-X``, tree ``h``) is compared under 07's
+names (``g-X``, ``g``), in the checkpoint record and the pickle.
+
+Modes:
+- ``whole RUN_DIR OUT_JSON``: Gate W0's second clause. Every unit of a run_k0.py run made in h with the parent's module
+  installed (variant ``parent``) compared whole with 07's unit. Module evidence: each unit's
+  ``AdaptiveLevin.levin_quadrature`` is the parent's file (state/variants/levin_quadrature_parent.py, 4e5da371...), counted
+  under the root ``variant``, every other module from h, none foreign; each worker's hello shows the variant installed and
+  bound (variant_loader's evidence all true, both production importers bound to it), the module's SHA-256 4e5da371..., h's
+  own file 4ea1948b... (not executed), h at its SHA and clean; each bye, no Ray.
+- ``calls RUN_DIR OUT_JSON``: per call, every Levin call (group ``calls`` and analytic ``other_calls``) of every unit of a
+  run at h (run_k0.py's ``units.jsonl``, or the census's ``checkpoint.jsonl``) against 07's call. Each call is classed:
+  - ``window``: a stationary-point window at 07 or at h (guard_07's ``n_windows`` > 0 in either record), with
+    ``window_both``, ``window_07_only`` and ``window_h_only`` apart;
+  - otherwise by section 6's diagnostics at h (route_07a): ``own`` if a solve of the class ``own`` (its span a region the
+    driver made) changed its C2 route or LU choice; ``aux_only`` if only solves of the auxiliary classes did (a trust-check
+    quarter, a comparison half, a fallback half, other); ``untouched`` if none did;
+  - ``no_diagnostics`` if route_07a is missing or in error at h (a fault of the harness, reported).
+  Every call is diffed against 07's on everything that belongs to it (compare_07.py's _call_parts: its summary, its
+  entries of the unit's ``scan``, ``deep_lines``, ``deep_lines_06e``, every pickle list entry carrying its index, and a
+  1e-7 group call's entry of the pickle's ``scan``), and each differing path is put in one category:
+  - ``location``: guard_07's ``location_skipped_phases``, ``unlocated_minima`` and ``n_unlocated_minima`` (summary and
+    pickle): the location's own report, which 07a section 1.1 item 1 changes (Rule A's entries carried a variation; 07a's
+    carry a reason, and a phase Rule A skipped is now scanned for minima);
+  - ``harness``: this harness's own re-solves after the timed section, which re-solve spans the driver may never have
+    visited, under h's routing: the summary's ``cc_floor`` and ``declared_06h``, the unit's ``deep_lines`` and
+    ``deep_lines_06e`` lines, the pickle's ``w3``, ``deep_lines``, ``deep_lines_06e``, ``cc_floor_terms`` and
+    ``declared_terms_06h``;
+  - ``call``: everything else (the call's value, abserr, converged, regions, leaves, its trust checks' and premise checks'
+    records, its guard_07 windows and counts, its scans, ...).
+  A unit all of whose calls are of the W1 classes under a reading is checked for ``result.total`` and
+  ``result.total_abserr`` (hex) under that reading, and is diffed whole with its differing paths categorised (the unit's
+  ``analytic_06h.parent_counterfactual`` and ``w3`` are ``harness``).
+  Writes OUT_JSON (provenance, counts by class and category, per-unit summaries) and OUT_JSON's ``.calls.jsonl`` beside it
+  (one line per call: unit, kind, index, class, windows at 07 and h, route_07a counts, the differing categories and the
+  first differing paths, and value, abserr and converged at 07 and at h, hex).
+- ``det RUN_DIR_A RUN_DIR_B OUT_JSON``: Gate W0's fourth clause. The units two run_k0.py runs share, compared whole with
+  compare_k0's EXCLUDED alone (timing and provenance): every key compared, 07a's included.
+- ``instr RUN_DIR_ON RUN_DIR_OFF OUT_JSON``: Gate W0's third clause. The units of a run with the diagnostics on and one
+  with them off, compared whole with compare_k0's EXCLUDED and NEW_KEYS_07A set aside; the run ON must carry route_07a on
+  every call and the run OFF on none.
+
+Floats are compared by bit pattern (``float.hex``; NaN by repr), arrays by dtype, shape and bytes. Prints a summary.
+Exits 1 unless every comparison the mode's verdict rests on is equal and every evidence check holds (``calls`` exits 0
+whatever the classes show: it describes, and the scoring reads it).
+
+Usage: PYTHONPATH=<tree h>:<scripts> python -B compare_07a.py whole|calls RUN_DIR OUT_JSON [--processes N]
+       PYTHONPATH=<tree h>:<scripts> python -B compare_07a.py det|instr RUN_DIR_A RUN_DIR_B OUT_JSON
+"""
+
+import argparse
+import hashlib
+import json
+import multiprocessing as mp
+import re
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+import compare_k0 as CK  # noqa: E402  (06l's, unchanged)
+from prov import (CHECKPOINT07_SHA256, H_TREE, LQ_SHA256_07AH, LQ_SHA256_07APARENT, PARENT_VARIANT_07A, RUN07,  # noqa: E402
+                  TREES, header, sha256)
+
+EXCLUDED_07 = frozenset(CK.EXCLUDED)  # 07's set (06m's), as compare_k0.py has it
+NEW_KEYS_07A = frozenset({"route_07a", "route_07a_post", "route_07a_post_unit", "instrument_07a"})
+INDEX = HERE.parent / "state" / "index_07.json"
+LOC_RE = re.compile(r"\.guard_07(\[\d+\])?\.(location_skipped_phases|unlocated_minima|n_unlocated_minima)\b")
+HARNESS_RE = re.compile(r"\.summary\[\d+\]\.(cc_floor|declared_06h)\b|\.data\.(deep_lines|deep_lines_06e)\b|"
+                        r"\.pickle\.(w3|deep_lines|deep_lines_06e|cc_floor_terms|declared_terms_06h)\b")
+UNIT_HARNESS_RE = re.compile(r"^data\.(analytic_06h\.parent_counterfactual|w3|deep_lines|deep_lines_06e)\b|"
+                             r"^data\.(calls|other_calls)\[\d+\]\.(cc_floor|declared_06h)\b|"
+                             r"^pickle\.(w3|deep_lines|deep_lines_06e|cc_floor_terms|declared_terms_06h)\b")
+UNIT_LOC_RE = re.compile(r"\.guard_07\.(location_skipped_phases|unlocated_minima|n_unlocated_minima)\b|"
+                         r"^pickle\.guard_07\[\d+\]\.(location_skipped_phases|unlocated_minima)\b")
+
+
+def set_excluded(with_new):
+    """compare_k0.excluded() reads compare_k0.EXCLUDED at call time."""
+    CK.EXCLUDED = set(EXCLUDED_07 | (NEW_KEYS_07A if with_new else frozenset()))
+
+
+def norm(p):
+    return re.sub(r"\[\d+\]", "[]", p)
+
+
+def span_key(x_span):
+    return tuple(e["hex"] if isinstance(e, dict) else float(e).hex() for e in x_span)
+
+
+def call_keys(d):
+    return [(kind, c["index"], c["notify_label"], span_key(c["x_span"]))
+            for kind, key in (("group", "calls"), ("analytic", "other_calls")) for c in d.get(key) or []]
+
+
+def region_keys(p):
+    return [(kind, r["index"], span_key(r["x_span"]))
+            for kind, key in (("group", "regions"), ("analytic", "other_regions")) for r in p.get(key) or []]
+
+
+def count_keys(x, names):
+    """How many times a name in names occurs as a dict key in x."""
+    n = 0
+    if isinstance(x, dict):
+        for k, v in x.items():
+            n += (k in names) + count_keys(v, names)
+    elif isinstance(x, (list, tuple)):
+        for v in x:
+            n += count_keys(v, names)
+    return n
+
+
+def load_07(ent):
+    with open(RUN07 / "checkpoint.jsonl", "rb") as f:
+        f.seek(ent["offset"])
+        line = f.read(ent["length"])
+    if hashlib.sha256(line).hexdigest() != ent["line_sha256"]:
+        raise SystemExit(f"07's checkpoint line sha256 differs at offset {ent['offset']}")
+    g = json.loads(line)["data"]
+    gp = ent["pickle"]
+    grec = CK.load_pickle(RUN07 / gp[0], gp[1], gp[2], gp[3])
+    return g, grec
+
+
+def load_mine(d, pdir):
+    return CK.load_pickle(Path(pdir) / d["pickle"], d["pickle_offset"], d["pickle_length"], d["pickle_record_sha256"])
+
+
+def uid_07(uid):
+    return "g-" + uid.split("-", 1)[1]
+
+
+def by_index(lst):
+    out = {}
+    for e in lst or []:
+        if isinstance(e, dict) and "index" in e:
+            out.setdefault(e["index"], []).append(e)
+    return out
+
+
+def normalise(uid, d, prec):
+    """Copies of a unit's record and pickle record with h's unit and tree under 07's names."""
+    d2, prec2 = dict(d), dict(prec)
+    normalised = {}
+    for name, rec in (("data", d2), ("pickle", prec2)):
+        if rec.get("unit") == uid and uid != uid_07(uid):
+            rec["unit"] = uid_07(uid)
+            normalised[f"{name}.unit"] = [uid, uid_07(uid)]
+    if d2.get("tree") != "g":
+        normalised["data.tree"] = [d2.get("tree"), "g"]
+        d2["tree"] = "g"
+    return d2, prec2, normalised
+
+
+def unit_evidence(d, variant):
+    mods = d.get("modules") or {}
+    lq_file = str(PARENT_VARIANT_07A.resolve()) if variant else str(H_TREE / "AdaptiveLevin" / "levin_quadrature.py")
+    roots = mods.get("counts_by_root") or {}
+    return {
+        "lq_file_is_expected": (mods.get("files") or {}).get("AdaptiveLevin.levin_quadrature") == lq_file,
+        "all_in_tree": bool(mods.get("all_in_tree")),
+        "no_foreign": not mods.get("foreign"),
+        "variant_root_as_expected": ("variant" in roots) == bool(variant),
+    }
+
+
+def one_whole(args):
+    """Mode whole: one unit (at the parent's module, in h) against 07's, whole (07a's new keys set aside)."""
+    uid, line, pdir, ent = args
+    set_excluded(True)
+    d = json.loads(line)["data"]
+    row = {"unit": uid, "unit_07": uid_07(uid), "status": d["status"]}
+    g, grec = load_07(ent)
+    row["status_07"] = g["status"]
+    if d["status"] != "done" or g["status"] != "done":
+        row.update(ok=False, differing=["status"], excl_present=[], excl_differing=[])
+        return row
+    prec = load_mine(d, pdir)
+    d2, prec2, normalised = normalise(uid, d, prec)
+    out = []
+    matched = call_keys(d2) == call_keys(g) and region_keys(prec2) == region_keys(grec)
+    if not matched:
+        out.append(("match", False, "calls or region records do not match"))
+    CK.diff(d2, g, "data", out)
+    CK.diff(prec2, grec, "pickle", out)
+    ev = unit_evidence(d, variant=True)
+    differing = sorted({p for p, e, _ in out if not e})
+    row.update(
+        ok=matched and not differing and all(ev.values()),
+        normalised=normalised,
+        differing=differing[:200],
+        detail=[list(x) for x in out if not x[1]][:50],
+        excl_differing=sorted({norm(p) for p, e, _ in out if e}),
+        new_keys_in_07_records=count_keys(g, NEW_KEYS_07A) + count_keys(grec, NEW_KEYS_07A),
+        phase_span_keys=count_keys(d, {"phase_span"}) + count_keys(prec, {"phase_span"}) + count_keys(g, {"phase_span"})
+        + count_keys(grec, {"phase_span"}),
+        n_calls=len(call_keys(d2)),
+        n_group_calls=sum(1 for k in call_keys(d2) if k[0] == "group"),
+        n_regions=len(region_keys(prec2)),
+        n_regions_returned=int(sum(len(r.get("start", [])) for r in (prec.get("regions") or []) + (prec.get("other_regions") or []))),
+        evidence=ev,
+    )
+    row["ok"] = row["ok"] and row["new_keys_in_07_records"] == 0 and row["phase_span_keys"] == 0
+    return row
+
+
+def _call_parts(d, prec, kind, i, pos):
+    """Everything that belongs to call i of a unit record d and its pickle record prec (compare_07.py's, unchanged)."""
+    key = "calls" if kind == "group" else "other_calls"
+    parts = {"summary": [c for c in (d.get(key) or []) if c["index"] == i], "data": {}, "pickle": {}}
+    parts["data"]["scan"] = [e for e in (d.get("scan") or []) if isinstance(e, dict) and e.get("index") == i]
+    for k in ("deep_lines", "deep_lines_06e"):
+        parts["data"][k] = {"lines": [e for e in ((d.get(k) or {}).get("lines") or []) if e.get("index") == i]}
+    for k in sorted(prec):
+        v = prec.get(k)
+        if k == "scan":
+            if kind == "group" and isinstance(v, list):
+                parts["pickle"]["scan"] = [v[pos]] if 0 <= pos < len(v) else ["<missing>"]
+            continue
+        if isinstance(v, list):
+            parts["pickle"][k] = by_index(v).get(i, [])
+    return parts
+
+
+def category(path):
+    if LOC_RE.search(path):
+        return "location"
+    if HARNESS_RE.search(path):
+        return "harness"
+    return "call"
+
+
+def unit_category(path):
+    if UNIT_LOC_RE.search(path):
+        return "location"
+    if UNIT_HARNESS_RE.search(path):
+        return "harness"
+    return "call"
+
+
+def fx_of(s, k):
+    v = s.get(k) if s else None
+    return v.get("hex") if isinstance(v, dict) else v
+
+
+def one_calls(args):
+    """Mode calls: every call against 07's, classed and diffed by category."""
+    uid, line, pdir, ent = args
+    set_excluded(True)
+    d = json.loads(line)["data"]
+    row = {"unit": uid, "unit_07": uid_07(uid), "status": d["status"], "rtol": d.get("rtol")}
+    g, grec = load_07(ent)
+    row["status_07"] = g["status"]
+    if d["status"] != "done" or g["status"] != "done":
+        row.update(ok=False, problem="status", calls=[])
+        return row
+    prec = load_mine(d, pdir)
+    d2, prec2, normalised = normalise(uid, d, prec)
+    ck_m, ck_g = call_keys(d2), call_keys(g)
+    row["matched"] = ck_m == ck_g and region_keys(prec2) == region_keys(grec)
+    if not row["matched"]:
+        row.update(ok=False, problem=f"calls {len(ck_m)}/{len(ck_g)} or region records do not match", calls=[])
+        return row
+    pos_of = {c["index"]: j for j, c in enumerate(d2.get("calls") or [])}
+    pos_of_g = {c["index"]: j for j, c in enumerate(g.get("calls") or [])}
+    calls = []
+    for kind, i, label, _span in ck_m:
+        key = "calls" if kind == "group" else "other_calls"
+        s = next(c for c in d2[key] if c["index"] == i)
+        s7 = next(c for c in g[key] if c["index"] == i)
+        g7h, g77 = s.get("guard_07") or {}, s7.get("guard_07") or {}
+        r7a = s.get("route_07a")
+        win07 = bool(g77.get("n_windows"))
+        winh = bool(g7h.get("n_windows"))
+        if win07 or winh:
+            cls = "window_both" if (win07 and winh) else ("window_07_only" if win07 else "window_h_only")
+        elif not isinstance(r7a, dict) or "error" in r7a:
+            cls = "no_diagnostics"
+        elif r7a["own_differs"]:
+            cls = "own"
+        elif r7a["aux_differs"]:
+            cls = "aux_only"
+        else:
+            cls = "untouched"
+        out = []
+        a = _call_parts(d2, prec2, kind, i, pos_of.get(i, -1))
+        b = _call_parts(g, grec, kind, i, pos_of_g.get(i, -1))
+        CK.diff(a, b, f"call[{kind}:{i}]", out)
+        diffs = sorted({p for p, e, _ in out if not e})
+        cats = {}
+        for p in diffs:
+            cats.setdefault(category(p), []).append(p)
+        post = s.get("route_07a_post") or {}
+        calls.append({
+            "kind": kind, "index": i, "label": label, "class": cls, "win07": win07, "winh": winh,
+            "n_windows_07": g77.get("n_windows"), "n_windows_h": g7h.get("n_windows"),
+            "n_points_07": g77.get("n_points"), "n_points_h": g7h.get("n_points"),
+            "guarded_07": g77.get("num_guarded_regions"), "guarded_h": g7h.get("num_guarded_regions"),
+            "skipped_07": g77.get("location_skipped_phases"), "skipped_h": g7h.get("location_skipped_phases"),
+            "route": None if not isinstance(r7a, dict) or "error" in r7a else {
+                k: r7a[k] for k in ("n_solves", "n_route_differs", "n_lu_differs", "n_route_differs_own", "n_lu_differs_own",
+                                    "n_route_differs_aux", "n_lu_differs_aux", "differs_by_class",
+                                    "n_partition_nonforced", "n_cc_with_levin_half", "n_forced")},
+            "post_differs": {k: [v.get("n_route_differs"), v.get("n_lu_differs")] for k, v in post.items() if v},
+            "equal": not diffs,
+            "categories": sorted(cats),
+            "n_differing": {c_: len(v) for c_, v in cats.items()},
+            "first_differing": {c_: v[:6] for c_, v in cats.items()},
+            "value": [fx_of(s, "value"), fx_of(s7, "value")],
+            "abserr": [fx_of(s, "abserr"), fx_of(s7, "abserr")],
+            "converged": [s.get("converged"), s7.get("converged")],
+            "num_regions": [s.get("num_regions"), s7.get("num_regions")],
+            "max_depth": [s.get("max_depth"), s7.get("max_depth")],
+        })
+    # the unit, under each reading of W1: (A) every solve counts, (B) the own solves only
+    w1_classes = {"A": {"untouched"}, "B": {"untouched", "aux_only"}}
+    t_m, t_g = d2.get("result") or {}, g.get("result") or {}
+    row["total"] = [fx_of(t_m, "total"), fx_of(t_g, "total")]
+    row["total_abserr"] = [fx_of(t_m, "total_abserr"), fx_of(t_g, "total_abserr")]
+    row["total_converged"] = [t_m.get("total_converged"), t_g.get("total_converged")]
+    row["totals_equal"] = row["total"][0] == row["total"][1] and row["total_abserr"][0] == row["total_abserr"][1]
+    row["all_w1"] = {r_: all(c["class"] in cl for c in calls) for r_, cl in w1_classes.items()}
+    if row["all_w1"]["A"] or row["all_w1"]["B"]:
+        out = []
+        CK.diff(d2, g, "data", out)
+        CK.diff(prec2, grec, "pickle", out)
+        diffs = sorted({p for p, e, _ in out if not e})
+        cats = {}
+        for p in diffs:
+            cats.setdefault(unit_category(p), []).append(p)
+        row["whole"] = {"equal": not diffs, "categories": sorted(cats), "n_differing": {c_: len(v) for c_, v in cats.items()},
+                        "first_differing": {c_: v[:8] for c_, v in cats.items()}}
+    row.update(
+        ok=True,
+        normalised=normalised,
+        new_keys_in_07_records=count_keys(g, NEW_KEYS_07A) + count_keys(grec, NEW_KEYS_07A),
+        phase_span_keys=count_keys(d, {"phase_span"}) + count_keys(prec, {"phase_span"}) + count_keys(g, {"phase_span"})
+        + count_keys(grec, {"phase_span"}),
+        post_unit=d.get("route_07a_post_unit") and {k: v for k, v in d["route_07a_post_unit"].items() if k != "changed"},
+        evidence=unit_evidence(d, variant=False),
+        calls=calls,
+    )
+    return row
+
+
+def worker_evidence(run_dir, variant):
+    out = {}
+    if (run_dir / "units.jsonl").exists():
+        for wdir in sorted(p for p in run_dir.iterdir() if p.is_dir()):
+            hello = bye = None
+            for line in open(wdir / "emit.jsonl"):
+                m = json.loads(line)
+                if m["kind"] == "hello":
+                    hello = m["evidence"]
+                elif m["kind"] == "bye":
+                    bye = m
+            out[wdir.name] = (hello, bye)
+    else:
+        for hp in sorted(run_dir.glob("worker_*_hello.json")):
+            name = hp.name[len("worker_"):-len("_hello.json")]
+            bp = run_dir / f"worker_{name}_bye.json"
+            out[name] = (json.loads(hp.read_text())["evidence"], json.loads(bp.read_text()) if bp.exists() else None)
+    checks = {}
+    for name, (h, b) in out.items():
+        tree = None if h is None else h.get("tree")
+        v = None if h is None else h.get("variant_06k")
+        c = {
+            "tree": tree,
+            "hello": h is not None,
+            "levin_quadrature_sha256": None if h is None else h.get("levin_quadrature_sha256"),
+            "lq_file": None if h is None else h["files"].get("AdaptiveLevin.levin_quadrature"),
+            "tree_file_is_h": h is not None and h.get("levin_quadrature_tree_file_sha256") == LQ_SHA256_07AH,
+            "all_in_tree": h is not None and bool(h.get("all_in_tree")),
+            "tree_head_clean": h is not None and tree in TREES and h.get("head") == TREES[tree][1]
+            and h.get("status_short", "x").strip() == "",
+            "bye": b is not None,
+            "ray_not_initialized": b is not None and b.get("ray_initialized") is False,
+            "no_foreign_modules": b is not None and not b.get("modules_foreign"),
+        }
+        if variant:
+            c["lq_is_parent"] = h is not None and h.get("levin_quadrature_sha256") == LQ_SHA256_07APARENT
+            c["variant_installed_and_bound"] = v is not None and v.get("path") == str(PARENT_VARIANT_07A.resolve()) and all(
+                v.get(k) for k in ("sys_modules_is_variant", "package_attribute_is_variant", "package_function_is_variants",
+                                   "QuadSourceIntegral_binds_variants", "three_bessel_integrals_binds_variants")) and \
+                v.get("sha256") == v.get("sha256_now") == LQ_SHA256_07APARENT
+            c["tree_lq_not_loaded"] = b is not None and not any(
+                f == str(H_TREE / "AdaptiveLevin" / "levin_quadrature.py") for _n, f in (b.get("modules_own_tree") or []))
+        else:
+            c["lq_is_h"] = h is not None and h.get("levin_quadrature_sha256") == LQ_SHA256_07AH
+            c["variant_none"] = h is not None and v is None
+        checks[name] = c
+    return checks
+
+
+def jobs_of(run_dir, idx):
+    src = run_dir / "units.jsonl"
+    k0 = src.exists()
+    if not k0:
+        src = run_dir / "checkpoint.jsonl"
+    jobs, seen, skipped = [], set(), []
+    with open(src, "rb") as f:
+        for line in f:
+            r = json.loads(line)
+            uid = r["unit"]
+            if not re.fullmatch(r"h-1e-[78]-\d+-\d+", uid):
+                skipped.append(uid)  # e.g. an over-budget pass's record, compared apart
+                continue
+            if uid in seen:
+                raise SystemExit(f"{uid} twice in {src}")
+            seen.add(uid)
+            if uid_07(uid) not in idx:
+                raise SystemExit(f"{uid_07(uid)} is not in 07's census")
+            pdir = run_dir / r["worker"] if k0 else run_dir
+            jobs.append((uid, line, str(pdir), idx[uid_07(uid)]))
+    return src, jobs, skipped
+
+
+def run_pair(a, mode):
+    """Modes det and instr: two run_k0.py runs, unit by unit, whole."""
+    set_excluded(mode == "instr")
+    ra, rb = Path(a.run_dir).resolve(), Path(a.run_dir_b).resolve()
+    title = ("Gate W0 (4), determinism" if mode == "det" else "Gate W0 (3), the instrument on against off")
+    hdr = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), f"levin-phase-floor 07a: {title}, {ra.name} against {rb.name}",
+                 [ra / "units.jsonl", rb / "units.jsonl", HERE / "compare_k0.py"])
+    for l in hdr:
+        print(l, flush=True)
+
+    def load(run):
+        recs = {}
+        for line in open(run / "units.jsonl", "rb"):
+            r = json.loads(line)
+            recs[r["unit"]] = (r["data"], load_mine(r["data"], run / r["worker"]))
+        return recs
+
+    A, B = load(ra), load(rb)
+    rows = []
+    for uid in sorted(set(A) | set(B)):
+        if uid not in A or uid not in B:
+            rows.append({"unit": uid, "ok": False, "differing": ["unit in one run only"]})
+            continue
+        (da, pa), (db, pb) = A[uid], B[uid]
+        out = []
+        CK.diff(da, db, "data", out)
+        CK.diff(pa, pb, "pickle", out)
+        differing = sorted({p for p, e, _ in out if not e})
+        cs_a = (da.get("calls") or []) + (da.get("other_calls") or [])
+        cs_b = (db.get("calls") or []) + (db.get("other_calls") or [])
+        g7 = [c.get("guard_07") for c in cs_a]
+        r7 = [c.get("route_07a") for c in cs_a]
+        ok = not differing and da["status"] == "done" and db["status"] == "done"
+        extra = {}
+        if mode == "instr":
+            extra["A_route_07a_on_every_call"] = bool(cs_a) and all(isinstance(c.get("route_07a"), dict) and "error" not in c["route_07a"] for c in cs_a)
+            extra["B_route_07a_on_no_call"] = all(c.get("route_07a") is None for c in cs_b) and db.get("instrument_07a") is False
+            ok = ok and extra["A_route_07a_on_every_call"] and extra["B_route_07a_on_no_call"] and da.get("instrument_07a") is True
+        rows.append({"unit": uid, "status": [da["status"], db["status"]], "ok": ok,
+                     "differing": differing[:200], "detail": [list(x) for x in out if not x[1]][:50],
+                     "excl_differing": sorted({norm(p) for p, e, _ in out if e}),
+                     "n_calls": len(g7), "n_calls_windowed": sum(1 for x in g7 if isinstance(x, dict) and x.get("n_windows")),
+                     "n_calls_route_changed": sum(1 for x in r7 if isinstance(x, dict) and (x.get("n_route_differs") or x.get("n_lu_differs"))),
+                     "route_changes": [(c["index"], c["route_07a"].get("n_route_differs"), c["route_07a"].get("n_lu_differs"),
+                                        c["route_07a"].get("own_differs"), c["route_07a"].get("aux_differs"))
+                                       for c in cs_a if isinstance(c.get("route_07a"), dict) and "error" not in c["route_07a"]
+                                       and (c["route_07a"].get("n_route_differs") or c["route_07a"].get("n_lu_differs"))],
+                     "windows": [(c["index"], c["guard_07"].get("n_windows"), c["guard_07"].get("n_points_by_kind"))
+                                 for c in cs_a if isinstance(c.get("guard_07"), dict) and c["guard_07"].get("n_windows")],
+                     "wall_s": [da.get("wall_s"), db.get("wall_s")], **extra})
+    wev = {**{f"A:{k}": v for k, v in worker_evidence(ra, False).items()}, **{f"B:{k}": v for k, v in worker_evidence(rb, False).items()}}
+    wev_ok = bool(wev) and all(all(v for k, v in c.items() if k not in ("levin_quadrature_sha256", "lq_file", "tree")) for c in wev.values())
+    n_fail = sum(1 for r in rows if not r["ok"])
+    doc = {"header": hdr, "mode": mode, "excluded": sorted(CK.EXCLUDED) + ["*_s"], "n_units": len(rows),
+           "n_units_identical": len(rows) - n_fail, "worker_evidence_all_hold": wev_ok, "worker_evidence": wev, "units": rows}
+    Path(a.out_json).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    Path(a.out_json).write_text(json.dumps(doc, indent=1, default=str))
+    for r in rows:
+        print(f"{r['unit']}: {'IDENTICAL' if r['ok'] else 'DIFFERS'}; status {r.get('status')}; calls {r.get('n_calls')}; "
+              f"windowed {r.get('n_calls_windowed')} {r.get('windows')}; route-changed {r.get('n_calls_route_changed')} "
+              f"{r.get('route_changes')}; wall {r.get('wall_s')}; differing {r['differing'][:10]}; excluded keys differing "
+              f"{r.get('excl_differing')}" + (f"; on/off {r.get('A_route_07a_on_every_call')}/{r.get('B_route_07a_on_no_call')}" if mode == "instr" else ""))
+    print(f"worker evidence all hold {wev_ok}")
+    print(f"wrote {a.out_json} sha256 {sha256(a.out_json)}")
+    return 0 if n_fail == 0 and wev_ok else 1
+
+
+CLASSES = ("untouched", "aux_only", "own", "window_both", "window_07_only", "window_h_only", "no_diagnostics")
+
+
+def summarise_calls(rows):
+    s = {"by_class": {}, "by_class_rtol": {}, "equal_by_class": {}, "categories_by_class": {}, "units": {}}
+    for r in rows:
+        for c in r.get("calls") or []:
+            k = c["class"]
+            s["by_class"][k] = s["by_class"].get(k, 0) + 1
+            kr = f"{r['rtol']}|{c['kind']}|{k}"
+            s["by_class_rtol"][kr] = s["by_class_rtol"].get(kr, 0) + 1
+            e = s["equal_by_class"].setdefault(k, {"equal": 0, "differing": 0})
+            e["equal" if c["equal"] else "differing"] += 1
+            key = "+".join(c["categories"]) or "none"
+            cc = s["categories_by_class"].setdefault(k, {})
+            cc[key] = cc.get(key, 0) + 1
+    for reading in ("A", "B"):
+        all_ = [r for r in rows if (r.get("all_w1") or {}).get(reading)]
+        s["units"][reading] = {"n_all_w1": len(all_), "n_totals_equal": sum(1 for r in all_ if r["totals_equal"]),
+                               "n_whole_equal": sum(1 for r in all_ if (r.get("whole") or {}).get("equal")),
+                               "whole_categories": {}}
+        for r in all_:
+            key = "+".join((r.get("whole") or {}).get("categories") or []) or "none"
+            s["units"][reading]["whole_categories"][key] = s["units"][reading]["whole_categories"].get(key, 0) + 1
+    return s
+
+
+def main():
+    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
+    ap.add_argument("mode", choices=("whole", "calls", "det", "instr"))
+    ap.add_argument("run_dir")
+    ap.add_argument("rest", nargs="+")
+    ap.add_argument("--processes", type=int, default=1)
+    a = ap.parse_args()
+    if a.processes > 6:
+        raise SystemExit("at most six processes")
+    if a.mode in ("det", "instr"):
+        if len(a.rest) != 2:
+            raise SystemExit(f"{a.mode} RUN_DIR_A RUN_DIR_B OUT_JSON")
+        a.run_dir_b, a.out_json = a.rest
+        return run_pair(a, a.mode)
+    if len(a.rest) != 1:
+        raise SystemExit(f"{a.mode} RUN_DIR OUT_JSON")
+    a.out_json = a.rest[0]
+    set_excluded(True)
+    run_dir = Path(a.run_dir).resolve()
+    idx_doc = json.loads(INDEX.read_text())
+    if idx_doc["checkpoint_sha256"] != CHECKPOINT07_SHA256:
+        raise SystemExit("the index is not of 07's checkpoint")
+    src, jobs, skipped = jobs_of(run_dir, idx_doc["units"])
+    hdr = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), f"levin-phase-floor 07a: {run_dir.name} against 07's census ({RUN07.name}), "
+                 f"mode {a.mode}", [src, INDEX, HERE / "compare_k0.py"])
+    for l in hdr:
+        print(l, flush=True)
+    print(f"# {len(jobs)} units to compare ({len(skipped)} other records skipped: {skipped[:10]}), {a.processes} "
+          f"process(es); excluded: 07's {sorted(EXCLUDED_07)} and every key ending _s; set aside as new: "
+          f"{sorted(NEW_KEYS_07A)}", flush=True)
+    fn = one_whole if a.mode == "whole" else one_calls
+    if a.processes > 1:
+        with mp.get_context("spawn").Pool(a.processes) as pool:
+            rows = list(pool.imap(fn, jobs, chunksize=8))
+    else:
+        rows = [fn(j) for j in jobs]
+    variant = a.mode == "whole"
+    wev = worker_evidence(run_dir, variant)
+    wev_ok = bool(wev) and all(all(v for k, v in c.items() if k not in ("levin_quadrature_sha256", "lq_file", "tree")) for c in wev.values())
+    ev_tot = {}
+    for r in rows:
+        for k, v in (r.get("evidence") or {}).items():
+            ev_tot.setdefault(k, [0, 0])[0 if v else 1] += 1
+    doc = {"header": hdr, "mode": a.mode, "run_dir": str(run_dir), "source": str(src), "source_sha256": sha256(src),
+           "run07": str(RUN07), "checkpoint07_sha256": CHECKPOINT07_SHA256,
+           "excluded_07": sorted(EXCLUDED_07) + ["*_s"], "set_aside_new_07a": sorted(NEW_KEYS_07A),
+           "records_skipped": skipped, "n_units": len(rows),
+           "statuses": {s: sum(1 for r in rows if r["status"] == s) for s in sorted({r["status"] for r in rows})},
+           "unit_evidence_true_false": ev_tot, "worker_evidence_all_hold": wev_ok, "worker_evidence": wev,
+           "new_keys_in_07_records": sum(r.get("new_keys_in_07_records", 0) or 0 for r in rows),
+           "phase_span_keys": sum(r.get("phase_span_keys", 0) or 0 for r in rows)}
+    out_p = Path(a.out_json)
+    out_p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    if a.mode == "whole":
+        n_fail = sum(1 for r in rows if not r["ok"])
+        inv = {}
+        for r in rows:
+            for p in r.get("excl_differing", []):
+                inv[p] = inv.get(p, 0) + 1
+        tot = {k: sum(r.get(k, 0) or 0 for r in rows) for k in ("n_calls", "n_group_calls", "n_regions", "n_regions_returned")}
+        doc.update(n_units_equal=len(rows) - n_fail, n_units_differing=n_fail, totals=tot,
+                   excluded_keys_differing_units=dict(sorted(inv.items())),
+                   first_differing_units=[r for r in rows if not r["ok"]][:20],
+                   units=[{k: v for k, v in r.items() if k != "detail"} | ({"detail": r.get("detail")} if not r["ok"] else {})
+                          for r in rows])
+        out_p.write_text(json.dumps(doc, indent=1, default=str))
+        for r in [r for r in rows if not r["ok"]][:20]:
+            print(f"DIFFERS {r['unit']}: {r['differing'][:10]}", flush=True)
+        print(f"units {len(rows)}; equal {len(rows) - n_fail}; differing {n_fail}; statuses {doc['statuses']}")
+        print(f"totals {tot}; new keys in 07's records {doc['new_keys_in_07_records']}; phase_span keys {doc['phase_span_keys']}")
+        print(f"unit evidence (true, false): {ev_tot}")
+        print(f"worker evidence: {len(wev)} workers, all hold {wev_ok}; levin_quadrature sha256 "
+              f"{sorted({str(c['levin_quadrature_sha256']) for c in wev.values()})}")
+        print(f"excluded keys differing (path: units): {doc['excluded_keys_differing_units']}")
+        print(f"wrote {out_p} sha256 {sha256(out_p)}")
+        return 0 if n_fail == 0 and wev_ok and doc["new_keys_in_07_records"] == 0 else 1
+    # calls
+    calls_p = out_p.with_name(out_p.name.replace(".json", "") + ".calls.jsonl")
+    with open(calls_p, "w") as fc:
+        for r in rows:
+            for c in r.get("calls") or []:
+                fc.write(json.dumps({"unit": r["unit"], "rtol": r["rtol"], **c}, default=str) + "\n")
+    summ = summarise_calls(rows)
+    problems = [{k: r.get(k) for k in ("unit", "status", "status_07", "problem")} for r in rows if not r.get("ok")]
+    doc.update(summary=summ, problems=problems, calls_jsonl=str(calls_p), calls_jsonl_sha256=sha256(calls_p),
+               units=[{k: v for k, v in r.items() if k != "calls"} for r in rows])
+    out_p.write_text(json.dumps(doc, indent=1, default=str))
+    print(f"units {len(rows)}; problems {len(problems)} {problems[:5]}; statuses {doc['statuses']}")
+    print(f"calls by class: {summ['by_class']}")
+    print(f"equal by class: {summ['equal_by_class']}")
+    print(f"differing categories by class: {json.dumps(summ['categories_by_class'])}")
+    print(f"units all of W1's classes, reading A (every solve) / B (own solves): {json.dumps(summ['units'])}")
+    print(f"new keys in 07's records {doc['new_keys_in_07_records']}; phase_span keys {doc['phase_span_keys']}")
+    print(f"unit evidence (true, false): {ev_tot}")
+    print(f"worker evidence: {len(wev)} workers, all hold {wev_ok}")
+    print(f"wrote {calls_p} sha256 {sha256(calls_p)}")
+    print(f"wrote {out_p} sha256 {sha256(out_p)}")
+    return 0 if not problems and wev_ok and doc["new_keys_in_07_records"] == 0 else 1
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    sys.exit(main())

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07a section 6: hash, read only, every file of 07's census run directory (07a's "before") and
+every file of 07's census working records, and check them against the list 07 kept. New in 07a, after 07's
+hash_runs_07.py (a record, not edited).
+
+- 07's run directory, levin-phase-floor-07-census-20261108T171137: every file is checked by SHA-256 and size against
+  docs/levin-phase-floor/07/census/KEPT_ON_MAC_STUDIO.txt (its section for this run directory, read in the tree h); the
+  file sets must be equal (150 files), the checkpoint must be 03dfe786..., and there must be 16 region pickles.
+- 07's working records, var/levin-phase-floor-07/census/: every file is hashed (for the before/after comparison), and
+  the 152 files the same KEPT list names under its first section ("pickles, and files over 2 MB") are checked by
+  SHA-256 and size.
+
+Every file is opened 'rb'. Writes OUT_TXT: a provenance header, then one line per file "sha256 size path" (run files
+relative to var/runs/, records files as "REC07/<relpath>"), then the checks' verdicts. Exits 1 if a check fails.
+
+Usage: python -B hash_runs_07a.py LABEL OUT_TXT
+"""
+
+import os
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+from prov import CHECKPOINT07_SHA256, H_TREE, REC07, RUN07, header, sha256  # noqa: E402
+
+KEPT07 = H_TREE / "docs" / "levin-phase-floor" / "07" / "census" / "KEPT_ON_MAC_STUDIO.txt"
+
+
+def hash_dir(root, rel_to):
+    out = {}
+    for r, _dirs, files in os.walk(root):
+        for name in files:
+            p = Path(r) / name
+            out[str(p.relative_to(rel_to))] = (sha256(p), p.stat().st_size)
+    return dict(sorted(out.items()))
+
+
+def kept07(section_marker):
+    want = {}
+    sec = False
+    for line in KEPT07.read_text().splitlines():
+        if line.startswith("## "):
+            sec = section_marker in line
+            continue
+        if sec and line and not line.startswith("#"):
+            sha, size, rel = line.split(None, 2)
+            want[rel] = (sha, int(size))
+    return want
+
+
+def main():
+    label, out_p = sys.argv[1], Path(sys.argv[2])
+    lines = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), f"levin-phase-floor 07a: 07's census run directory and working records, {label}",
+                   [KEPT07])
+    ok = True
+    verdicts = []
+    # the run directory
+    kept = kept07(f"run directory var/runs/{RUN07.name}/")
+    got = hash_dir(RUN07, RUN07.parent)
+    lines.append(f"# {RUN07}: {len(got)} files, {sum(s for _, s in got.values())} bytes")
+    for rel, (sha, size) in got.items():
+        lines.append(f"{sha}  {size}  {rel}")
+    ck = got.get(f"{RUN07.name}/checkpoint.jsonl", (None, None))[0]
+    verdicts.append(f"{RUN07.name}: checkpoint sha256 {ck} {'EQUAL' if ck == CHECKPOINT07_SHA256 else 'DIFFERS FROM'} "
+                    f"{CHECKPOINT07_SHA256}")
+    ok &= ck == CHECKPOINT07_SHA256
+    n_pkl = sum(1 for r in got if Path(r).name.startswith("regions_") and r.endswith(".pkl"))
+    verdicts.append(f"{RUN07.name}: {n_pkl} region pickles")
+    ok &= n_pkl == 16
+    bad = [r for r, v in kept.items() if got.get(r) != v]
+    extra = sorted(set(got) - set(kept))
+    verdicts.append(f"{RUN07.name}: against 07's KEPT_ON_MAC_STUDIO.txt: {len(kept)} listed, {len(got)} present, "
+                    f"{len(bad)} differing or missing {bad[:5]}, {len(extra)} not listed {extra[:5]}")
+    ok &= not bad and not extra and len(kept) == 150
+    # the working records
+    kept_r = kept07("under var/levin-phase-floor-07/census/")
+    got_r = hash_dir(REC07, REC07)
+    lines.append(f"# {REC07}: {len(got_r)} files, {sum(s for _, s in got_r.values())} bytes")
+    for rel, (sha, size) in got_r.items():
+        lines.append(f"{sha}  {size}  REC07/{rel}")
+    bad_r = [r for r, v in kept_r.items() if got_r.get(r) != v]
+    verdicts.append(f"{REC07}: against 07's KEPT_ON_MAC_STUDIO.txt (pickles and files over 2 MB): {len(kept_r)} listed, "
+                    f"{len(bad_r)} differing or missing {bad_r[:5]}; {len(got_r)} files hashed in all")
+    ok &= not bad_r and len(kept_r) == 152
+    lines += ["# " + v for v in verdicts] + [f"# verdict: {'ALL HOLD' if ok else 'A CHECK FAILS'}"]
+    out_p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    out_p.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
+    print("\n".join(l for l in lines if l.startswith("#")))
+    print(f"wrote {out_p} sha256 {sha256(out_p)}")
+    return 0 if ok else 1
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    sys.exit(main())

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07a section 6: an index of 07's census checkpoint (07a's "before"), read only. Copied from
+07's census scripts/index_06m_07.py (a record, not edited), changed only where marked "prompt 07a": the run is 07's census
+(prov.RUN07), its checkpoint SHA-256 03dfe786... (prov.CHECKPOINT07_SHA256), and the title; each unit also records,
+per call, its guard_07 window and location counts, for the classing (n_windows, n_points, num_guarded_regions,
+location_skipped_phases). 07's text follows.
+
+levin-phase-floor prompt 07 section 6: an index of 06m's census checkpoint (07's "before"), read only. Copied from
+06m's census scripts/index_routeB_06m.py (a record, not edited), changed only where marked "prompt 07": the run is
+06m's census (prov.RUN06M), its checkpoint SHA-256 ea5bc3ab... (prov.CHECKPOINT06M_SHA256), and the title. 06m's text
+follows.
+
+levin-phase-floor prompt 06m section 5: an index of route B's census checkpoint (06l's, the "before"), read only.
+Copied from 06l's scripts/index_06g.py (a record, not edited), changed only where marked "prompt 06m": the run is
+route B's census (prov.RUN06L_B), its checkpoint SHA-256 5cb3d3c0... (prov.CHECKPOINT06L_B_SHA256), and the title.
+06l's state/index_06g.json was deleted on 2026-11-05 (06l's KEPT_ON_MAC_STUDIO.md), and no index of route B's checkpoint
+is kept; this one is built from the kept checkpoint. 06l's text follows.
+
+levin-phase-floor prompt 06k: an index of 06g's census checkpoint, read only. New in 06k.
+
+One pass over var/runs/levin-phase-floor-06g-census-20261101T114315/checkpoint.jsonl (its SHA-256
+checked against 03adcbba... first). Per unit: its byte offset and length in the file, the line's
+SHA-256, the record's status, worker, wall_s, rebuild_s, recorded time, the number of group and
+other calls, the number of unconverged group calls, and the region-pickle pointer. Writes OUT_JSON.
+Usage: python -B index_06g.py OUT_JSON
+"""
+
+import hashlib
+import json
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+import refs_common as RC  # noqa: E402
+from prov import CHECKPOINT06L_B_SHA256, RUN06L_B, header  # noqa: E402  prompt 06m
+from prov import CHECKPOINT06M_SHA256, RUN06M  # noqa: E402  prompt 07
+from prov import CHECKPOINT07_SHA256, RUN07  # noqa: E402  prompt 07a
+
+CHECKPOINT06G_SHA256 = "03adcbba8a78d82d9d6b899b8eee8b9ae1659763f52deb066d2840af3d82c0fa"
+
+
+def main():
+    out_p = Path(sys.argv[1])
+    run = RUN07  # prompt 07a (07: RUN06M; 06m: RUN06L_B; 06l: RC.census_run_06g())
+    ck = run / "checkpoint.jsonl"
+    hdr = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), "levin-phase-floor 07a: an index of 07's census checkpoint", [ck])  # prompt 07a  # prompt 06l: the title (06k: 06k); prompt 06m: the title
+    for l in hdr:
+        print(l)
+    if RC.sha256(ck) != CHECKPOINT07_SHA256:  # prompt 07a (07: CHECKPOINT06M_SHA256; 06m: CHECKPOINT06L_B_SHA256)
+        raise SystemExit("07's checkpoint sha256 differs")
+    units = {}
+    off = 0
+    with open(ck, "rb") as f:
+        for line in f:
+            r = json.loads(line)
+            d = r["data"]
+            uid = r["unit"]
+            if uid in units:
+                raise SystemExit(f"{uid} recorded twice")
+            units[uid] = {
+                "offset": off,
+                "length": len(line),
+                "line_sha256": hashlib.sha256(line).hexdigest(),
+                "recorded": r.get("recorded"),
+                "status": d.get("status"),
+                "worker": d.get("worker"),
+                "rtol": d.get("rtol"),
+                "row": d.get("row"),
+                "k_key": d.get("k_key"),
+                "wall_s": d.get("wall_s"),
+                "rebuild_s": d.get("rebuild_s"),
+                "n_group_calls": len(d.get("calls") or []),
+                "n_other_calls": len(d.get("other_calls") or []),
+                "n_group_unconverged": sum(1 for c in (d.get("calls") or []) if not c.get("converged")),
+                "n_cache_built": len(d.get("cache_built") or []),
+                "pickle": [d.get("pickle"), d.get("pickle_offset"), d.get("pickle_length"), d.get("pickle_record_sha256")],
+                # prompt 07a: per call, 07's guard_07 counts: [kind, index, n_windows, n_points, num_guarded_regions,
+                # num_guard_at_depth_max, location_skipped_phases]
+                "guard_07": [[kind, c.get("index"), (c.get("guard_07") or {}).get("n_windows"),
+                              (c.get("guard_07") or {}).get("n_points"), (c.get("guard_07") or {}).get("num_guarded_regions"),
+                              (c.get("guard_07") or {}).get("num_guard_at_depth_max"),
+                              (c.get("guard_07") or {}).get("location_skipped_phases")]
+                             for kind, key in (("group", "calls"), ("analytic", "other_calls")) for c in d.get(key) or []],
+            }
+            off += len(line)
+    doc = {"header": hdr, "checkpoint": str(ck), "checkpoint_sha256": CHECKPOINT07_SHA256,  # prompt 07a
+           "n_units": len(units), "units": units}
+    out_p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    out_p.write_text(json.dumps(doc))
+    walls = sorted(u["wall_s"] for u in units.values() if u["wall_s"] is not None)
+    st = {}
+    for u in units.values():
+        st[u["status"]] = st.get(u["status"], 0) + 1
+    print(f"units {len(units)}; statuses {st}; wall_s max {walls[-1]:.1f} p99 {walls[int(0.99 * len(walls))]:.1f} "
+          f"median {walls[len(walls) // 2]:.2f} sum {sum(walls):.0f}")
+    rb = sorted((u["rebuild_s"] or 0.0) for u in units.values())
+    print(f"rebuild_s max {rb[-1]:.1f} p99 {rb[int(0.99 * len(rb))]:.2f} median {rb[len(rb) // 2]:.3f} sum {sum(rb):.0f}")
+    print(f"wrote {out_p} sha256 {RC.sha256(out_p)}")
+    return 0
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    sys.exit(main())

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07a section 6: the list of files kept on the Mac Studio only (not copied to the laptop):
+every file of the census, references and k0 run directories, and every pickle and every file over 2 MB under
+var/levin-phase-floor-07a/census/, each with its SHA-256 and size. Copied from 07's kept_list_07.py (a record, not edited), changed only in its run directories, its paths and its title. Read only.
+Usage: python -B kept_list_07a.py OUT_TXT"""
+import os
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+from prov import MAIN, WIDER, sha256  # noqa: E402
+
+RUNS = ["levin-phase-floor-07a-census-20261112T132744", "levin-phase-floor-07a-references-20261112T203555",  # prompt 07a
+        "levin-phase-floor-07a-k0-20261112T130135"]  # prompt 07a
+
+
+def main():
+    out_p = Path(sys.argv[1])
+    L = ["# levin-phase-floor 07a census agent: files kept on the Mac Studio only (mps026298), not copied to the laptop.",
+         f"# sha256  size_bytes  path. Written by scripts/kept_list_07a.py (sha256 {sha256(Path(__file__))})."]
+    L += ["", "## pickles, and files over 2 MB, under var/levin-phase-floor-07a/census/"]
+    n = 0
+    for root, _d, files in sorted(os.walk(WIDER)):
+        for f in sorted(files):
+            p = Path(root) / f
+            if p == out_p.resolve():
+                continue
+            s = p.stat().st_size
+            if f.endswith(".pkl") or s > 2 * 1024 * 1024:
+                L.append(f"{sha256(p)}  {s}  {p.relative_to(WIDER)}")
+                n += 1
+    L.append(f"# {n} files")
+    for run in RUNS:
+        d = MAIN / "var" / "runs" / run
+        L += ["", f"## run directory var/runs/{run}/ (every file)"]
+        tot = cnt = 0
+        for root, _d, files in sorted(os.walk(d)):
+            for f in sorted(files):
+                p = Path(root) / f
+                s = p.stat().st_size
+                L.append(f"{sha256(p)}  {s}  {p.relative_to(d.parent)}")
+                tot += s
+                cnt += 1
+        L.append(f"# {cnt} files, {tot} bytes")
+    out_p.write_text("\n".join(L) + "\n")
+    print(f"wrote {out_p} ({len(L)} lines) sha256 {sha256(out_p)}")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07a section 6: write Gate W0's plan and launch 06j's driver run_06j.py (07's copy, unchanged) on
+it as one registered, detached run (campaign levin-phase-floor, prompt 07a, slug k0). Copied from 07's census
+launch_07.py (a record, not edited), with its plans replaced, each change marked "prompt 07a". The launch itself is 07's
+(06m's, 06l's, 06k's), unchanged: ``runregistrykit.begin`` first (manifest, script run_06j.py), then
+``nohup sh -c '<driver>; exit $?'`` in a new session, stdin from /dev/null, stdout and stderr into the run directory, the
+sh pid recorded with ``Run.heartbeat(pid=...)``; the driver replaces it with its own.
+
+Plan ``k0`` (Gate W0, slug ``k0``), stage by stage, each stage after the one before, never more than six computing
+processes:
+1. ``k0_run_parent``: 06k's 100 K0 units (docs/levin-phase-floor/06l/out/k0/units.json, in the tree h) run by run_k0.py in
+   the tree h with the parent's module installed (variant ``parent``: state/variants/levin_quadrature_parent.py, git show
+   dadd63a0:..., 4e5da371..., byte-identical to 07's census tree e182bc7a's), the diagnostics on, six workers, into
+   state/k0/parent (stop_on_fail: if the run itself fails, nothing further runs);
+2. ``k0_compare_parent``: compare_07a.py ``whole`` against 07's census into out/k0/compare_w0b.json (W0's second clause);
+3. ``k0_run_h``: the same 100 units at h (h's own module, 4ea1948b..., code 1ec734d7's), six workers, into state/k0/h:
+   the census's own unit timings at h for its cost projection, and the units the third clause chooses from;
+4. ``k0_compare_h``: compare_07a.py ``calls`` against 07's census into out/k0/compare_k0_h_calls.json (descriptive: the
+   classing of section 6's comparison on those units, before the census);
+5. ``w0_select``: select_w0_units_07a.py on state/k0/h: the units of the third and fourth clauses, by the rule in its
+   docstring (state/w0c3_units.json, state/w0c4_units.json);
+6. ``w0c3_run``: W0's third clause, the two units (one with a window, one with a route change) run at h with the
+   diagnostics on and, at the same time in fresh workers, off, into state/w0c3/on and state/w0c3/off (at most four
+   workers);
+7. ``w0c3_compare``: compare_07a.py ``instr`` on the two into out/w0c3/compare_instr.json;
+8. ``w0c4_run``: W0's fourth clause, the window unit run at h twice, in two fresh workers at once, into state/w0c4/run1
+   and state/w0c4/run2;
+9. ``w0c4_compare``: compare_07a.py ``det`` on the two into out/w0c4/compare_det.json.
+
+Plan ``references`` (the user's references at the W5 stop, state/ruling_w5.md item 2; slug ``references``), as 07's plan
+``references`` (launch_07.py, the user's ruling at 07's stop 4): one stage, one reading at a time (each reading's tool runs
+six worker processes), FCC r1 and r2 on each of the four calls of out/refs/inputs.json, QAWO r1 and r2 on those whose QAWO
+readings project at 30 minutes or less (out/refs/refs_projection.json, its ``readings``), each call's readings in the
+tool's own order, the calls cheapest first, each reading by 06g's one_reading.py (unchanged) in its own process,
+PYTHONPATH the tree h then this directory, into out/refs/readings/<sid>-<impl>-r<res>.json; run_06j.py's run backstop
+as 07's (report past 30 minutes, stop at a projected finish of 48 h).
+
+Usage (from var/levin-phase-floor-07a/census/): python -B scripts/launch_07a.py k0|references
+"""
+
+import json
+import os
+import shlex
+import subprocess

+           "for bit; and (4) the window unit run twice at h in two fresh workers and compared bit for bit. Every unit "
+           f"reads 06's certified scratch copy of A3 v2 read-only (fingerprint {A3V2_DIGEST[:8]}) on a stand-in "
+           "ShardedPool, 300 s per unit. No census. At most six computing processes; Ray-free, no lock, no store built, "
+           "nothing written in a tree"),
+}
+PURPOSES["references"] = (  # prompt 07a
+    "levin-phase-floor prompt 07a section 6 on the Mac Studio (mps026298; Python 3.13.16): the user's references at the W5 "
+    "stop (state/ruling_w5.md: 'Yes, W5 gated only, and take references for both calls'), by 07's V4 method: "
+    "tools/filon_reference.py's readings (d37e633b, as 07's) of rows 3-678 and 1-34599, group calls 2 (G+q-r) and 3 (G-q+r), "

+    "rtols, out/refs/integrands.json); FCC r1 and r2 on all four, QAWO r1 and r2 where projected at 30 minutes or less "
+    "(out/refs/refs_projection.json, 737 s in all with margin); one reading at a time, six worker processes each, one "
+    "process per reading (06g's one_reading.py); stopped only at a projected finish of 48 h. Ray-free, no store opened, "
+    "nothing written in a tree")
+SCOPES = {  # prompt 07a
+    "k0": ("docs/levin-phase-floor/06l/out/k0/units.json (100 units, in the tree h, read only), state/w0c3_units.json (2 "
+           "units) and state/w0c4_units.json (1 unit), the last two written by the run's stage 5; outputs under "
+           "var/levin-phase-floor-07a/census/: state/k0/parent, state/k0/h, state/w0c3/on, state/w0c3/off, state/w0c4/run1, "
+           "state/w0c4/run2, out/k0/, out/w0c3/, out/w0c4/, out/cmd/k0"),
+}
+
+SCOPES["references"] = ("4 captured calls (out/refs/inputs.json), up to 16 readings; outputs out/refs/readings/, "  # prompt 07a
+                         "out/cmd/references")
+HEARTBEAT_MEANS = ("Refreshed by the launcher once with the sh pid, by the driver at start with its own pid, at each "
+                   "command's start and end, and every 60 s by a thread of the driver while its process lives.")
+
+
+def main():
+    label = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else None
+    if label not in PURPOSES:
+        raise SystemExit(f"usage: launch_07a.py {'|'.join(PURPOSES)}")
+    plan = {"k0": plan_k0, "references": plan_references}[label]()  # prompt 07a
+    plan_p = ROOT / "state" / f"plan_{label}.json"
+    if plan_p.exists():
+        raise SystemExit(f"{plan_p} exists; not overwritten")
+    plan_p.write_text(json.dumps(plan, indent=1))
+    n = sum(len(st["jobs"]) for st in plan["stages"])
+    argv = [PYTHON, "-B", str(SCRIPT), "--run-dir", "<run dir>", "--plan", str(plan_p)]
+    run = runregistrykit.begin(
+        campaign="levin-phase-floor", prompt="07a", slug=label, purpose=PURPOSES[label], argv=argv, cwd=str(ROOT),
+        script=str(SCRIPT), unit="commands", expected_units=n, checkpoint=True, results=None, scope=SCOPES[label],
+        heartbeat_means=HEARTBEAT_MEANS)
+    env = dict(os.environ)
+    env.pop("PYTHONPATH", None)
+    env["PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE"] = "1"
+    stdout = open(run.stdout_path, "ab")
+    stderr = open(run.stderr_path, "ab")
+    command = " ".join(shlex.quote(a) for a in [PYTHON, "-B", str(SCRIPT), "--run-dir", run.path, "--plan", str(plan_p)]) + "; exit $?"
+    child = subprocess.Popen(["nohup", "sh", "-c", command], cwd=str(ROOT), env=env, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
+                             stdout=stdout, stderr=stderr, start_new_session=True, close_fds=True)
+    stdout.close()
+    stderr.close()
+    run.heartbeat(pid=child.pid, stage="launched (detached sh started)")
+    print(f"run id      {run.id}")
+    print(f"run dir     {run.path}")
+    print(f"plan        {plan_p} ({n} commands)")
+    print(f"sh pid      {child.pid}")
+    print(f"command     {command}")
+    print(f"launcher    pid {os.getpid()}, exiting without waiting")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()
+    sys.exit(0)
