+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07a section 6 (descriptive; new in 07a; read only): every call whose value moved from 07's by
+more than an abserr (|v_h - v_07| > abserr_07, and whether also > abserr_h, and > max of the two), by class, with its
+route changes; the classes of V4's 33 calls; and the aux_only calls whose abserr shrank by more than 2x, listed.
+Usage: python -B moves_beyond_07a.py OUT_JSON
+"""
+import json
+import sys
+from collections import Counter
+from fractions import Fraction
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+from prov import WIDER, header, sha256  # noqa: E402
+
+CALLS = WIDER / "out" / "stop3" / "compare_census_calls.calls.jsonl"
+W4 = WIDER / "out" / "scores" / "w4_scores.json"
+
+
+def F(h):
+    return Fraction(float.fromhex(h))
+
+
+def main():
+    out_p = Path(sys.argv[1])
+    hdr = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), "levin-phase-floor 07a: calls moved beyond an abserr; V4's classes", [CALLS, W4])
+    w4 = json.loads(W4.read_text())
+    want = {("h-" + x["unit"].split("-", 1)[1], x.get("kind", "group"), x["index"]): x["sid"] for x in w4["calls"] + w4["z"]}
+    beyond, v4cls, shrink = [], {}, []
+    for line in open(CALLS):
+        c = json.loads(line)
+        k = (c["unit"], c["kind"], c["index"])
+        if k in want:
+            v4cls[want[k]] = c["class"]
+        vh, v7, eh, e7 = F(c["value"][0]), F(c["value"][1]), F(c["abserr"][0]), F(c["abserr"][1])
+        d = abs(vh - v7)
+        if d > e7:
+            beyond.append({k_: c[k_] for k_ in ("unit", "kind", "index", "label", "class", "converged", "num_regions", "max_depth")}
+                          | {"over_e07": float(d / e7), "over_eh": float(d / eh) if eh else None, "beyond_both": d > max(eh, e7),
+                             "abserr_h_over_07": float(eh / e7), "route": c["route"] and {kk: c["route"][kk] for kk in ("n_route_differs", "n_lu_differs", "differs_by_class")}})
+        if c["class"] == "aux_only" and e7 and eh / e7 < Fraction(1, 2):
+            shrink.append({k_: c[k_] for k_ in ("unit", "kind", "index", "label", "converged", "num_regions")}
+                          | {"abserr_h_over_07": float(eh / e7), "value_move_over_e07": float(d / e7), "value_move_over_eh": float(d / eh)})
+    doc = {"header": hdr, "n_beyond_e07": len(beyond), "beyond_by_class": dict(Counter(b["class"] for b in beyond)),
+           "beyond_both_by_class": dict(Counter(b["class"] for b in beyond if b["beyond_both"])), "beyond": beyond,
+           "v4_classes": v4cls, "v4_by_class": dict(Counter(v4cls.values())), "aux_shrink_gt_2x": shrink,
+           "aux_shrink_gt_2x_move_over_eh": {"max": max((s["value_move_over_eh"] for s in shrink), default=None),
+                                            "n_gt_1": sum(1 for s in shrink if s["value_move_over_eh"] > 1)}}
+    out_p.write_text(json.dumps(doc, indent=1, default=str))
+    print(json.dumps({k: v for k, v in doc.items() if k not in ("header", "beyond", "aux_shrink_gt_2x")}, indent=1))
+    for b in beyond:
+        print(b["unit"], b["kind"], b["index"], b["label"][-38:], b["class"], f"{b['over_e07']:.3g} {b['over_eh']:.3g} both={b['beyond_both']} eh/e7={b['abserr_h_over_07']:.3g}", b["converged"], b["num_regions"], (b["route"] or {}).get("differs_by_class") and {k: v for k, v in b["route"]["differs_by_class"].items() if v["route"] or v["lu"]})
+    print(f"wrote {out_p} sha256 {sha256(out_p)}")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()

 docs/levin-phase-floor/06m/census/scripts/refs_common.py), changed only where marked "prompt 07": ROOT is

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07a section 6, the user's references (new in 07a; read only): are the captured integrands the
+same? For each selected call, its capture at h (out/refs/captures_h.json) and its capture with the parent's module
+(out/refs/captures_parent.json) are unpickled (each blob's SHA-256 checked) and compared: x_span (hex), the keyword
+arguments, and the integrand's two components f[0], f[1], the raw phase theta["theta"], theta["theta_deriv"] and
+theta["theta_mod_2pi"] sampled at 1001 points evenly spaced over x_span, by bit pattern. Then, among the h captures, the
+same comparison across the two rtols of one call (kw's rtol and atol aside) and between index 2 (G+q-r) and index 3
+(G-q+r) of one row and rtol. Writes OUT_JSON. Usage: PYTHONPATH=<tree h>:<scripts> python -B refs_integrand_07a.py OUT_JSON
+"""
+import hashlib
+import json
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import numpy as np
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+from prov import WIDER, header, sha256  # noqa: E402
+
+CH = WIDER / "out" / "refs" / "captures_h.json"
+CP = WIDER / "out" / "refs" / "captures_parent.json"
+
+
+def load(e):
+    import ray.cloudpickle as cp
+
+    with open(Path(e["dir"]) / e["pickle"], "rb") as f:
+        f.seek(e["offset"])
+        blob = f.read(e["length"])
+    if hashlib.sha256(blob).hexdigest() != e["sha256"]:
+        raise SystemExit(f"{e['sid']}: sha256 differs")
+    return cp.loads(blob)
+
+
+def samples(rec):
+    a, b = rec["x_span"]
+    xs = np.linspace(min(a, b), max(a, b), 1001)
+    th = rec["theta"]
+    out = {"f0": [float(rec["f"][0](x)) for x in xs], "f1": [float(rec["f"][1](x)) for x in xs],
+           "theta": [float(th["theta"](x)) for x in xs], "theta_deriv": [float(th["theta_deriv"](x)) for x in xs]}
+    if "theta_mod_2pi" in th:
+        out["theta_mod_2pi"] = [float(th["theta_mod_2pi"](x)) for x in xs]
+    return {k: [v_.hex() for v_ in v] for k, v in out.items()}
+
+
+def cmp(r1, s1, r2, s2, kw_aside=()):
+    res = {"x_span": [float(x).hex() for x in r1["x_span"]] == [float(x).hex() for x in r2["x_span"]]}
+    k1 = {k: repr(v) for k, v in r1["kw"].items() if k not in kw_aside}
+    k2 = {k: repr(v) for k, v in r2["kw"].items() if k not in kw_aside}
+    res["kw"] = k1 == k2
+    if k1 != k2:
+        res["kw_differs"] = sorted(k for k in set(k1) | set(k2) if k1.get(k) != k2.get(k))
+    for k in s1:
+        res[k] = s1[k] == s2.get(k)
+    res["all_equal"] = all(v for k, v in res.items() if k != "kw_differs")
+    return res
+
+
+def main():
+    out_p = Path(sys.argv[1])
+    hdr = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), "levin-phase-floor 07a: the reference calls' integrands compared", [CH, CP])
+    H = {e["sid"]: e for e in json.loads(CH.read_text())}
+    Pa = {e["sid"]: e for e in json.loads(CP.read_text())}
+    recs, samp = {}, {}
+    for sid in sorted(H):
+        recs[("h", sid)] = load(H[sid])
+        recs[("p", sid)] = load(Pa[sid])
+        samp[("h", sid)] = samples(recs[("h", sid)])
+        samp[("p", sid)] = samples(recs[("p", sid)])
+    doc = {"header": hdr, "h_vs_parent": {}, "across_rtol": {}, "index2_vs_index3": {}}
+    for sid in sorted(H):
+        doc["h_vs_parent"][sid] = cmp(recs[("h", sid)], samp[("h", sid)], recs[("p", sid)], samp[("p", sid)])
+    for sid in sorted(H):
+        if "-1e-7-" in sid:
+            s8 = sid.replace("-1e-7-", "-1e-8-")
+            doc["across_rtol"][f"{sid}~{s8}"] = cmp(recs[("h", sid)], samp[("h", sid)], recs[("h", s8)], samp[("h", s8)], ("rtol", "atol"))
+        if sid.endswith("-2"):
+            s3 = sid[:-2] + "-3"
+            doc["index2_vs_index3"][f"{sid}~{s3}"] = cmp(recs[("h", sid)], samp[("h", sid)], recs[("h", s3)], samp[("h", s3)])
+    doc["kw_example"] = {k: repr(v) for k, v in recs[("h", sorted(H)[0])]["kw"].items()}
+    out_p.write_text(json.dumps(doc, indent=1))
+    for sec in ("h_vs_parent", "across_rtol", "index2_vs_index3"):
+        for k, v in doc[sec].items():
+            print(sec, k, "ALL EQUAL" if v["all_equal"] else {kk: vv for kk, vv in v.items() if vv is not True})
+    print("kw", doc["kw_example"])
+    print(f"wrote {out_p} sha256 {sha256(out_p)}")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()
diff -ruN /Users/ds283/Documents/Code/SecondaryGWKit-lpf07a-4bf50d3/docs/levin-phase-floor/07/census/scripts/refs_projection_07a.py scripts/refs_projection_07a.py
--- /Users/ds283/Documents/Code/SecondaryGWKit-lpf07a-4bf50d3/docs/levin-phase-floor/07/census/scripts/refs_projection_07a.py	1970-01-01 01:00:00
+++ scripts/refs_projection_07a.py	2026-11-12 20:35:10

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07a section 6, the user's references: project each reading's wall time before any is taken.
+New in 07a, after 07's refs_projection_07.py (a record, not edited), whose features (``features``, imported from it,
+unchanged) and model form it keeps. 07's calibration (06g's readings on this host, P1's, the laptop's) is no longer all
+on this host, so the model is fitted instead to 07's own 74 V4 readings, taken on this host with the same tool
+(tools/filon_reference.py d37e633b..., byte-identical in h) and the same driver settings:
+- per reading kind (impl, res): wall = alpha n_seg + beta span + gamma D, by non-negative least squares over the readings
+  of that kind among 07's 24 calls (QAWO: span_q, D_q; FCC: span_f, D_f), each call's features from its 07 capture
+  (var/levin-phase-floor-07/census/state/v4/inputs/<sid>.pkl, read only);
+- the projection of each of the four reference calls (out/refs/inputs.json), with the fit's largest ratio of actual to
+  fitted wall over 07's readings as a margin;
+- 07's ruling at its stop 4 decides which readings are taken: FCC r1 and r2 on every call, QAWO r1 and r2 on a call whose
+  QAWO readings each project at 30 minutes or less (with the margin).
+Writes OUT_JSON. Usage: PYTHONPATH=<tree h>:<scripts> python -B refs_projection_07a.py OUT_JSON
+"""
+import json
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import numpy as np
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+from prov import REC07, WIDER, header, sha256  # noqa: E402
+from refs_projection_07 import features, load_capture  # noqa: E402  (07's, unchanged)
+
+V4IN = REC07 / "out" / "v4" / "inputs.json"
+READ = REC07 / "out" / "v4" / "readings"
+MINE = WIDER / "out" / "refs" / "inputs.json"
+KINDS = [("qawo", 1), ("qawo", 2), ("fcc", 1), ("fcc", 2)]
+
+
+def nnls(A, y):
+    from scipy.optimize import nnls as _n
+
+    return _n(A, y)[0]
+
+
+def main():
+    out_p = Path(sys.argv[1])
+    hdr = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), "levin-phase-floor 07a: the user's references, projected", [V4IN, MINE])
+    v4 = json.loads(V4IN.read_text())["inputs"]
+    feat07 = {}
+    for sid, e in sorted(v4.items()):
+        if sha256(e["path"]) != e["sha256"]:
+            raise SystemExit(f"{sid}: 07's capture has another SHA-256")
+        feat07[sid] = features(load_capture(e["path"]))
+    fits = {}
+    for impl, res in KINDS:
+        X, y, names = [], [], []
+        for sid in sorted(v4):
+            p = READ / f"{sid}-{impl}-r{res}.json"
+            if not p.exists():
+                continue
+            f = feat07[sid]
+            X.append([f["n_seg"], f["span_q" if impl == "qawo" else "span_f"], f["D_q" if impl == "qawo" else "D_f"]])
+            y.append(json.loads(p.read_text())["wall_s"])
+            names.append(sid)
+        A, Y = np.array(X, float), np.array(y, float)
+        coef = nnls(A, Y)
+        fit = A @ coef
+        ratio = Y / np.where(fit > 0, fit, np.nan)
+        fits[f"{impl}_r{res}"] = {"n": len(y), "alpha_beta_gamma": coef.tolist(), "max_actual_over_fit": float(np.nanmax(ratio)),
+                                  "min_actual_over_fit": float(np.nanmin(ratio)), "readings": dict(zip(names, y))}
+    mine = json.loads(MINE.read_text())["inputs"]
+    calls = {}
+    for sid, e in sorted(mine.items()):
+        if sha256(e["path"]) != e["sha256"]:
+            raise SystemExit(f"{sid}: capture SHA-256 differs")
+        f = features(load_capture(e["path"]))
+        proj = {}
+        for impl, res in KINDS:
+            k = f"{impl}_r{res}"
+            a, b, g = fits[k]["alpha_beta_gamma"]
+            w = a * f["n_seg"] + b * f["span_q" if impl == "qawo" else "span_f"] + g * f["D_q" if impl == "qawo" else "D_f"]
+            proj[k] = {"fit_s": w, "with_margin_s": w * max(1.0, fits[k]["max_actual_over_fit"])}
+        qawo_ok = all(proj[f"qawo_r{r}"]["with_margin_s"] <= 1800.0 for r in (1, 2))
+        calls[sid] = {"features": f, "projection": proj, "qawo": qawo_ok,
+                      "readings": ([("qawo", 1), ("qawo", 2)] if qawo_ok else []) + [("fcc", 1), ("fcc", 2)]}
+    total = sum(calls[s]["projection"][f"{i}_r{r}"]["with_margin_s"] for s in calls for i, r in calls[s]["readings"])
+    doc = {"header": hdr, "fits": fits, "calls": calls, "total_with_margin_s": total}
+    out_p.write_text(json.dumps(doc, indent=1))
+    for k, v in fits.items():
+        print(k, {kk: vv for kk, vv in v.items() if kk != "readings"})
+    for s, c in calls.items():
+        print(s, c["features"], {k: round(v["with_margin_s"]) for k, v in c["projection"].items()}, "QAWO" if c["qawo"] else "FCC only")
+    print(f"total, one reading at a time, with margin: {total:.0f} s")
+    print(f"wrote {out_p} sha256 {sha256(out_p)}")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()

--- /Users/ds283/Documents/Code/SecondaryGWKit-lpf07a-4bf50d3/docs/levin-phase-floor/07/census/scripts/refs_select_07a.py	1970-01-01 01:00:00

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07a section 6, the user's references (state/ruling_w5.md item 2; new in 07a; read only but for
+its output): the calls of rows 3-678 and 1-34599 as out/scores/moves_beyond.json and the aux_only table identify them --
+the group calls index 2 (G+q-r) and 3 (G-q+r), at 1e-7 and at 1e-8, the rtols each was flagged at -- with their census
+records at h and at 07 (value, abserr hex; num_regions, max_depth, converged; label). sid r<row>-<rtol>-<index>.
+Usage: python -B refs_select_07a.py OUT_JSON
+"""
+import json
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+from prov import WIDER, header, sha256  # noqa: E402
+
+CALLS = WIDER / "out" / "stop3" / "compare_census_calls.calls.jsonl"
+MB = WIDER / "out" / "scores" / "moves_beyond.json"
+ROWS = ("3-678", "1-34599")
+
+
+def main():
+    out_p = Path(sys.argv[1])
+    if out_p.exists():
+        raise SystemExit(f"{out_p} exists")
+    hdr = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), "levin-phase-floor 07a: the user's two reference calls, selected", [CALLS, MB])
+    mb = json.loads(MB.read_text())
+    flagged = {(b["unit"], b["kind"], b["index"]) for b in mb["beyond"] if b["beyond_both"]} | {
+        (s["unit"], s["kind"], s["index"]) for s in mb["aux_shrink_gt_2x"] if s["value_move_over_eh"] > 1}

+                    "index": c["index"], "kind": c["kind"], "notify_label": c["label"], "class": c["class"],

+                    "census07_value": c["value"][1], "census07_abserr": c["abserr"][1], "census07_num_regions": c["num_regions"][1],
+                    "census07_max_depth": c["max_depth"][1], "census07_converged": c["converged"][1]})
+    sel.sort(key=lambda e: e["sid"])
+    out_p.write_text(json.dumps(sel, indent=1))
+    for l in hdr:

+
+

+    sys.exit(main())

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07a section 6, the user's references: 07's census slice_v4_07.py (a record, not edited),
+changed only where marked "prompt 07a": the captures are the h captures of the user's calls (out/refs/captures_h.json,
+checked by out/refs/check_captures_h.json), and only the 1e-7 call of each (row, index) is sliced: the 1e-8 call's
+integrand is the same bit for bit (out/refs/integrands.json, across_rtol), so one reference serves both rtols.
+Writes state/refs/inputs/<sid>.pkl and out/refs/inputs.json. 07's text follows.
+
+levin-phase-floor prompt 07 section 6, V4: each of the 24 captures (and z12's calibration capture) as a file of its
+own, for tools.filon_reference (one captured call per pickle). After 06g's slice_refs.py (a record, not edited). Each
+call's blob is copied from its capture worker's inputs pickle, at the offset and length x1_capture.py recorded, and
+checked against the recorded SHA-256; only captures that reproduce the census (out/v4/check_captures.json) are
+sliced. Nothing is unpickled. Writes state/v4/inputs/<sid>.pkl (refusing to overwrite) and out/v4/inputs.json.
+Usage: python -B slice_v4_07.py"""
+import hashlib
+import json
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+from prov import WIDER, header, sha256  # noqa: E402
+
+CAPS = WIDER / "out" / "refs" / "captures_h.json"  # prompt 07a
+CHECK = WIDER / "out" / "refs" / "check_captures_h.json"  # prompt 07a
+INTEG = WIDER / "out" / "refs" / "integrands.json"  # prompt 07a
+
+
+def main():
+    out_dir = WIDER / "state" / "refs" / "inputs"  # prompt 07a
+    out_json = WIDER / "out" / "refs" / "inputs.json"  # prompt 07a
+    if out_json.exists():
+        raise SystemExit(f"{out_json} exists")
+    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    hdr = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), "levin-phase-floor 07a: the reference calls, one file each", [CAPS, CHECK, INTEG])  # prompt 07a
+    integ = json.loads(INTEG.read_text())["across_rtol"]  # prompt 07a
+    if not all(v["all_equal"] for v in integ.values()) or len(integ) != 4:
+        raise SystemExit("the integrands are not the same across the rtols")
+    chk = {r["sid"]: r for r in json.loads(CHECK.read_text())}
+    doc = {"header": hdr, "inputs": {}}
+    for e in json.loads(CAPS.read_text()):
+        sid = e["sid"]
+        if "-1e-8-" in sid:  # prompt 07a: the 1e-7 call's integrand serves both rtols
+            continue
+        if not (e["capture_matches_census"] and chk[sid]["reproduces_census"]):
+            raise SystemExit(f"{sid}: the capture does not reproduce the census")
+        with open(Path(e["dir"]) / e["pickle"], "rb") as f:
+            f.seek(e["offset"])
+            blob = f.read(e["length"])
+        if hashlib.sha256(blob).hexdigest() != e["sha256"]:
+            raise SystemExit(f"{sid}: sha256 differs")
+        p = out_dir / f"{sid}.pkl"
+        if p.exists():
+            raise SystemExit(f"{p} exists")
+        p.write_bytes(blob)
+        doc["inputs"][sid] = {"path": str(p), "sha256": sha256(p), "bytes": len(blob), "unit": e["unit"], "index": e["index"],
+                              "rtol": e["unit"].split("-")[1] + "-" + e["unit"].split("-")[2]}
+        print(f"{sid}: {p} sha256 {doc['inputs'][sid]['sha256']} ({len(blob)} bytes)")
+    out_json.write_text(json.dumps(doc, indent=1))
+    print(f"wrote {out_json} sha256 {sha256(out_json)}")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07a section 6: one pass over the census h (and 07's census beside it, by state/index_07.json,
+each line's SHA-256 checked) gathering what W2, W3, W5 and the descriptive items need. New in 07a, after 07's
+score_07.py (a record, not edited), whose V2 and V3 extraction (scan_07 against the returned leaves and the located
+roots) it keeps. Read only. W1, W7 and the classing are compare_07a.py's (out/stop3/), W4 score_v4_07a.py's, W6
+xcheck_07a.py's; this script decides no verdict (summarise_07a.py does, by state/predictions.md).
+
+Per unit: rtol, row, band, k, in the 720 (07's census scan at 1e-7, as 07 defined the 720), status, unit times, totals,
+row depth and group regions at h and at 07. Per call, kept when it carries anything W2, W3, W5 or a descriptive item
+reads: its route_07a summary (solves changed by class, the partition counts, forced and gated, the Clenshaw-Curtis
+regions with a Levin half, the margins, the largest |theta| at a gated end), every changed solve in full from the
+pickle (``changed_all``: V, 07's estimate, direction, role, class), guard_07 at h and 07 (points, windows with their stop
+records, unlocated minima, guarded counts), converged, value, abserr, depth and regions at both, the scan (V2's listed
+leaves with their window and guard flags, V3's sign changes and h), and an unconverged call's flag measures.
+
+Usage: PYTHONPATH=<tree h>:<scripts> python -B score_07a.py RUN_DIR OUT_JSON [--processes N]
+"""
+
+import argparse
+import hashlib
+import json
+import math
+import multiprocessing as mp
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+from prov import CHECKPOINT07_SHA256, RUN07, WIDER, header, sha256  # noqa: E402
+
+INDEX07 = WIDER / "state" / "index_07.json"
+ROWS = WIDER / "state" / "rows.json"
+SIX = 6.0 * math.pi
+RUN = None
+
+
+def H(x):
+    return None if x is None else float.fromhex(x["hex"] if isinstance(x, dict) else x)
+
+
+def load_pickle(path, off, length, sha):
+    import ray.cloudpickle as cp
+
+    with open(path, "rb") as f:
+        f.seek(off)
+        blob = f.read(length)
+    if hashlib.sha256(blob).hexdigest() != sha:
+        raise RuntimeError(f"{path}@{off}: sha256 differs")
+    return cp.loads(blob)
+
+
+def unit_time(d):
+    t = d.get("wall_s") or 0.0
+    for k in ("scan_s", "scan_07_s", "cc_floor_s", "declared_06h_s", "checked_06g_s"):
+        t += d.get(k) or 0.0
+    for k in ("w3", "deep_lines", "analytic_06h"):
+        t += (d.get(k) or {}).get("seconds") or 0.0
+    return t
+
+
+def call_flag(c, total_abserr):
+    v, e = H(c["value"]), H(c["abserr"])
+    req = max(c["atol"], c["rtol"] * abs(v))
+    return {"within_request": e <= req, "over_request_ratio": e / req if req > 0 else math.inf,
+            "node_limited": bool(c.get("node_limited")), "untrusted": int((c.get("leaves") or {}).get("untrusted") or 0),
+            "n_at_depth_max": c.get("n_at_depth_max"), "share": e / total_abserr if total_abserr else None}
+
+
+def one(args):
+    uid, off, length, ent, rowinfo, in720, run = args
+    with open(Path(run) / "checkpoint.jsonl", "rb") as f:
+        f.seek(off)
+        line = f.read(length)
+    r = json.loads(line)
+    assert r["unit"] == uid
+    d = r["data"]
+    with open(RUN07 / "checkpoint.jsonl", "rb") as f:
+        f.seek(ent["offset"])
+        line7 = f.read(ent["length"])
+    if hashlib.sha256(line7).hexdigest() != ent["line_sha256"]:
+        raise RuntimeError(f"{uid}: 07's line sha256 differs")
+    m = json.loads(line7)["data"]
+    out = {"unit": uid, "rtol": d["rtol"], "row": d["row"], "band": rowinfo["band"], "k": rowinfo["k"], "in720": in720,
+           "status": d["status"], "status_07": m["status"], "wall_s": [d.get("wall_s"), m.get("wall_s")],
+           "time_s": [unit_time(d), unit_time(m)], "scan_07_error": d.get("scan_07_error"),
+           "post_unit": d.get("route_07a_post_unit") and {k: v for k, v in d["route_07a_post_unit"].items() if k != "changed"}}
+    if d["status"] != "done":
+        return out
+    calls = [("group", c) for c in d["calls"]] + [("analytic", c) for c in d["other_calls"]]
+    calls7 = {(k, c["index"]): c for k, c in [("group", c) for c in m["calls"]] + [("analytic", c) for c in m["other_calls"]]}
+    res, res7 = d.get("result") or {}, m.get("result") or {}
+    out["total"] = [(res.get("total") or {}).get("hex"), (res7.get("total") or {}).get("hex")]
+    out["total_abserr"] = [(res.get("total_abserr") or {}).get("hex"), (res7.get("total_abserr") or {}).get("hex")]
+    out["total_converged"] = [res.get("total_converged"), res7.get("total_converged")]
+    out["row_depth"] = [max((c["max_depth"] for _, c in calls), default=None),
+                        max((c["max_depth"] for c in calls7.values()), default=None)]
+    out["group_regions"] = [sum(c["num_regions"] for c in d["calls"]), sum(c["num_regions"] for c in m["calls"])]
+    out["all_regions"] = [sum(c["num_regions"] for _, c in calls), sum(c["num_regions"] for c in calls7.values())]
+    out["n_solves"] = sum(((c.get("route_07a") or {}).get("n_solves") or 0) for _, c in calls)
+    tae, tae7 = H(res.get("total_abserr")), H(res7.get("total_abserr"))
+    need = any((c.get("guard_07") or {}).get("locates") or (c.get("guard_07") or {}).get("guards")
+               or ((c.get("route_07a") or {}).get("n_route_differs") or 0) + ((c.get("route_07a") or {}).get("n_lu_differs") or 0)
+               > (c.get("route_07a") or {}).get("n_changed_kept", 0) for _, c in calls)
+    need |= any((s.get("n_regions_listed") or 0) > 0 or (s.get("n_sign_changes") or 0) > 0 for s in d.get("scan_07") or [])
+    rec = load_pickle(Path(run) / d["pickle"], d["pickle_offset"], d["pickle_length"], d["pickle_record_sha256"]) if need else None
+    g7full = {e["index"]: e for e in (rec or {}).get("guard_07") or []}
+    s7full = {e["index"]: e for e in (rec or {}).get("scan_07") or []}
+    r7afull = {e["index"]: e for e in (rec or {}).get("route_07a") or []}
+    regarr = {e["index"]: e for e in ((rec or {}).get("regions") or []) + ((rec or {}).get("other_regions") or [])}
+    out["calls"] = []
+    agg = {"partition_forced": 0, "partition_nonforced": 0, "cc_lh": 0, "cc_lh_07est": 0, "guarded": 0,
+           "guard_at_depth_max": 0, "no_build": 0, "route_errors": 0, "theta_max": {}, "m6": None, "m20": None,
+           "n_route": 0, "n_lu": 0}
+    for kind, c in calls:
+        g7 = c.get("guard_07") or {}
+        c7 = calls7.get((kind, c["index"]))
+        g77 = (c7 or {}).get("guard_07") or {}
+        ra = c.get("route_07a")
+        if not isinstance(ra, dict) or "error" in ra:
+            agg["route_errors"] += 1
+            ra = {}
+        part = ra.get("partition") or {}
+        agg["partition_forced"] += part.get("forced", 0)
+        agg["partition_nonforced"] += ra.get("n_partition_nonforced", 0)
+        agg["cc_lh"] += ra.get("n_cc_with_levin_half", 0)
+        agg["cc_lh_07est"] += ra.get("n_cc_with_levin_half_07est", 0)
+        agg["guarded"] += int(g7.get("num_guarded_regions") or 0)
+        agg["guard_at_depth_max"] += int(g7.get("num_guard_at_depth_max") or 0)
+        agg["no_build"] += ra.get("n_no_build", 0)
+        agg["n_route"] += ra.get("n_route_differs", 0)
+        agg["n_lu"] += ra.get("n_lu_differs", 0)
+        th = H(ra.get("theta_end_max_nonforced"))
+        if th is not None and th == th and (agg["theta_max"].get(kind) is None or th > agg["theta_max"][kind][0]):

+                agg[nm] = (H(mm["margin"]), kind, c["index"], {k_: (H(v_) if isinstance(v_, dict) else v_) for k_, v_ in mm.items()})
+        row = {"kind": kind, "index": c["index"], "label": c["notify_label"], "converged": [c["converged"], (c7 or {}).get("converged")],
+               "value": [c["value"]["hex"], (c7 or {}).get("value", {}).get("hex")],
+               "abserr": [c["abserr"]["hex"], (c7 or {}).get("abserr", {}).get("hex")],
+               "max_depth": [c["max_depth"], (c7 or {}).get("max_depth")], "regions": [c["num_regions"], (c7 or {}).get("num_regions")],
+               "n_at_depth_max": c.get("n_at_depth_max"), "locates": g7.get("locates"), "guards": g7.get("guards"),
+               "n_windows": [g7.get("n_windows"), g77.get("n_windows")], "n_points": [g7.get("n_points"), g77.get("n_points")],
+               "skipped": [g7.get("location_skipped_phases") or [], g77.get("location_skipped_phases") or []],
+               "route": {k_: ra.get(k_) for k_ in ("n_solves", "n_route_differs", "n_lu_differs", "differs_by_class", "partition",
+                                                    "n_cc_with_levin_half", "n_cc_with_levin_half_07est", "n_forced")}}
+        keep = bool(g7.get("locates") or g7.get("guards") or g77.get("locates") or row["skipped"][0] or row["skipped"][1]
+                    or not c["converged"] or not (c7 or {}).get("converged") or ra.get("n_route_differs") or ra.get("n_lu_differs")
+                    or ra.get("n_partition_nonforced") or part.get("forced") or ra.get("n_cc_with_levin_half"))
+        if ra.get("n_route_differs") or ra.get("n_lu_differs"):
+            full_r = (r7afull.get(c["index"]) or {}).get("record") or {}
+            row["changed"] = [{k_: x.get(k_) for k_ in ("a", "b", "desc", "role", "class", "made", "returned", "depth",
+                                                           "V", "est_07", "est_over_V", "decision", "is_direct", "lu_used")}
+                              for x in (full_r.get("changed_all") if full_r else ra.get("changed") or [])]
+        if ra.get("n_cc_with_levin_half"):
+            row["cc_with_levin_half"] = ra.get("cc_with_levin_half")
+        if not c["converged"]:
+            row["flag"] = call_flag(c, tae)
+        if c7 is not None and not c7["converged"]:
+            row["flag_07"] = call_flag(c7, tae7)
+        if g7.get("locates") or g7.get("guards") or g77.get("locates"):
+            full = g7full.get(c["index"]) or {}
+            row["node_limited"] = c.get("node_limited")
+            row["points"] = [{"kind": p["kind"], "found_as": p.get("found_as"), "x": p["x"], "lambda": p.get("lambda"),
+                              "window_pieces": p.get("window_pieces")} for p in full.get("stationary_points") or []]
+            row["windows"] = [{k: w.get(k) for k in ("lo", "hi", "iroff1_count", "stopped", "stopped_after_split",
+                                                    "leaves_at_stop", "leaves_falling_at_stop", "max_depth_at_stop")}
+                              | {"n_pieces": max(len(w.get("cuts") or []) - 1, 0)} for w in full.get("stationary_windows") or []]
+            row["windows_07"] = [{k: w.get(k) for k in ("iroff1_count", "stopped", "leaves_falling_at_stop", "n_pieces")}
+                                 for w in g77.get("windows") or []]
+            row["n_windows_stopped"] = [g7.get("n_windows_stopped"), g77.get("n_windows_stopped")]
+            row["num_window_pieces"] = [full.get("num_window_pieces"), g77.get("num_window_pieces")]
+            row["num_guarded_regions"] = full.get("num_guarded_regions")
+            row["returned"] = g7.get("returned")
+        row["unlocated_minima_lambda"] = [m_.get("lambda") for m_ in (g7full.get(c["index"]) or {}).get("unlocated_minima") or []]
+        if not row["unlocated_minima_lambda"] and g7.get("n_unlocated_minima"):
+            row["unlocated_minima_lambda"] = [m_.get("lambda") for m_ in g7.get("unlocated_minima") or []]
+        keep |= bool(row["unlocated_minima_lambda"])
+        s7c = [s for s in d.get("scan_07") or [] if s["index"] == c["index"]]
+        if s7c:

+                keep = True
+            else:

+                    row["listed"] = [{"region_index": x["region_index"], "how": x["how"], "branch": x["branch"],
+                                      "depth": x["depth"], "start": x["start"], "end": x["end"],
+                                      "window": int(ra_["window_07"][x["region_index"]]),
+                                      "guarded": int(ra_["guarded_07"][x["region_index"]]),
+                                      "guard_at_cap": int(ra_["guard_at_depth_max_07"][x["region_index"]])}
+                                     for x in full_s["listed"]]
+                    keep = True
+                row["scan"] = {"h": sc["h"]["hex"], "n_sign_changes": sc["n_sign_changes"],
+                               "min_over_max_abs_theta_deriv": sc.get("min_over_max_abs_theta_deriv"),
+                               "n_nonfinite": sc.get("n_nonfinite"),
+                               "sign_change_x": [] if not sc["n_sign_changes"] else list(full_s["sign_change_x"])}
+                keep |= bool(sc["n_sign_changes"])
+        else:
+            row["scanned"] = False
+        if keep:
+            out["calls"].append(row)
+    out["agg"] = agg
+    out["n_calls"] = len(calls)
+    out["n_scanned"] = len(d.get("scan_07") or [])
+    out["n_scanned_by_kind"] = {"group": sum(1 for s in d.get("scan_07") or [] if s["kind"] == "group"),
+                                "analytic": sum(1 for s in d.get("scan_07") or [] if s["kind"] == "analytic")}
+    return out
+
+
+def main():
+    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
+    ap.add_argument("run_dir")
+    ap.add_argument("out_json")
+    ap.add_argument("--processes", type=int, default=6)
+    a = ap.parse_args()
+    if a.processes > 6:
+        raise SystemExit("at most six processes")
+    run = Path(a.run_dir).resolve()
+    ck = run / "checkpoint.jsonl"
+    hdr = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), "levin-phase-floor 07a: W2, W3, W5 and the descriptive items, gathered from the census h",
+                 [ck, INDEX07, ROWS])
+    idx = json.loads(INDEX07.read_text())
+    if idx["checkpoint_sha256"] != CHECKPOINT07_SHA256:
+        raise SystemExit("the index is not of 07's checkpoint")
+    rows = {r["name"]: r for r in json.loads(ROWS.read_text())["rows"]}
+    jobs, off = [], 0
+    with open(ck, "rb") as f:
+        for line in f:
+            uid = line[:120].decode()[len('{"unit": "'):].split('"', 1)[0]
+            jobs.append([uid, off, len(line)])
+            off += len(line)
+    sp_rows = set()  # the 720: rows whose 1e-7 G+q+r call shows a sign change in 07's census's 06a scan
+    with open(RUN07 / "checkpoint.jsonl") as f:
+        for line in f:
+            r = json.loads(line)
+            if r["data"]["rtol"] == "1e-7" and any(s and s["n_sign_changes"] > 0 for s in r["data"]["scan"] or []):
+                sp_rows.add(r["data"]["row"])
+    if len(sp_rows) != 720:
+        raise SystemExit(f"the 720: {len(sp_rows)} rows")
+    full = []
+    for uid, o, n in jobs:
+        p = uid.split("-")
+        if len(p) != 5 or p[0] != "h":
+            raise SystemExit(f"unexpected unit {uid}")
+        row = f"{p[3]}-{p[4]}"
+        full.append((uid, o, n, idx["units"]["g-" + uid.split("-", 1)[1]], rows[row], row in sp_rows, str(run)))
+    with mp.get_context("spawn").Pool(a.processes) as pool:
+        units = list(pool.imap(one, full, chunksize=16))
+    doc = {"header": hdr, "run": str(run), "checkpoint_sha256": sha256(ck), "n_units": len(units), "n_720": len(sp_rows), "units": units}
+    Path(a.out_json).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    Path(a.out_json).write_text(json.dumps(doc, default=str))
+    print(f"units {len(units)}; statuses {sorted({u['status'] for u in units})}; wrote {a.out_json} sha256 {sha256(a.out_json)}")
+    return 0
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    sys.exit(main())

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07a section 6, the user's references (state/ruling_w5.md item 2): score the two rows' calls.
+New in 07a, after 07's score_v4_07.py (a record, not edited), whose reading-usability check and exact arithmetic it keeps.
+Read only.
+
+- **Readings:** out/refs/readings/<sid>-<impl>-r<res>.json for the four captured calls (the 1e-7 capture of each row and
+  index; the 1e-8 call's integrand is the same bit for bit, out/refs/integrands.json). A reading is usable if made by the
+  tool d37e633b..., on the capture out/refs/inputs.json lists (SHA-256 checked), Route B, Ray not initialised, every
+  non-monotone segment integrated directly, with a value (07's check, unchanged).
+- **The reference:** FCC r2's ``value_30``. **Its stated error:** the spread of the call's usable readings (largest minus
+  smallest ``value_30``), 07's agreement measure.
+- **Each selected call (row, index, rtol):** |v - ref| / abserr at h (07a's census) and at 07 (07's census), each with its
+  abserr; and whether the stated error is at least 10x below both abserrs (spread <= min(abserr_h, abserr_07)/10). Where
+  it is not, the call is reported and not scored. Also 07's own agreement test (spread <= min/100) beside it.
+Exact: doubles as Fractions from their hex, readings as Fractions of their 30-digit strings.
+Usage: python -B score_refs_07a.py OUT_JSON OUT_MD
+"""
+import json
+import sys
+from fractions import Fraction
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+from prov import WIDER, header, sha256  # noqa: E402
+
+SEL = WIDER / "state" / "refs" / "selection.json"
+INP = WIDER / "out" / "refs" / "inputs.json"
+INTEG = WIDER / "out" / "refs" / "integrands.json"
+PLAN = WIDER / "state" / "plan_references.json"
+RD = WIDER / "out" / "refs" / "readings"
+TOOL_SHA256 = "d37e633bf54a179114b2462529eaaa3836cccff8fe22e0250e6d36d61992c7be"
+
+
+def FH(h):
+    return Fraction(float.fromhex(h))
+
+
+def main():
+    out_json, out_md = Path(sys.argv[1]), Path(sys.argv[2])
+    hdr = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), "levin-phase-floor 07a: the user's references scored", [SEL, INP, INTEG, PLAN])
+    inputs = json.loads(INP.read_text())["inputs"]
+    if not all(v["all_equal"] for v in json.loads(INTEG.read_text())["across_rtol"].values()):
+        raise SystemExit("the integrands differ across the rtols")
+    plan = {j["name"] for j in json.loads(PLAN.read_text())["stages"][0]["jobs"]}
+    refs = {}
+    for sid, e in sorted(inputs.items()):
+        readings, notes, walls = {}, [], {}
+        for impl, r in (("qawo", 1), ("qawo", 2), ("fcc", 1), ("fcc", 2)):
+            name = f"{sid}-{impl}-r{r}"
+            if name not in plan:
+                continue
+            p = RD / f"{name}.json"
+            if not p.exists():
+                notes.append(f"{name}: missing")
+                continue
+            d = json.loads(p.read_text())
+            why = []
+            if d.get("tool_sha256") != TOOL_SHA256:
+                why.append("tool sha256")
+            if d.get("pickle_sha256") != e["sha256"] or sha256(d["pickle"]) != e["sha256"]:
+                why.append("pickle sha256")
+            if d.get("route") != "B":
+                why.append("route")
+            ri = d.get("ray_initialized")
+            if (any(bool(x) for x in ri) if isinstance(ri, list) else ri):
+                why.append("ray initialised")
+            if any((not s.get("monotone")) and not s.get("direct") for s in d.get("segments") or []):
+                why.append("a non-monotone segment not integrated directly")
+            if d.get("value_30") is None:
+                why.append("no value")
+            if why:
+                notes.append(f"{name}: unusable ({', '.join(why)})")
+                continue
+            readings[f"{impl}_r{r}"] = Fraction(d["value_30"])
+            walls[f"{impl}_r{r}"] = d["wall_s"]
+        if "fcc_r2" not in readings:
+            raise SystemExit(f"{sid}: no usable FCC r2 reading ({notes})")
+        vals = list(readings.values())
+        refs[sid[: -len("-1e-7-2")] + sid[-2:]] = {"capture": sid, "ref": readings["fcc_r2"], "spread": max(vals) - min(vals),
+                                                   "readings": readings, "notes": notes, "walls": walls}
+    calls = []
+    for c in json.loads(SEL.read_text()):
+        key = c["sid"][: -len("-1e-7-2")] + c["sid"][-2:]
+        R = refs[key]
+        vh, eh, v7, e7 = FH(c["census_value"]), FH(c["census_abserr"]), FH(c["census07_value"]), FH(c["census07_abserr"])
+        ok10 = R["spread"] <= min(eh, e7) / 10
+        row = {"sid": c["sid"], "unit": c["unit"], "index": c["index"], "class": c["class"], "label": c["notify_label"],
+               "reference_capture": R["capture"], "ref_30": str(float(R["ref"])), "ref_exact_30": None,
+               "stated_error_spread": float(R["spread"]), "readings": {k: str(float(v)) for k, v in R["readings"].items()},
+               "reading_notes": R["notes"], "reading_walls_s": R["walls"],
+               "value_h": c["census_value"], "abserr_h": c["census_abserr"], "abserr_h_float": float(eh), "converged_h": c["census_converged"],
+               "value_07": c["census07_value"], "abserr_07": c["census07_abserr"], "abserr_07_float": float(e7), "converged_07": c["census07_converged"],
+               "regions_h_07": [c["census_num_regions"], c["census07_num_regions"]],
+               "stated_error_over_min_abserr": float(R["spread"] / min(eh, e7)),
+               "stated_error_10x_below_both": ok10, "readings_agree_07_rule": R["spread"] <= min(eh, e7) / 100}
+        if ok10:
+            row["ratio_h"] = float(abs(vh - R["ref"]) / eh)
+            row["ratio_07"] = float(abs(v7 - R["ref"]) / e7)
+            row["honest_h"] = abs(vh - R["ref"]) <= eh
+            row["honest_07"] = abs(v7 - R["ref"]) <= e7
+        else:
+            row["verdict"] = "the stated error is not 10x below both abserrs: reported, not scored"
+        calls.append(row)
+    out_json.write_text(json.dumps({"header": hdr, "rule": __doc__, "calls": calls}, indent=1, default=str))
+    L = hdr + ["", "# The user's references (rows 3-678 and 1-34599)", "",
+               "| call | class | ref (FCC r2) | stated error (spread) | spread / min(abserr) | abserr_h | abserr_07 | "
+               "|v_h - ref|/abserr_h | |v_07 - ref|/abserr_07 | regions h / 07 |",
+               "|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|"]
+    for r in calls:
+        L.append(f"| {r['sid']} | {r['class']} | {r['ref_30']} | {r['stated_error_spread']:.3g} | {r['stated_error_over_min_abserr']:.3g} | "
+                 f"{r['abserr_h_float']:.4g} | {r['abserr_07_float']:.4g} | "
+                 + (f"{r['ratio_h']:.4g} | {r['ratio_07']:.4g}" if "ratio_h" in r else "not scored | not scored")
+                 + f" | {r['regions_h_07'][0]} / {r['regions_h_07'][1]} |")
+    out_md.write_text("\n".join(L) + "\n")
+    print("\n".join(L[len(hdr):]))
+    print(f"wrote {out_json} sha256 {sha256(out_json)}; {out_md} sha256 {sha256(out_md)}")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07a section 6, W4: V4's 33 calls re-scored from 07a's census h against the readings 07
+scored them on, by 07's rule. Copied from 07's census score_v4_07.py (a record, not edited), changed only where marked
+"prompt 07a": "the new" is h's census (the run given), "the before" is 07's census (its checkpoint read by
+state/index_07.json, each line's SHA-256 checked); 07's V4 records are read from 07's working records
+(var/levin-phase-floor-07/census/: state/plan_references.json, state/v4_reading_list_20261109.json, state/v4_selection.json,
+out/v4/inputs.json, out/v4/readings/; read only); 06g's scores from the tree h. The tolerance is therefore
+min(abserr_07, abserr_h)/100 (state/predictions.md part 5), and each call also carries 07's own recorded verdict,
+tolerance and ratio (07's v4_scores.json) beside it. Keys keep 07's names: ``_g`` is h, ``_06m`` is 07.
+Usage: python -B score_v4_07a.py RUN_DIR OUT_JSON OUT_MD
+
+07's text follows.
+levin-phase-floor prompt 07 section 6, V4: score the 24 calls against their references, and z00, z12 and Z3's seven
+against 06g's, and give "the stationary-point calls before". New in 07. Read only.
+
+**The rule** (state/predictions.md, V4, as the user ruled at stop 4; the log's deviation 27):
+- the tolerance of a call is min(abserr_06m, abserr_g)/100, both the call's own census abserrs at its rtol;
+- a call with QAWO r1, r2 and FCC r1, r2 is scored as written: its readings agree when their spread (largest minus
+  smallest real part, ``value_30``) is at most the tolerance; the reference is FCC r2;
+- a call without QAWO is scored on the FCC readings it has: with both, the reference is FCC r2 and the two must agree
+  within the same tolerance; with only one, against that reading, with no agreement test;
+- a call whose readings do not agree is reported, not scored; a scored call is honest iff |v_g - ref| <= abserr_g.
+- **A reading is usable** if it is present, made by the tool at g (SHA-256 d37e633b...), on the capture listed in
+  out/v4/inputs.json (SHA-256 checked), Route B, with Ray not initialised, and every non-monotone segment of its own
+  table integrated directly (``direct``; the tool's own condition for a sound reading of a segment with a stationary
+  point). An unusable or missing reading is recorded, and the call is scored on the rest by the rule above.
+- **z00, z12 and Z3's seven** are scored on 06g's readings as scores_stage3/scores.json records them (``rows``: each
+  reading's ``value_30``; the readings 06g marked ``unusable`` left out; the reference its ``ref_30``, FCC r2), with this
+  rule's tolerance and agreement test, against 07's census value and abserr; 06m's from 06m's census.
+- **"The stationary-point calls before":** |v_06m - ref| / abserr_06m for every scored call.
+Every comparison is exact: doubles as Fractions from their hex, readings as Fractions of their 30-digit decimal strings.
+
+Usage: python -B score_v4_07.py OUT_JSON OUT_MD
+"""
+
+import hashlib
+import json
+import sys
+from fractions import Fraction
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+import refs_common as RC  # noqa: E402
+from prov import RUN06M, WIDER, header, sha256  # noqa: E402
+from prov import CHECKPOINT07_SHA256, H_TREE, REC07, RUN07  # noqa: E402  prompt 07a
+
+V4 = REC07 / "out" / "v4"  # prompt 07a: 07's records
+PLAN = REC07 / "state" / "plan_references.json"  # prompt 07a
+LIST = REC07 / "state" / "v4_reading_list_20261109.json"  # prompt 07a
+INPUTS = V4 / "inputs.json"
+SEL = REC07 / "state" / "v4_selection.json"  # prompt 07a
+SCORES06G = H_TREE / "docs" / "levin-phase-floor" / "06g" / "references" / "out" / "scores_stage3" / "scores.json"
+INDEX06M = WIDER / "state" / "index_07.json"  # prompt 07a: the before is 07's census
+V4_07 = REC07 / "out" / "v4" / "v4_scores.json"  # prompt 07a: 07's own scores, beside
+TOOL_SHA256 = "d37e633bf54a179114b2462529eaaa3836cccff8fe22e0250e6d36d61992c7be"
+Z = ("z00", "z12", "z07", "z16", "z18", "z21", "z24", "z28", "z29")
+
+
+def FH(h):
+    return Fraction(float.fromhex(h))
+
+
+def FD(s):
+    return Fraction(s)
+
+
+def census_call(ck, off, kind, index):
+    with open(ck, "rb") as f:
+        f.seek(off[0])
+        line = f.read(off[1])
+    d = json.loads(line)["data"]
+    key = "calls" if kind == "group" else "other_calls"
+    c = [c for c in d[key] if c["index"] == index]
+    if len(c) != 1:
+        raise SystemExit(f"{d['unit']} #{index}: not one call")
+    return c[0]
+
+
+def offsets07(units, ck=None):  # prompt 07a: the run's checkpoint (h's), its units h-... keyed as 07's g-...
+    out, off = {}, 0
+    with open(ck, "rb") as f:
+        for line in f:
+            uid = line[10:60].decode().split('"', 1)[0]
+            gu = "g-" + uid.split("-", 1)[1]
+            if gu in units:
+                out[gu] = (off, len(line))
+            off += len(line)
+    return out
+
+
+def score(readings, kinds_ref, vg, eg, vm, em):
+    """readings: {kind: Fraction}; kinds_ref: the reference kind. Returns the verdict dict."""
+    tol = min(eg, em) / 100
+    vals = list(readings.values())
+    spread = max(vals) - min(vals) if len(vals) > 1 else None
+    if len(vals) == 0:
+        return {"verdict": "no usable reading", "tol": float(tol)}
+    if len(vals) > 1 and spread > tol:
+        return {"verdict": "readings disagree (reported, not scored)", "tol": float(tol), "spread": float(spread),
+                "spread_over_tol": float(spread / tol)}
+    ref = readings[kinds_ref] if kinds_ref in readings else vals[0]
+    dg, dm = abs(vg - ref), abs(vm - ref)
+    return {"verdict": "honest" if dg <= eg else "NOT honest", "tol": float(tol),
+            "spread": None if spread is None else float(spread), "spread_over_tol": None if spread is None else float(spread / tol),
+            "ref_kind": kinds_ref if kinds_ref in readings else list(readings)[0], "ref": float(ref),
+            "ratio_g": float(dg / eg), "ratio_06m": float(dm / em), "honest_06m": dm <= em}
+
+
+def main():
+    run_h, out_json, out_md = Path(sys.argv[1]), Path(sys.argv[2]), Path(sys.argv[3])  # prompt 07a
+    CKH = run_h / "checkpoint.jsonl"  # prompt 07a
+    hdr = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), "levin-phase-floor 07a: W4 scored (V4's 33 calls at h, on 07's readings)",
+                 [PLAN, LIST, INPUTS, SEL, SCORES06G, CKH, INDEX06M, V4_07])  # prompt 07a
+    if json.loads(INDEX06M.read_text())["checkpoint_sha256"] != CHECKPOINT07_SHA256 or sha256(RUN07 / "checkpoint.jsonl") != CHECKPOINT07_SHA256:
+        raise SystemExit("07's census checkpoint has another SHA-256")  # prompt 07a
+    v4_07 = {x["sid"]: x for x in (lambda d: d["calls"] + d["z"])(json.loads(V4_07.read_text()))}  # prompt 07a
+    plan_jobs = {j["name"] for j in json.loads(PLAN.read_text())["stages"][0]["jobs"]}
+    inputs = json.loads(INPUTS.read_text())["inputs"]
+    sel = {c["sid"]: c for c in json.loads(SEL.read_text())["calls"]}
+    rows06g = {r["sid"]: r for r in json.loads(SCORES06G.read_text())["rows"]}
+    idx06 = json.loads(INDEX06M.read_text())["units"]
+    units = {c["unit"] for c in sel.values()} | {rows06g[z]["unit"] for z in Z}
+    off07 = offsets07(units, CKH)  # prompt 07a: h's offsets, by 07's unit names
+    res = {"header": hdr, "rule": __doc__, "calls": [], "z": []}
+    for sid in sorted(sel):
+        c = sel[sid]
+        g = census_call(CKH, off07[c["unit"]], "group", c["index"])  # prompt 07a: h
+        e6 = idx06[c["unit"]]
+        m = census_call(RUN07 / "checkpoint.jsonl", (e6["offset"], e6["length"]), "group", c["index"])  # prompt 07a: 07
+        vg, eg, vm, em = FH(g["value"]["hex"]), FH(g["abserr"]["hex"]), FH(m["value"]["hex"]), FH(m["abserr"]["hex"])
+        readings, notes = {}, []
+        for impl, r in (("qawo", 1), ("qawo", 2), ("fcc", 1), ("fcc", 2)):
+            name = f"{sid}-{impl}-r{r}"
+            if name not in plan_jobs:
+                continue
+            p = V4 / "readings" / f"{name}.json"
+            if not p.exists():
+                notes.append(f"{name}: missing")
+                continue
+            d = json.loads(p.read_text())
+            why = []
+            if d.get("tool_sha256") != TOOL_SHA256:
+                why.append("tool sha256")
+            if d.get("pickle_sha256") != inputs[sid]["sha256"] or sha256(d["pickle"]) != inputs[sid]["sha256"]:
+                why.append("pickle sha256")
+            if d.get("route") != "B":
+                why.append("route")
+            if any(bool(x) for x in (d.get("ray_initialized") or [])) if isinstance(d.get("ray_initialized"), list) else d.get("ray_initialized"):
+                why.append("ray initialised")
+            if any((not s.get("monotone")) and not s.get("direct") for s in d.get("segments") or []):
+                why.append("a non-monotone segment not integrated directly")
+            if d.get("value_30") is None:
+                why.append("no value")
+            if why:
+                notes.append(f"{name}: unusable ({', '.join(why)})")
+                continue
+            readings[f"{impl}_r{r}"] = FD(d["value_30"])
+        basis = ("four readings" if len(readings) == 4 else "FCC r1 and r2" if set(readings) == {"fcc_r1", "fcc_r2"}
+                 else "one FCC" if len(readings) == 1 and list(readings)[0].startswith("fcc") else f"other: {sorted(readings)}")
+        if basis.startswith("other"):
+            raise SystemExit(f"{sid}: a basis the rule does not decide: {sorted(readings)} ({notes})")
+        s = score(readings, "fcc_r2", vg, eg, vm, em)
+        res["calls"].append({"sid": sid, "unit": c["unit"], "rtol": c["rtol"], "index": c["index"], "band": c["band"],
+                             "k": c["k"], "basis": basis, "notes": notes, "value_g": g["value"]["hex"],
+                             "abserr_g": g["abserr"]["hex"], "value_06m": m["value"]["hex"], "abserr_06m": m["abserr"]["hex"],
+                             "converged_g": g["converged"], "converged_06m": m["converged"],
+                             "readings_30": {k: str(float(v)) for k, v in readings.items()},
+                             "wall_s": {f"{i}_r{r}": json.loads((V4 / "readings" / f"{sid}-{i}-r{r}.json").read_text())["wall_s"]
+                                        for i, r in (("qawo", 1), ("qawo", 2), ("fcc", 1), ("fcc", 2))
+                                        if (V4 / "readings" / f"{sid}-{i}-r{r}.json").exists()}, **s})
+    for z in Z:
+        r = rows06g[z]
+        kind = r["kind"]
+        g = census_call(CKH, off07[r["unit"]], kind, r["index"])  # prompt 07a: h
+        e6 = idx06[r["unit"]]
+        m = census_call(RUN07 / "checkpoint.jsonl", (e6["offset"], e6["length"]), kind, r["index"])  # prompt 07a: 07
+        if g["notify_label"] != r["label"] or m["notify_label"] != r["label"]:
+            raise SystemExit(f"{z}: label differs")
+        vg, eg, vm, em = FH(g["value"]["hex"]), FH(g["abserr"]["hex"]), FH(m["value"]["hex"]), FH(m["abserr"]["hex"])
+        unusable = set(r.get("unusable") or [])
+        readings = {k: FD(v["value_30"]) for k, v in r["readings"].items() if k not in unusable and v.get("value_30")}
+        if r.get("ref_30") is None or FD(r["ref_30"]) != readings.get("fcc_r2"):
+            raise SystemExit(f"{z}: 06g's ref_30 is not its FCC r2 reading")
+        s = score(readings, "fcc_r2", vg, eg, vm, em)
+        res["z"].append({"sid": z, "unit": r["unit"], "rtol": r["rtol"], "kind": kind, "index": r["index"], "label": r["label"],
+                         "basis": f"06g's readings: {sorted(readings)}", "unusable_06g": sorted(unusable),
+                         "value_g": g["value"]["hex"], "abserr_g": g["abserr"]["hex"], "value_06m": m["value"]["hex"],
+                         "abserr_06m": m["abserr"]["hex"], "converged_g": g["converged"], "converged_06m": m["converged"],
+                         "ratio_06g_record": r.get("r_g"), **s})
+    for x in res["calls"] + res["z"]:  # prompt 07a: 07's own score of the call, beside
+        o = v4_07[x["sid"]]
+        x["score_07"] = {k: o.get(k) for k in ("verdict", "tol", "ratio_g", "spread_over_tol", "value_g", "abserr_g", "converged_g")}
+        x["value_equal_07"] = x["value_g"] == o["value_g"]
+        x["abserr_equal_07"] = x["abserr_g"] == o["abserr_g"]
+    allc = res["calls"] + res["z"]
+    tot = {}
+    for x in allc:
+        tot[x["verdict"]] = tot.get(x["verdict"], 0) + 1
+    res["totals"] = {"24": {v: sum(1 for x in res["calls"] if x["verdict"] == v) for v in sorted({x["verdict"] for x in res["calls"]})},
+                     "z": {v: sum(1 for x in res["z"] if x["verdict"] == v) for v in sorted({x["verdict"] for x in res["z"]})},
+                     "all": tot,
+                     "basis_24": {b: sum(1 for x in res["calls"] if x["basis"] == b) for b in sorted({x["basis"] for x in res["calls"]})}}
+    scored = [x for x in allc if "ratio_g" in x]
+    res["V4"] = {"scored": len(scored), "honest": sum(1 for x in scored if x["verdict"] == "honest"),
+                 "not_honest": [x["sid"] for x in scored if x["verdict"] != "honest"],
+                 "reported_not_scored": [x["sid"] for x in allc if "ratio_g" not in x],
+                 "holds": all(x["verdict"] == "honest" for x in scored) and bool(scored)}
+    res["before"] = {"honest_06m": sum(1 for x in scored if x["honest_06m"]), "of": len(scored),
+                     "ratio_06m": {x["sid"]: x["ratio_06m"] for x in scored}}
+    out_json.write_text(json.dumps(res, indent=1, default=str))
+    L = [l for l in hdr] + ["", "# V4 scored", "",
+                            f"V4: {'HOLDS' if res['V4']['holds'] else 'FAILS'}: {res['V4']['honest']} of {res['V4']['scored']} scored "
+                            f"calls honest; not honest {res['V4']['not_honest']}; reported, not scored {res['V4']['reported_not_scored']}.",
+                            f"Totals: {json.dumps(res['totals'])}", "",
+                            "| call | unit (07's name) | rtol | basis | |v_h - ref|/abserr_h | |v_07 - ref|/abserr_07 | spread/tol | verdict | notes |",
+                            "|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|"]
+    for x in allc:
+        L.append(f"| {x['sid']} | {x['unit']} | {x['rtol']} | {x['basis']} | {x.get('ratio_g', '-'):.4g} | {x.get('ratio_06m', '-'):.4g} | "
+                 f"{x.get('spread_over_tol') if x.get('spread_over_tol') is None else format(x['spread_over_tol'], '.3g')} | {x['verdict']} | "
+                 f"{'; '.join(x.get('notes') or [])} |" if "ratio_g" in x else
+                 f"| {x['sid']} | {x['unit']} | {x['rtol']} | {x['basis']} | - | - | {x.get('spread_over_tol')} | {x['verdict']} | "
+                 f"{'; '.join(x.get('notes') or [])} |")
+    L += ["", f"The before (07's census): honest on {res['before']['honest_06m']} of {res['before']['of']} scored calls.",  # prompt 07a
+          "", "Beside 07's own scores (07's v4_scores.json): value and abserr at h equal 07's (hex), 07's verdict and ratio:", ""]
+    L += [f"- {x['sid']}: value equal {x['value_equal_07']}, abserr equal {x['abserr_equal_07']}; 07: {x['score_07']['verdict']}, "
+          f"{x['score_07']['ratio_g']:.4g}" for x in allc]
+    out_md.write_text("\n".join(L) + "\n")
+    print("\n".join(L[len(hdr):]))
+    print(f"wrote {out_json} sha256 {sha256(out_json)}; {out_md} sha256 {sha256(out_md)}")
+    return 0
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    sys.exit(main())

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07a section 6, Gate W0's third and fourth clauses: the units, chosen by a rule fixed before
+any is run (this docstring; new in 07a). Read only, except the two units files it writes.
+
+- **The unit with a window** (clauses 3 and 4): z12's row at 1e-8, ``g-1e-8-1-2174`` (07's own V0 (c) unit, whose
+  ``G+q+r`` call #1 locates one root and integrates its window at 07; run at h as ``h-1e-8-1-2174``). Fixed by name. That it
+  has a windowed call at h is read from the clause runs' own records (compare_07a.py prints each unit's windowed calls).
+- **The unit with a route change** (clause 3): from Gate W0's K0 run at h (K0_H_DIR/units.jsonl), in the order of 06k's
+  K0 list (docs/levin-phase-floor/06l/out/k0/units.json), the first unit, other than the window unit, that has no
+  windowed call at h and has a call one of whose ``own`` solves (its span a region the driver made) changed its C2 route
+  (route_07a ``n_route_differs_own`` > 0). If none: the first such unit with an own solve whose LU choice changed
+  (``n_lu_differs_own`` > 0); if none, the first with any solve whose route or LU choice changed. If none at all, the
+  script exits 1 and writes nothing.
+
+Writes state/w0c3_units.json (the two units) and state/w0c4_units.json (the window unit), in run_k0.py's form
+({"units": [{"uid": "g-..."}]}, 07's census names), each with this rule's basis; prints the choice.
+
+Usage: python -B select_w0_units_07a.py K0_H_DIR
+"""
+
+import json
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+from prov import H_TREE, WIDER, header, sha256  # noqa: E402
+
+K0_UNITS = H_TREE / "docs" / "levin-phase-floor" / "06l" / "out" / "k0" / "units.json"
+WINDOW_UNIT = "g-1e-8-1-2174"
+
+
+def main():
+    k0_dir = Path(sys.argv[1])
+    src = k0_dir / "units.jsonl"
+    hdr = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), "levin-phase-floor 07a: Gate W0's clause 3 and 4 units", [K0_UNITS, src])
+    for l in hdr:
+        print(l)
+    order = [u["uid"] for u in json.loads(K0_UNITS.read_text())["units"]]
+    recs = {}
+    for line in open(src):
+        r = json.loads(line)
+        recs["g-" + r["unit"].split("-", 1)[1]] = r["data"]
+    tiers = {1: None, 2: None, 3: None}
+    for uid in order:
+        if uid == WINDOW_UNIT or uid not in recs:
+            continue
+        d = recs[uid]
+        cs = (d.get("calls") or []) + (d.get("other_calls") or [])
+        if any((c.get("guard_07") or {}).get("n_windows") for c in cs):
+            continue
+        rs = [c.get("route_07a") for c in cs if isinstance(c.get("route_07a"), dict) and "error" not in c["route_07a"]]
+        if tiers[1] is None and any(r_["n_route_differs_own"] for r_ in rs):
+            tiers[1] = uid
+        if tiers[2] is None and any(r_["n_lu_differs_own"] for r_ in rs):
+            tiers[2] = uid
+        if tiers[3] is None and any(r_["n_route_differs"] or r_["n_lu_differs"] for r_ in rs):
+            tiers[3] = uid
+    tier = next((t for t in (1, 2, 3) if tiers[t] is not None), None)
+    print(f"candidates by tier (1: an own solve's C2 route; 2: an own solve's LU choice; 3: any solve): {tiers}")
+    if tier is None:
+        print("no K0 unit at h without a window has a route or LU change: nothing written")
+        return 1
+    route_unit = tiers[tier]
+    d = recs[route_unit]
+    detail = [(c["index"], c["notify_label"], {k: c["route_07a"][k] for k in ("n_route_differs_own", "n_lu_differs_own",
+                                                                              "n_route_differs_aux", "n_lu_differs_aux")})
+              for c in (d.get("calls") or []) + (d.get("other_calls") or [])
+              if isinstance(c.get("route_07a"), dict) and (c["route_07a"].get("n_route_differs") or c["route_07a"].get("n_lu_differs"))]
+    basis = {"rule": __doc__.split("Writes ")[0].strip(), "header": hdr, "window_unit": WINDOW_UNIT,
+             "route_unit": route_unit, "route_unit_tier": tier, "route_unit_calls_changed": detail}
+    p3 = WIDER / "state" / "w0c3_units.json"
+    p4 = WIDER / "state" / "w0c4_units.json"
+    for p in (p3, p4):
+        if p.exists():
+            raise SystemExit(f"{p} exists; not overwritten")
+    p3.write_text(json.dumps({"basis": basis, "units": [{"uid": WINDOW_UNIT}, {"uid": route_unit}]}, indent=1))
+    p4.write_text(json.dumps({"basis": basis, "units": [{"uid": WINDOW_UNIT}]}, indent=1))
+    print(f"window unit {WINDOW_UNIT}; route unit {route_unit} (tier {tier}); its changed calls {detail}")
+    print(f"wrote {p3} sha256 {sha256(p3)}")
+    print(f"wrote {p4} sha256 {sha256(p4)}")
+    return 0
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    sys.exit(main())

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07a section 6, stop 3 (new in 07a; read only): from compare_07a.py's calls output, the
+classes by rtol and kind; the untouched calls that differ, by the paths that differ and by their location record (skipped
+at 07, at h; points and windows at h); the calls of each class whose `call` category differs; and, for reading B's extra
+class (aux_only), how value, abserr and converged moved. Writes OUT_JSON and prints a summary. No verdict is scored.
+Usage: python -B stop3_classes_07a.py CALLS_JSONL OUT_JSON
+"""
+import json
+import sys
+from fractions import Fraction
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+from prov import header, sha256  # noqa: E402
+
+
+def fr(h):
+    return Fraction(float.fromhex(h))
+
+
+def main():
+    src, out_p = Path(sys.argv[1]), Path(sys.argv[2])
+    hdr = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), "levin-phase-floor 07a: stop 3, the comparison with 07's census by class", [src])
+    by = {}
+    unt_loc = {"n": 0, "paths": {}, "n_points_h_nonzero": 0, "n_windows_h_nonzero": 0, "skipped_07_kinds": {},
+               "skipped_h_kinds": {}, "examples": []}
+    call_cat = {}
+    aux = {"n": 0, "value_equal": 0, "abserr_equal": 0, "abserr_smaller_h": 0, "abserr_larger_h": 0, "conv_changes": [],
+           "abserr_ratio_h_over_07": [], "value_move_over_abserr07": [], "regions_equal": 0}
+    for line in open(src):
+        c = json.loads(line)
+        k = (c["rtol"], c["kind"], c["class"])
+        by[f"{k[0]}|{k[1]}|{k[2]}"] = by.get(f"{k[0]}|{k[1]}|{k[2]}", 0) + 1
+        if "call" in c["categories"]:
+            call_cat[c["class"]] = call_cat.get(c["class"], 0) + 1
+        if c["class"] == "untouched" and not c["equal"]:
+            unt_loc["n"] += 1
+            for p in sum(c["first_differing"].values(), []):
+                q = p.split("]", 1)[1] if "]" in p else p
+                unt_loc["paths"][q] = unt_loc["paths"].get(q, 0) + 1
+            unt_loc["n_points_h_nonzero"] += bool(c["n_points_h"])
+            unt_loc["n_windows_h_nonzero"] += bool(c["n_windows_h"])
+            s7 = json.dumps([sorted(e.keys()) for e in (c["skipped_07"] or [])])
+            sh = json.dumps([e.get("reason") for e in (c["skipped_h"] or [])])
+            unt_loc["skipped_07_kinds"][s7] = unt_loc["skipped_07_kinds"].get(s7, 0) + 1
+            unt_loc["skipped_h_kinds"][sh] = unt_loc["skipped_h_kinds"].get(sh, 0) + 1
+            if len(unt_loc["examples"]) < 5:
+                unt_loc["examples"].append({k_: c[k_] for k_ in ("unit", "kind", "index", "label", "first_differing", "skipped_07", "skipped_h")})
+        if c["class"] == "aux_only":
+            aux["n"] += 1
+            v, a, cv = c["value"], c["abserr"], c["converged"]
+            aux["value_equal"] += v[0] == v[1]
+            aux["regions_equal"] += c["num_regions"][0] == c["num_regions"][1]
+            ah, a7 = fr(a[0]), fr(a[1])
+            aux["abserr_equal"] += ah == a7
+            aux["abserr_smaller_h"] += ah < a7
+            aux["abserr_larger_h"] += ah > a7
+            if a7:
+                aux["abserr_ratio_h_over_07"].append(float(ah / a7))
+                aux["value_move_over_abserr07"].append(float(abs(fr(v[0]) - fr(v[1])) / a7))
+            if cv[0] != cv[1]:
+                aux["conv_changes"].append({"unit": c["unit"], "index": c["index"], "kind": c["kind"], "converged_h_07": cv})
+    for k_ in ("abserr_ratio_h_over_07", "value_move_over_abserr07"):
+        xs = sorted(aux[k_])
+        aux[k_] = {"n": len(xs), "min": xs[0] if xs else None, "p10": xs[len(xs) // 10] if xs else None,
+                   "median": xs[len(xs) // 2] if xs else None, "p90": xs[9 * len(xs) // 10] if xs else None,
+                   "max": xs[-1] if xs else None}
+    doc = {"header": hdr, "by_rtol_kind_class": dict(sorted(by.items())), "untouched_differing": unt_loc,
+           "calls_with_call_category_differing_by_class": call_cat, "aux_only": aux}
+    out_p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    out_p.write_text(json.dumps(doc, indent=1))
+    print(json.dumps({k_: v_ for k_, v_ in doc.items() if k_ != "header"}, indent=1)[:9000])
+    print(f"wrote {out_p} sha256 {sha256(out_p)}")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()
