+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07a section 6: W1, W2, W3, W5 and W7 scored, W4 and W6 gathered, and the descriptive tables.
+New in 07a, after 07's summarise_07.py (a record, not edited), whose V2 and V3 rules it keeps. Read only.
+
+The rules are state/predictions.md's, under the user's rulings at STOP 2 (state/ruling_stop2.md: Q1 reading A, Q2 the
+call's outputs, with the ``location`` and ``harness`` categories set aside as phase_span is). Each W is also given under
+reading B and strictly, for the record. Inputs: state/score_07a_units.json (score_07a.py), out/stop3/
+compare_census_calls.json and its .calls.jsonl (compare_07a.py), out/scores/w4_scores.json (score_v4_07a.py), and the
+cross-checks out/xcheck/h_vs_07_pop07/xcheck.json, out/xcheck/h_vs_07_poph/xcheck.json and
+out/xcheck/h_vs_06m_pop07/disagreement.json (xcheck_07a.py, xcheck_06m_from07_07a.py). Floats compared exactly
+(Fraction of their hex).
+
+Usage: python -B summarise_07a.py OUT_JSON
+"""
+
+import json
+import math
+import sys
+from collections import Counter, defaultdict
+from fractions import Fraction
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+from prov import WIDER, header, sha256  # noqa: E402
+
+UNITS = WIDER / "state" / "score_07a_units.json"
+CMP = WIDER / "out" / "stop3" / "compare_census_calls.json"
+CALLS = WIDER / "out" / "stop3" / "compare_census_calls.calls.jsonl"
+W4 = WIDER / "out" / "scores" / "w4_scores.json"
+X07 = WIDER / "out" / "xcheck" / "h_vs_07_pop07" / "xcheck.json"
+XH = WIDER / "out" / "xcheck" / "h_vs_07_poph" / "xcheck.json"
+X06 = WIDER / "out" / "xcheck" / "h_vs_06m_pop07" / "disagreement.json"
+SIX = 6.0 * math.pi
+LU = 20.0 * math.pi
+
+
+def F(h):
+    return Fraction(float.fromhex(h))
+
+
+def H(x):
+    return None if x is None else float.fromhex(x["hex"] if isinstance(x, dict) else x)
+
+
+def q(xs):
+    xs = sorted(x for x in xs if x is not None)
+    if not xs:
+        return None
+    return {"n": len(xs), "min": xs[0], "p10": xs[len(xs) // 10], "median": xs[len(xs) // 2], "p90": xs[9 * len(xs) // 10],
+            "p99": xs[min(len(xs) - 1, 99 * len(xs) // 100)], "max": xs[-1]}
+
+
+def ulp(x):
+    return math.ulp(x) if x is not None else None
+
+
+def main():
+    out_p = Path(sys.argv[1])
+    hdr = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), "levin-phase-floor 07a: W1-W7 and the descriptive tables",
+                 [UNITS, CMP, CALLS, W4, X07, XH, X06])
+    U = json.loads(UNITS.read_text())["units"]
+    cmpd = json.loads(CMP.read_text())
+    S = {"header": hdr, "statuses": dict(Counter(u["status"] for u in U)), "statuses_07": dict(Counter(u["status_07"] for u in U)),
+         "scan_07_errors": sum(u.get("scan_07_error") or 0 for u in U),
+         "n_scanned_calls": {rt: {k: sum(u["n_scanned_by_kind"][k] for u in U if u["rtol"] == rt) for k in ("group", "analytic")}
+                             for rt in ("1e-7", "1e-8")},
+         "route_errors": sum(u["agg"]["route_errors"] for u in U), "no_build_solves": sum(u["agg"]["no_build"] for u in U)}
+    unit_info = {u["unit"]: u for u in U}
+    calls = [json.loads(l) for l in open(CALLS)]
+    # ---------------- W1
+    w1 = {}
+    for reading, cls in (("A", {"untouched"}), ("B", {"untouched", "aux_only"})):
+        for rt in ("1e-7", "1e-8"):
+            cs = [c for c in calls if c["rtol"] == rt and c["class"] in cls]
+            w1[f"{reading}|{rt}"] = {"calls": len(cs), "strict_equal": sum(1 for c in cs if c["equal"]),
+                                     "call_category_differing": sum(1 for c in cs if "call" in c["categories"]),
+                                     "location_only": sum(1 for c in cs if c["categories"] == ["location"]),
+                                     "harness_only": sum(1 for c in cs if c["categories"] == ["harness"]),
+                                     "harness_and_location_only": sum(1 for c in cs if c["categories"] == ["harness", "location"]),
+                                     "call_differing_list": [{k: c[k] for k in ("unit", "kind", "index", "class", "first_differing")}
+                                                             for c in cs if "call" in c["categories"]][:50]}
+            us = [u for u in cmpd["units"] if u["rtol"] == rt and (u.get("all_w1") or {}).get(reading)]
+            w1[f"{reading}|{rt}"].update(rows_all_w1=len(us), rows_totals_equal=sum(1 for u in us if u["totals_equal"]),
+                                         rows_whole_equal=sum(1 for u in us if (u.get("whole") or {}).get("equal")),
+                                         rows_whole_categories=dict(Counter("+".join((u.get("whole") or {}).get("categories") or []) or "none" for u in us)),
+                                         rows_totals_differing=[u["unit"] for u in us if not u["totals_equal"]][:50])
+    S["W1"] = {"by_reading_rtol": w1,
+               "holds_A_ruled": all(w1[f"A|{rt}"]["call_category_differing"] == 0 and w1[f"A|{rt}"]["rows_all_w1"] == w1[f"A|{rt}"]["rows_totals_equal"]
+                                    for rt in ("1e-7", "1e-8")),
+               "holds_A_strict": all(w1[f"A|{rt}"]["calls"] == w1[f"A|{rt}"]["strict_equal"] for rt in ("1e-7", "1e-8")),
+               "holds_B_ruled": all(w1[f"B|{rt}"]["call_category_differing"] == 0 and w1[f"B|{rt}"]["rows_all_w1"] == w1[f"B|{rt}"]["rows_totals_equal"]
+                                    for rt in ("1e-7", "1e-8"))}
+    # ---------------- per-call walk of score_07a's units (W2, W3, W5, descriptive)
+    V2_levin, listed_other = [], Counter()
+    V3_viol, V3_set_aside, v3_scored = [], [], 0
+    w5 = Counter()
+    w5_calls = {"partition_nonforced": [], "partition_forced": [], "cc_lh": [], "guarded": []}
+    changed_solves = []
+    win = defaultdict(Counter)
+    stopped_list = []
+    loc_kinds, pieces_h, gt64 = Counter(), [], []
+    newly = []
+    unconv = defaultdict(Counter)
+    for u in U:
+        a = u["agg"]
+        w5["partition_nonforced"] += a["partition_nonforced"]
+        w5["partition_forced"] += a["partition_forced"]
+        w5["cc_with_levin_half"] += a["cc_lh"]
+        w5["cc_with_levin_half_07est"] += a["cc_lh_07est"]
+        w5["num_guarded_regions"] += a["guarded"]
+        w5["num_guard_at_depth_max"] += a["guard_at_depth_max"]
+        for c in u.get("calls") or []:
+            key = {"unit": u["unit"], "rtol": u["rtol"], "kind": c["kind"], "index": c["index"], "label": c["label"],
+                   "band": u["band"], "k": u["k"], "in720": u["in720"]}
+            r = c.get("route") or {}
+            if r.get("n_partition_nonforced") or (r.get("partition") or {}).get("nonforced") and sum((r.get("partition") or {}).get("nonforced", {}).values()):
+                w5_calls["partition_nonforced"].append(dict(key, partition=r.get("partition")))
+            if (r.get("partition") or {}).get("forced"):
+                w5_calls["partition_forced"].append(dict(key, partition=r.get("partition")))
+            if r.get("n_cc_with_levin_half"):
+                w5_calls["cc_lh"].append(dict(key, n=r["n_cc_with_levin_half"], detail=c.get("cc_with_levin_half")))
+            if c.get("num_guarded_regions"):
+                w5_calls["guarded"].append(dict(key, n=c["num_guarded_regions"]))
+            for x in c.get("listed") or []:
+                cls = ("Levin" if x["branch"] == "Levin" else "window" if x["window"] == 1 else
+                       "guard_at_cap" if x["guard_at_cap"] == 1 else "CC")
+                if cls == "Levin":
+                    V2_levin.append(dict(key, **x))
+                listed_other[(u["rtol"], c["kind"], cls)] += 1
+            if c.get("scanned") and "scan" in c:
+                sc = c["scan"]
+                if c["skipped"][0]:
+                    V3_set_aside.append(dict(key, skipped=c["skipped"][0], n_sign_changes=sc["n_sign_changes"],
+                                             min_over_max=sc.get("min_over_max_abs_theta_deriv"), locates=c.get("locates")))
+                else:
+                    v3_scored += 1
+                    h = H(sc["h"])
+                    S_x = sc["sign_change_x"]
+                    roots = [p["x"] for p in c.get("points") or [] if p["kind"] == "root"]
+                    miss_change = [x for x in S_x if not any(abs(rr - x) <= h for rr in roots)]
+                    miss_root = [rr for rr in roots if not any(abs(rr - x) <= h for x in S_x)]
+                    if miss_change or miss_root:
+                        V3_viol.append(dict(key, h=h, sign_change_x=S_x[:8], roots=roots[:8], changes_without_root=miss_change[:8],
+                                            roots_without_change=miss_root[:8], ends=[p["x"] for p in c.get("points") or [] if p["kind"] == "end"]))
+            for x in c.get("changed") or []:
+                V, est = H(x["V"]), H(x["est_07"])
+                if x["decision"] == 1:
+                    dirn = "to CC (V < 6pi <= est)" if V < SIX else "to Levin (est < 6pi <= V)"
+                else:
+                    dirn = "to LU (est <= 20pi < V)" if V > LU else "to least squares (V <= 20pi < est)"
+                changed_solves.append(dict(key, cls=x["class"], role=x["role"], dirn=dirn, V=V, est=est,
+                                           ratio=(est / V) if V else None, returned=x["returned"]))
+            # windows (both codes) for the calls with a window at h or at 07
+            for w in c.get("windows") or []:
+                win[u["rtol"]]["windows_h"] += 1
+                if w["stopped"]:
+                    win[u["rtol"]]["stopped_h"] += 1
+                    if w["leaves_falling_at_stop"]:
+                        win[u["rtol"]]["stopped_falling_h"] += 1
+                    stopped_list.append(dict(key, iroff1=w["iroff1_count"], falling=w["leaves_falling_at_stop"],
+                                             converged=c["converged"]))
+            for w in c.get("windows_07") or []:
+                win[u["rtol"]]["windows_07_recorded"] += 1
+            if c.get("windows"):
+                st = [w for w in c["windows"] if w["stopped"]]
+                if st:
+                    win[u["rtol"]]["calls_with_stopped_h"] += 1
+                    win[u["rtol"]]["calls_with_stopped_h_unconverged"] += not c["converged"][0]
+            for p in c.get("points") or []:
+                loc_kinds[(u["rtol"], c["kind"], p["kind"])] += 1
+            if c.get("points") is not None and c["n_points"][0]:
+                pieces_h.append((c.get("num_window_pieces") or [None])[0])
+                if c["n_points"][0] > 64:
+                    gt64.append(dict(key, n_points=c["n_points"][0]))
+            if c["n_windows"][0] and not c["n_windows"][1]:
+                newly.append(dict(key, n_points=c["n_points"][0], kinds=dict(Counter(p["kind"] for p in c.get("points") or [])),
+                                  pieces=(c.get("num_window_pieces") or [None])[0], skipped_07=c["skipped"][1],
+                                  converged=c["converged"]))
+            for j, code in ((0, "h"), (1, "07")):
+                if c["converged"][j] is False:
+                    unconv[code][(u["rtol"], c["kind"], "720" if u["in720"] else "outside")] += 1
+    S["W2"] = {"n_levin_leaves_listed": len(V2_levin), "levin_leaves": V2_levin[:100],
+               "listed_by_rtol_kind_class": {"|".join(k): v for k, v in sorted(listed_other.items())}, "holds": len(V2_levin) == 0}
+    bad_skip = [x for x in V3_set_aside if any(s.get("reason") != "zero_rate" for s in x["skipped"]) or x["n_sign_changes"]]
+    S["W3"] = {"n_calls_scored": v3_scored, "n_violating": len(V3_viol), "violations": V3_viol[:100],
+               "n_set_aside": len(V3_set_aside),
+               "set_aside_by": dict(Counter(f"{x['rtol']}|{x['kind']}|{','.join(s.get('reason') or 'NO REASON' for s in x['skipped'])}|"
+                                            f"sign_changes={x['n_sign_changes']}|min_over_max={x['min_over_max']}" for x in V3_set_aside)),
+               "set_aside_not_zero_rate_or_with_sign_change": bad_skip[:50], "set_aside_list": V3_set_aside,
+               "holds": len(V3_viol) == 0 and not bad_skip}
+    S["W5"] = {"counts": dict(w5), "calls": {k: v[:50] for k, v in w5_calls.items()},
+               "n_calls": {k: len(v) for k, v in w5_calls.items()},
+               "holds": w5["cc_with_levin_half"] == 0 and w5["partition_nonforced"] == 0 and w5["partition_forced"] == 0
+               and w5["num_guarded_regions"] == 0,
+               "holds_gated_partition_only": w5["cc_with_levin_half"] == 0 and w5["partition_nonforced"] == 0 and w5["num_guarded_regions"] == 0}
+    # ---------------- W4, W6 (gathered)
+    w4 = json.loads(W4.read_text())
+    S["W4"] = {"V4": w4["V4"], "ratios": {x["sid"]: x["ratio_g"] for x in w4["calls"] + w4["z"]},
+               "three_figures": {x["sid"]: float(f"{x['ratio_g']:.3g}") for x in w4["calls"] + w4["z"] if x["verdict"] != "honest"},
+               "all_bit_identical_to_07": all(x["value_equal_07"] and x["abserr_equal_07"] for x in w4["calls"] + w4["z"])}
+    x07 = json.loads(X07.read_text())
+    S["W6"] = {"pairs": x07["A"]["pairs"], "r_g_le_1": x07["A"]["overall"]["r_g<=1"], "unpaired": len(x07["A"]["unpaired"]),
+               "r_g_gt_1": [{k: p[k] for k in ("row", "kind", "index", "group", "r_g", "r_m", "converged")} for p in x07["A"]["r_g_gt_1"]],
+               "dist_r_g": x07["A"]["dist"]["r_g"], "checkpoints_unchanged": x07["checkpoints_unchanged"]}
+    S["W6"]["holds"] = S["W6"]["r_g_le_1"] == S["W6"]["pairs"]
+    xh = json.loads(XH.read_text())
+    S["W6"]["h_population_descriptive"] = {"pairs": xh["A"]["pairs"], "r_g_le_1": xh["A"]["overall"]["r_g<=1"],
+                                          "r_g_gt_1": [(p["row"], p["kind"], p["index"], p["r_g"]) for p in xh["A"]["r_g_gt_1"]]}
+    # ---------------- W7
+    w7 = {}
+    for reading in ("A", "B"):
+        for rt in ("1e-7", "1e-8"):
+            cov, viol = [], []
+            for c in calls:
+                if c["rtol"] != rt or c["class"] != "window_both":
+                    continue
+                r = c["route"] or {}
+                if reading == "A" and (r.get("n_route_differs") or r.get("n_lu_differs")):
+                    continue
+                if reading == "B" and (r.get("n_route_differs_own") or r.get("n_lu_differs_own")):
+                    continue
+                newly_loc = bool(c["skipped_07"]) and not c["skipped_h"]
+                if newly_loc:
+                    continue
+                vh, v7, e7 = F(c["value"][0]), F(c["value"][1]), F(c["abserr"][1])
+                mv = abs(vh - v7)
+                ok = mv <= Fraction(1, 100000) * e7 and c["converged"][0] == c["converged"][1]
+                rec = {k: c[k] for k in ("unit", "kind", "index", "converged")} | {
+                    "move_over_abserr07": float(mv / e7) if e7 else (0.0 if mv == 0 else math.inf)}
+                cov.append(rec)
+                if not ok:
+                    viol.append(rec)
+            w7[f"{reading}|{rt}"] = {"calls": len(cov), "violating": len(viol), "violations": viol[:50],
+                                     "n_moved": sum(1 for x in cov if x["move_over_abserr07"] > 0),
+                                     "max_move_over_abserr07": max((x["move_over_abserr07"] for x in cov), default=None),
+                                     "converged_changes": sum(1 for x in cov if x["converged"][0] != x["converged"][1])}
+    S["W7"] = {"by_reading_rtol": w7, "holds_A_ruled": all(w7[f"A|{rt}"]["violating"] == 0 for rt in ("1e-7", "1e-8")),
+               "holds_B": all(w7[f"B|{rt}"]["violating"] == 0 for rt in ("1e-7", "1e-8"))}
+    # ---------------- descriptive: route changes
+    cs_by = Counter((x["rtol"], x["kind"], x["band"], x["k"], x["cls"], x["dirn"]) for x in changed_solves)
+    S["route_changes"] = {
+        "n_solves_changed": len(changed_solves),
+        "by_direction_class": dict(Counter(f"{x['dirn']}|{x['cls']}" for x in changed_solves)),
+        "by_rtol_kind_direction": dict(Counter(f"{x['rtol']}|{x['kind']}|{x['dirn']}" for x in changed_solves)),
+        "by_band_k": dict(Counter(f"{x['band']}|{x['k']}" for x in changed_solves)),
+        "by_all": {"|".join(map(str, k)): v for k, v in sorted(cs_by.items())},
+        "est_over_V_by_direction": {d: q([x["ratio"] for x in changed_solves if x["dirn"] == d]) for d in sorted({x["dirn"] for x in changed_solves})},
+        "V_by_direction": {d: q([x["V"] for x in changed_solves if x["dirn"] == d]) for d in sorted({x["dirn"] for x in changed_solves})},
+        "calls_by_class": dict(Counter(f"{c['rtol']}|{c['kind']}|{c['class']}" for c in calls if c["class"] in ("own", "aux_only"))),
+        "n_solves_route_lu_from_units": [sum(u["agg"]["n_route"] for u in U), sum(u["agg"]["n_lu"] for u in U)],
+        "n_solves_total": sum(u.get("n_solves") or 0 for u in U)}
+    # ---------------- descriptive: moves of totals
+    mv = {"rows_total_differs": 0, "rel": [], "over_tae07": [], "by_band_k": Counter(), "converged_changes": []}
+    for u in U:
+        t, ta, tc = u.get("total"), u.get("total_abserr"), u.get("total_converged")
+        if not t or t[0] is None:
+            continue
+        if t[0] != t[1] or ta[0] != ta[1]:
+            mv["rows_total_differs"] += 1
+            d = abs(F(t[0]) - F(t[1]))
+            mv["rel"].append(float(d / abs(F(t[1]))) if F(t[1]) else None)
+            mv["over_tae07"].append(float(d / F(ta[1])) if F(ta[1]) else None)
+            mv["by_band_k"][f"{u['rtol']}|{u['band']}|{u['k']}"] += 1
+        if tc[0] != tc[1]:
+            mv["converged_changes"].append({"unit": u["unit"], "h_07": tc})
+    S["moves"] = {"rows_total_or_abserr_differs": mv["rows_total_differs"], "rel": q(mv["rel"]), "over_total_abserr_07": q(mv["over_tae07"]),
+                  "n_over_total_abserr_07_gt_1": sum(1 for x in mv["over_tae07"] if x and x > 1),
+                  "by_rtol_band_k": dict(mv["by_band_k"]), "total_converged_changes": mv["converged_changes"]}
+    cm = [c for c in calls if c["value"][0] != c["value"][1] or c["abserr"][0] != c["abserr"][1]]
+    S["moves"]["calls_value_or_abserr_differs_by_class"] = dict(Counter(c["class"] for c in cm))
+    S["moves"]["calls_value_move_over_abserr07_by_class"] = {
+        k: q([float(abs(F(c["value"][0]) - F(c["value"][1])) / F(c["abserr"][1])) for c in cm if c["class"] == k and F(c["abserr"][1])])
+        for k in sorted({c["class"] for c in cm})}
+    S["moves"]["calls_converged_changes"] = [{k: c[k] for k in ("unit", "kind", "index", "class", "converged", "abserr")}
+                                             for c in calls if c["converged"][0] != c["converged"][1]]
+    # ---------------- descriptive: newly located, windows, convergence, depth
+    S["newly_located"] = {"n_calls": len(newly), "by": dict(Counter(f"{x['rtol']}|{x['kind']}|{x['label'] if x['kind'] == 'analytic' else 'group'}" for x in newly)),
+                          "points_kinds": dict(Counter(k for x in newly for k in x["kinds"])), "pieces": q([x["pieces"] for x in newly]),
+                          "converged_h_07": dict(Counter(str(x["converged"]) for x in newly)),
+                          "skipped_07_variation": q([H(s["variation"]) for x in newly for s in x["skipped_07"] if "variation" in s]),
+                          "calls_over_64_points": gt64, "list": newly[:30]}
+    S["locations"] = {"by_rtol_kind_point": {"|".join(k): v for k, v in sorted(loc_kinds.items())}, "window_pieces_per_call": q(pieces_h)}
+    S["windows"] = {rt: dict(v) for rt, v in win.items()}
+    S["windows"]["07_recorded"] = {"1e-7": {"stopped": 1157, "falling": 1153}, "1e-8": {"stopped": 1195, "falling": 1192}}
+    S["windows"]["stopped_falling_hist"] = dict(Counter(x["falling"] for x in stopped_list))
+    S["unconverged"] = {code: {"|".join(k): v for k, v in sorted(c.items())} for code, c in unconv.items()}
+    S["row_depth"] = {rt: {"h": dict(Counter(u["row_depth"][0] for u in U if u["rtol"] == rt)),
+                           "07": dict(Counter(u["row_depth"][1] for u in U if u["rtol"] == rt))} for rt in ("1e-7", "1e-8")}
+    S["row_depth_20"] = {rt: [sum(1 for u in U if u["rtol"] == rt and u["row_depth"][j] == 20) for j in (0, 1)] for rt in ("1e-7", "1e-8")}
+    # ---------------- descriptive: cost
+    S["cost"] = {"unit_wall_s": {rt: [sum((u["wall_s"][j] or 0) for u in U if u["rtol"] == rt) for j in (0, 1)] for rt in ("1e-7", "1e-8")},
+                 "unit_time_s": {rt: [sum(u["time_s"][j] for u in U if u["rtol"] == rt) for j in (0, 1)] for rt in ("1e-7", "1e-8")},
+                 "group_regions": {rt: [sum(u["group_regions"][j] for u in U if u["rtol"] == rt) for j in (0, 1)] for rt in ("1e-7", "1e-8")},
+                 "all_regions": {rt: [sum(u["all_regions"][j] for u in U if u["rtol"] == rt) for j in (0, 1)] for rt in ("1e-7", "1e-8")},
+                 "census_wall_s": {"h": 23284.0, "07": 22708.0}}
+    # ---------------- descriptive: section 10.4's item
+    item104 = []
+    cmap = {(u["unit"], c["kind"], c["index"]): c for u in U for c in u.get("calls") or []}
+    for c in calls:
+        if c["converged"][1] is True and c["converged"][0] is False and not c["win07"] and not c["winh"] and c["class"] in ("own", "aux_only"):
+            sc = cmap.get((c["unit"], c["kind"], c["index"])) or {}
+            item104.append({k: c[k] for k in ("unit", "kind", "index", "label", "class")} | {
+                "abserr_h_07": c["abserr"], "abserr_h_07_repr": [repr(H(c["abserr"][0])), repr(H(c["abserr"][1]))],
+                "regions_h_07": c["num_regions"],
+                "changed": [{k: x.get(k) for k in ("a", "b", "role", "class", "V", "est_07", "decision")} for x in sc.get("changed") or []][:8]})
+    S["item_10_4"] = {"n": len(item104), "list": item104}
+    # ---------------- descriptive: |theta| at a gated end, margins
+    thm = {}
+    for u in U:
+        for kind, v in u["agg"]["theta_max"].items():
+            k = f"{u['rtol']}|{kind}"
+            if k not in thm or v[0] > thm[k]["theta"]:
+                thm[k] = {"theta": v[0], "ulp": ulp(v[0]), "unit": u["unit"], "index": v[1], "label": v[2]}
+    S["theta_end_max"] = thm
+    marg = {}
+    for nm in ("m6", "m20"):
+        for u in U:
+            m = u["agg"][nm]
+            if m is None:
+                continue
+            k = f"{nm}|{u['rtol']}|{m[1]}"
+            if k not in marg or m[0] < marg[k]["margin"]:
+                marg[k] = {"margin": m[0], "unit": u["unit"], "index": m[2], "detail": m[3]}
+    S["margins"] = marg
+    # ---------------- descriptive: the aux_only calls (the orchestrator's request)
+    aux = [c for c in calls if c["class"] == "aux_only"]
+    ratio = [float(F(c["abserr"][0]) / F(c["abserr"][1])) for c in aux if F(c["abserr"][1])]
+    vmove = [float(abs(F(c["value"][0]) - F(c["value"][1])) / F(c["abserr"][1])) for c in aux if F(c["abserr"][1])]
+    w4_by = {}
+    for x in w4["calls"] + w4["z"]:
+        w4_by[("h-" + x["unit"].split("-", 1)[1], x.get("kind", "group"), x["index"])] = x
+    xp = {}
+    for p in x07["pairs"]:
+        for rt in ("1e-7", "1e-8"):
+            xp[(f"h-{rt}-{p['row']}", p["kind"], p["index"])] = p
+    ref_rows = []
+    for c in aux:
+        k = (c["unit"], c["kind"], c["index"])
+        if k in w4_by:
+            x = w4_by[k]
+            ref_rows.append({"call": x["sid"], "unit": c["unit"], "ratio_h": x["ratio_g"], "ratio_07": x["score_07"]["ratio_g"],
+                             "abserr_h_07": c["abserr"]})
+        if k in xp:
+            p = xp[k]
+            ref_rows.append({"call": f"xcheck {p['row']} {p['kind']} {p['index']}", "unit": c["unit"], "r_g_h": p["r_g"],
+                             "r_g_07": p["r_m"], "abserr_h_07": c["abserr"]})
+    S["aux_only"] = {
+        "n": len(aux), "by_rtol_kind": dict(Counter(f"{c['rtol']}|{c['kind']}" for c in aux)),
+        "abserr_equal": sum(1 for c in aux if c["abserr"][0] == c["abserr"][1]),
+        "abserr_smaller_h": sum(1 for x in ratio if x < 1), "abserr_larger_h": sum(1 for x in ratio if x > 1),
+        "abserr_shrank_more_than_2x": sum(1 for x in ratio if x < 0.5),
+        "abserr_shrank_more_than_2x_list": [{k: c[k] for k in ("unit", "kind", "index", "label", "abserr", "value", "converged")}
+                                            for c in aux if F(c["abserr"][1]) and F(c["abserr"][0]) / F(c["abserr"][1]) < Fraction(1, 2)],
+        "abserr_grew_more_than_2x": sum(1 for x in ratio if x > 2),
+        "abserr_ratio_h_over_07": q(ratio),
+        "abserr_ratio_bins": dict(Counter("<0.5" if x < 0.5 else "[0.5,1)" if x < 1 else "1" if x == 1 else "(1,2]" if x <= 2 else ">2" for x in ratio)),
+        "value_equal": sum(1 for c in aux if c["value"][0] == c["value"][1]),
+        "value_move_over_abserr07": q(vmove), "value_move_over_abserr07_gt_1": [
+            {k: c[k] for k in ("unit", "kind", "index", "label", "value", "abserr")} for c in aux
+            if F(c["abserr"][1]) and abs(F(c["value"][0]) - F(c["value"][1])) > F(c["abserr"][1])],
+        "value_move_over_abserr_h_gt_1": sum(1 for c in aux if F(c["abserr"][0]) and abs(F(c["value"][0]) - F(c["value"][1])) > F(c["abserr"][0])),
+        "converged_changes": [{k: c[k] for k in ("unit", "kind", "index", "converged")} for c in aux if c["converged"][0] != c["converged"][1]],
+        "regions_changed": sum(1 for c in aux if c["num_regions"][0] != c["num_regions"][1]),
+        "referenced": ref_rows,
+        "differs_by_class": dict(Counter(k for c in aux for k, v in ((c["route"] or {}).get("differs_by_class") or {}).items() if v["route"] or v["lu"]))}
+    S["disagreement"] = {"h_vs_07_pop07": json.loads(X07.read_text())["B"]["by_class"] | {"n": x07["B"]["n_listed_calls"]},
+                         "h_vs_07_poph": xh["B"]["by_class"] | {"n": xh["B"]["n_listed_calls"]},
+                         "h_vs_06m_pop07": {k: v for k, v in json.loads(X06.read_text()).items() if k not in ("calls", "header")}}
+    S["post_unit_counterfactual"] = {"n_solves": sum((u.get("post_unit") or {}).get("n_solves") or 0 for u in U),
+                                     "n_route": sum((u.get("post_unit") or {}).get("n_route_differs") or 0 for u in U),
+                                     "n_lu": sum((u.get("post_unit") or {}).get("n_lu_differs") or 0 for u in U)}
+    out_p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    out_p.write_text(json.dumps(S, indent=1, default=str))
+    brief = {k: S[k] for k in ("statuses", "route_errors", "no_build_solves")}
+    brief["W1"] = {k: v for k, v in S["W1"].items() if k != "by_reading_rtol"} | {
+        k: {kk: vv for kk, vv in v.items() if kk not in ("call_differing_list", "rows_totals_differing")} for k, v in S["W1"]["by_reading_rtol"].items()}
+    brief["W2"] = {k: S["W2"][k] for k in ("n_levin_leaves_listed", "listed_by_rtol_kind_class", "holds")}
+    brief["W3"] = {k: S["W3"][k] for k in ("n_calls_scored", "n_violating", "n_set_aside", "set_aside_by", "holds")}
+    brief["W4"] = S["W4"]["V4"] | {"three_figures": S["W4"]["three_figures"], "bit_identical": S["W4"]["all_bit_identical_to_07"]}
+    brief["W5"] = {k: S["W5"][k] for k in ("counts", "n_calls", "holds", "holds_gated_partition_only")}
+    brief["W6"] = {k: S["W6"][k] for k in ("pairs", "r_g_le_1", "r_g_gt_1", "holds", "h_population_descriptive")}
+    brief["W7"] = S["W7"]
+    print(json.dumps(brief, indent=1, default=str))
+    print(f"wrote {out_p} sha256 {sha256(out_p)}")

+    main()

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07a section 6, Gate W0's first clause and the parent's install: the files of tree h that the
+K0 run's workers loaded (each worker's ``bye``: ``modules_own_tree``, and the module counted under the root ``variant``),
+against ``git diff --name-only dadd63a0 4bf50d34 -- . ':!docs' ':!prompts'`` (the parent against h) and against
+``git diff --name-only e182bc7a dadd63a0 -- . ':!docs' ':!prompts'`` (S0', 07's census tree against the parent). New in
+07a, after 07's v0a_imports_07.py (a record, not edited). Read only.
+
+The K0 run executes the parent's code if: no file h loaded differs from the parent's except the two LiouvilleGreen modules
+whose AST without docstrings is equal (out/w0/w0a_ast_lg.txt); h's own AdaptiveLevin/levin_quadrature.py is not among the
+files loaded; the variant loaded is the parent's file (its SHA-256 4e5da371...); and nothing the K0 workers loaded differs
+between 07's census tree e182bc7a and the parent (S0').
+
+Usage: python -B w0a_imports_07a.py RUN_K0_DIR OUT_TXT
+"""
+import json
+import subprocess
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+from prov import H_SHA, H_TREE, LQ_SHA256_07APARENT, PARENT_SHA_07A, PARENT_VARIANT_07A, header, sha256  # noqa: E402
+
+AST_EQUAL = {"LiouvilleGreen/bessel_phase.py", "LiouvilleGreen/three_bessel_integrals.py"}  # out/w0/w0a_ast_lg.txt
+
+
+def git_names(a, b):
+    return subprocess.run(["git", "--no-optional-locks", "-C", str(H_TREE), "diff", "--name-only", a, b, "--", ".",
+                           ":!docs", ":!prompts"], capture_output=True, text=True, check=True).stdout.split()
+
+
+def main():
+    run_dir, out_p = Path(sys.argv[1]), Path(sys.argv[2])
+    files = set()
+    variants = set()
+    n_workers = 0
+    for wdir in sorted(p for p in run_dir.iterdir() if p.is_dir()):
+        for line in open(wdir / "emit.jsonl"):
+            m = json.loads(line)
+            if m["kind"] == "bye":
+                n_workers += 1
+                for _name, f in m["modules_own_tree"]:
+                    files.add(str(Path(f).resolve().relative_to(H_TREE)))
+            if m["kind"] == "hello":
+                v = m["evidence"].get("variant_06k") or {}
+                variants.add((v.get("path"), v.get("sha256_now")))
+    diff_ph = git_names(PARENT_SHA_07A, H_SHA)
+    diff_s0 = git_names("e182bc7a827142cd4f42594f1a9d8cf85ccc3a5f", PARENT_SHA_07A)
+    loaded = files | {"main.py"}  # main.py is read by the harness (test_main_plumbing reads it with ast), not imported
+    inter_ph = sorted(set(diff_ph) & loaded)
+    inter_s0 = sorted(set(diff_s0) & loaded)
+    lq_loaded = "AdaptiveLevin/levin_quadrature.py" in files
+    variant_ok = variants == {(str(PARENT_VARIANT_07A.resolve()), LQ_SHA256_07APARENT)}
+    ok = set(inter_ph) <= AST_EQUAL and not lq_loaded and variant_ok and not inter_s0
+    lines = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), "levin-phase-floor 07a: Gate W0 (1), the files of h the K0 workers loaded",
+                   [PARENT_VARIANT_07A])
+    lines.append(f"# workers with a bye: {n_workers}; distinct files of h loaded: {len(files)}")
+    lines.append(f"# variants installed (path, sha256 at hello): {sorted(variants)}; the parent's: {variant_ok}")
+    lines.append(f"# h's own AdaptiveLevin/levin_quadrature.py among the files loaded: {lq_loaded}")
+    lines.append(f"# git diff --name-only {PARENT_SHA_07A[:8]} {H_SHA[:8]} -- . ':!docs' ':!prompts': {diff_ph}")
+    lines.append(f"# files both changed (parent to h) and loaded: {inter_ph}; of these, AST-equal without docstrings "
+                 f"(out/w0/w0a_ast_lg.txt): {sorted(set(inter_ph) & AST_EQUAL)}; other: {sorted(set(inter_ph) - AST_EQUAL)}")
+    lines.append(f"# S0': git diff --name-only e182bc7a {PARENT_SHA_07A[:8]} -- . ':!docs' ':!prompts': {diff_s0}; "
+                 f"loaded among them: {inter_s0}")
+    lines.append(f"# verdict: {'HOLDS (the code the K0 workers executed is the parent code, and the parent is 07s)' if ok else 'FAILS'}")
+    lines += sorted(files)
+    out_p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    out_p.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
+    print("\n".join(l for l in lines if l.startswith("#")))
+    print(f"wrote {out_p} sha256 {sha256(out_p)}")
+    return 0 if ok else 1
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    sys.exit(main())

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07a section 6, W5 (new in 07a; read only): the forced solves whose V took the node-partition
+sum (a window piece, or a window leaf's halves and quarters, all forced to the nested pair, whose V routes nothing), by
+rtol, kind, the call's located points' kinds, and the solve's role (from the pickle's arrays); and the calls' scanned
+sign changes. Usage: python -B w5_forced_detail_07a.py RUN_DIR OUT_JSON
+"""
+import hashlib
+import json
+import sys
+from collections import Counter
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+from prov import WIDER, header, sha256  # noqa: E402
+
+UNITS = WIDER / "state" / "score_07a_units.json"
+
+
+def main():
+    import ray.cloudpickle as cp
+
+    run, out_p = Path(sys.argv[1]), Path(sys.argv[2])
+    hdr = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), "levin-phase-floor 07a: W5's forced solves on the node-partition sum", [UNITS])
+    U = json.loads(UNITS.read_text())["units"]
+    offs, off = {}, 0
+    with open(run / "checkpoint.jsonl", "rb") as f:
+        for line in f:
+            offs[line[10:60].decode().split('"', 1)[0]] = (off, len(line))
+            off += len(line)
+    by, roles, calls_by = Counter(), Counter(), Counter()
+    examples = []
+    for u in U:
+        cs = [c for c in u.get("calls") or [] if ((c.get("route") or {}).get("partition") or {}).get("forced")]
+        if not cs:
+            continue
+        with open(run / "checkpoint.jsonl", "rb") as f:
+            f.seek(offs[u["unit"]][0])
+            d = json.loads(f.read(offs[u["unit"]][1]))["data"]
+        with open(run / d["pickle"], "rb") as f:
+            f.seek(d["pickle_offset"])
+            blob = f.read(d["pickle_length"])
+        assert hashlib.sha256(blob).hexdigest() == d["pickle_record_sha256"]
+        rec = {e["index"]: e for e in cp.loads(blob)["route_07a"]}
+        for c in cs:
+            pk = "+".join(sorted(Counter(p["kind"] for p in c.get("points") or []))) or "none"
+            calls_by[f"{u['rtol']}|{c['kind']}|points={pk}"] += 1
+            r = rec[c["index"]]["record"]
+            fl, ro = r["flags"], r["role"]
+            for i in range(len(fl)):
+                if (fl[i] & 1) and (fl[i] & 8):
+                    roles[f"{r['roles'][ro[i]]}|made={bool(fl[i] & 2)}"] += 1
+                    by[f"{u['rtol']}|{c['kind']}|points={pk}"] += 1
+                    if len(examples) < 10:
+                        examples.append({"unit": u["unit"], "index": c["index"], "a": float(r["a"][i]), "b": float(r["b"][i]),
+                                         "V": float(r["V"][i]), "est_07": float(r["est_07"][i]), "role": r["roles"][ro[i]],
+                                         "n_sign_changes_scan": (c.get("scan") or {}).get("n_sign_changes")})
+    doc = {"header": hdr, "solves_by": dict(by), "solves_by_role": dict(roles), "calls_by": dict(calls_by),
+           "n_solves": sum(by.values()), "n_calls": sum(calls_by.values()), "examples": examples}
+    out_p.write_text(json.dumps(doc, indent=1))
+    print(json.dumps({k: v for k, v in doc.items() if k != "header"}, indent=1))
+    print(f"wrote {out_p} sha256 {sha256(out_p)}")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07a section 6 (new in 07a; read only): W7's violating calls (reading A, as ruled), in detail:
+each call's label, located points by kind, its windows at h and at 07 (iroff1 count, stopped, leaves falling at the
+stop, pieces), regions, depth, value and abserr at both, and |v_h - v_07| against abserr_07 and abserr_h; and the same
+counts over every W7-covered call that moved. Usage: python -B w7_detail_07a.py OUT_JSON
+"""
+import json
+import sys
+from collections import Counter
+from fractions import Fraction
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+from prov import WIDER, header, sha256  # noqa: E402
+
+UNITS = WIDER / "state" / "score_07a_units.json"
+SUMM = WIDER / "out" / "scores" / "summary.json"
+CALLS = WIDER / "out" / "stop3" / "compare_census_calls.calls.jsonl"
+
+
+def F(h):
+    return Fraction(float.fromhex(h))
+
+
+def main():
+    out_p = Path(sys.argv[1])
+    hdr = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), "levin-phase-floor 07a: W7's violating calls in detail", [UNITS, SUMM, CALLS])
+    U = {u["unit"]: u for u in json.loads(UNITS.read_text())["units"]}
+    S = json.loads(SUMM.read_text())
+    moved = []
+    for line in open(CALLS):
+        c = json.loads(line)
+        r = c["route"] or {}
+        if c["class"] != "window_both" or r.get("n_route_differs") or r.get("n_lu_differs") or (c["skipped_07"] and not c["skipped_h"]):
+            continue
+        if c["value"][0] == c["value"][1]:
+            continue
+        sc = next(x for x in U[c["unit"]]["calls"] if x["kind"] == c["kind"] and x["index"] == c["index"])
+        vh, v7, eh, e7 = F(c["value"][0]), F(c["value"][1]), F(c["abserr"][0]), F(c["abserr"][1])
+        moved.append({"unit": c["unit"], "rtol": c["rtol"], "kind": c["kind"], "index": c["index"], "label": c["label"],
+                      "band": U[c["unit"]]["band"], "k": U[c["unit"]]["k"],
+                      "move_over_abserr07": float(abs(vh - v7) / e7), "move_over_abserr_h": float(abs(vh - v7) / eh),
+                      "abserr_h_over_07": float(eh / e7), "converged": c["converged"], "regions": c["num_regions"],
+                      "max_depth": c["max_depth"], "points": dict(Counter(p["kind"] for p in sc.get("points") or [])),
+                      "windows_h": [[w["iroff1_count"], w["stopped"], w["leaves_falling_at_stop"], w["n_pieces"]] for w in sc.get("windows") or []],
+                      "windows_07": [[w["iroff1_count"], w["stopped"], w["leaves_falling_at_stop"], w["n_pieces"]] for w in sc.get("windows_07") or []],
+                      "n_windows_stopped_h_07": sc.get("n_windows_stopped")})
+    viol = [m for m in moved if m["move_over_abserr07"] > 1e-5]
+    doc = {"header": hdr, "n_moved": len(moved), "n_violating": len(viol),
+           "violating_by": dict(Counter(f"{m['rtol']}|{m['kind']}|{m['label'].split('group ')[-1] if 'group' in m['label'] else m['label']}|points={m['points']}" for m in viol)),
+           "violating_by_band_k": dict(Counter(f"{m['band']}|{m['k']}" for m in viol)),
+           "moved_by": dict(Counter(f"{m['rtol']}|{m['label'].split('group ')[-1] if 'group' in m['label'] else m['label']}|{m['move_over_abserr07'] > 1e-5}" for m in moved)),
+           "violating": viol, "moved": moved}
+    out_p.write_text(json.dumps(doc, indent=1, default=str))
+    print(json.dumps({k: v for k, v in doc.items() if k not in ("header", "moved", "violating")}, indent=1))
+    for m in viol:
+        print(m["unit"], m["index"], m["label"][-40:], f"{m['move_over_abserr07']:.3g} {m['move_over_abserr_h']:.3g} e_h/e_07 {m['abserr_h_over_07']:.4g}",
+              m["regions"], m["max_depth"], m["points"], "h", m["windows_h"][:3], "07", m["windows_07"][:3], m["n_windows_stopped_h_07"])
+    print(f"wrote {out_p} sha256 {sha256(out_p)}")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()

+levin-phase-floor prompt 07a section 6: 07's census worker (docs/levin-phase-floor/07/census/scripts/worker.py, a record,

+  the estimate < SIX_PI) or, both on the Levin route, its LU choice (> _LEVIN_DIRECT_SOLVE_PHASE_SPAN) differs; a forced
+  solve makes no such decision. Also: each non-forced Clenshaw-Curtis solve with a visited half that went to the Levin

     }
 
 
+# prompt 07a ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# section 6's diagnostics, route_07a (see the module docstring). Role codes from the caller's description, as
+# _adaptive_levin's _solve names its solves; class names for the W1/W7 readings.
+R07A_ROLES = ("Levin subregion", "stationary-point window piece", "guarded leaf at depth_max", "comparison half",
+              "fallback half", "trust-check quarter", "other")
+R07A_CLASSES = ("own", "quarter", "comparison_half", "fallback_half", "other")
+R07A_N_KEEP = 64  # prompt 07a: changed solves kept in detail per call (every solve is in the pickle's arrays)
+R07A_FLAG_BITS = ("forced", "made", "returned", "partition", "is_direct", "lu_used", "multi_build")
+
+
+def _r07a_role(desc):
+    """prompt 07a: a solve's role code from its caller's description."""
+    if desc == "Levin subregion":
+        return 0
+    if desc == "stationary-point window piece":
+        return 1
+    if desc == "guarded leaf at depth_max":
+        return 2
+    if desc in ("Levin left-comparison region", "Levin right-comparison region"):
+        return 3
+    if desc in ("left half of fallback region", "right half of fallback region"):
+        return 4
+    if desc.startswith("trust check:"):
+        return 5
+    return 6
+
+
+def _r07a_class(role, made):
+    """prompt 07a: own (the span is a region the driver made) or the auxiliary role."""
+    if made:
+        return "own"
+    return {5: "quarter", 3: "comparison_half", 4: "fallback_half"}.get(role, "other")
+
+
+def _r07a_estimate_07(theta_prime_Cheb, grid):
+    """prompt 07a: 07's estimate of the phase span, the parent's expression verbatim (dadd63a0's
+    _Basis_SinCos.build_Levin_data: float(np.mean(np.fabs(theta_prime_Cheb)) * np.fabs(grid[0] - grid[-1])))."""
+    return float(np.mean(np.fabs(theta_prime_Cheb)) * np.fabs(grid[0] - grid[-1]))
+
+
+def _r07a_decision(ent, six, lu):
+    """prompt 07a: 3 forced (no route decision); -1 no build recorded; 1 the C2 route differs at some build; 2 both on
+    the Levin route and the LU choice differs at some build; 0 the same at every build."""
+    if ent["forced"]:
+        return 3
+    if not ent["builds"]:
+        return -1
+    for est, V, _part, _th in ent["builds"]:
+        cc_h, cc_7 = V < six, est < six
+        if cc_h != cc_7:
+            return 1
+        if not cc_h and (V > lu) != (est > lu):
+            return 2
+    return 0
+
+
+def _r07a_key(a, b):
+    return (float(a).hex(), float(b).hex())
+
+
+def _r07a_solve_detail(ent, dec, made, returned, ret_region):
+    est, V, part, th = ent["builds"][0] if ent["builds"] else (float("nan"),) * 2 + (None, float("nan"))
+    role = _r07a_role(ent["desc"])
+    return {
+        "a": fx(ent["a"]), "b": fx(ent["b"]), "desc": ent["desc"], "role": R07A_ROLES[role],
+        "class": _r07a_class(role, made), "forced": ent["forced"], "made": made, "returned": returned,
+        "depth": None if ret_region is None else int(ret_region.depth),
+        "V": fx(V), "est_07": fx(est), "est_over_V": (est / V) if V else None, "partition": part,
+        "decision": dec, "is_direct": ent.get("is_direct"), "lu_used": ent.get("lu_used"),
+        "n_builds": len(ent["builds"]), "builds": [[fx(e_), fx(v_), p_, fx(t_)] for e_, v_, p_, t_ in ent["builds"]],
+        "theta_end_max": fx(th),
+    }
+
+
+def _r07a_classify(entries, made, regions, six, lu):
+    """prompt 07a: (the checkpoint's summary, the pickle's record) of one call's solves; see the module docstring."""
+    made_k = {_r07a_key(r.start, r.end) for r in (made or [])}
+    ret = {_r07a_key(r.start, r.end): r for r in regions}
+    n = len(entries)
+    A = np.full(n, np.nan)
+    B = np.full(n, np.nan)
+    Vs = np.full(n, np.nan)
+    Es = np.full(n, np.nan)
+    TH = np.full(n, np.nan)
+    role = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8)
+    dec = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8)
+    flags = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.uint8)
+    nb = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int16)
+    by_cls = {c_: {"route": 0, "lu": 0} for c_ in R07A_CLASSES}
+    n_role = [0] * len(R07A_ROLES)
+    part = {"nonforced": {c_: 0 for c_ in R07A_CLASSES}, "forced": 0}
+    changed = []
+    m6 = m20 = None
+    th_nf = th_f = None
+    idx_nf = {}
+    for i, e in enumerate(entries):
+        k = _r07a_key(e["a"], e["b"])
+        is_made = k in made_k
+        is_ret = k in ret
+        r_ = _r07a_role(e["desc"])
+        d_ = _r07a_decision(e, six, lu)
+        cls = _r07a_class(r_, is_made)
+        A[i], B[i] = e["a"], e["b"]
+        role[i] = r_
+        dec[i] = d_
+        nb[i] = len(e["builds"])
+        n_role[r_] += 1
+        any_part = any(b_[2] for b_ in e["builds"])
+        if e["builds"]:
+            Es[i], Vs[i] = e["builds"][0][0], e["builds"][0][1]
+            ths = [b_[3] for b_ in e["builds"] if b_[3] == b_[3]]
+            TH[i] = max(ths) if ths else np.nan
+        bits = (e["forced"], is_made, is_ret, any_part, bool(e.get("is_direct")), bool(e.get("lu_used")),
+                len(e["builds"]) > 1)
+        flags[i] = sum(1 << j for j, x in enumerate(bits) if x)
+        if d_ == 1:
+            by_cls[cls]["route"] += 1
+        elif d_ == 2:
+            by_cls[cls]["lu"] += 1
+        if any_part:
+            if e["forced"]:
+                part["forced"] += 1
+            else:
+                part["nonforced"][cls] += 1
+        if d_ in (1, 2) and len(changed) < R07A_N_KEEP:
+            changed.append(_r07a_solve_detail(e, d_, is_made, is_ret, ret.get(k)))
+        if TH[i] == TH[i]:
+            if e["forced"]:
+                th_f = TH[i] if th_f is None else max(th_f, TH[i])
+            else:
+                th_nf = TH[i] if th_nf is None else max(th_nf, TH[i])
+        if d_ == 0:
+            for est_, V_, _p, _t in e["builds"]:
+                g6 = abs(V_ - six)
+                if m6 is None or g6 < m6[0]:
+                    m6 = (g6, V_, est_, e["a"], e["b"], cls, r_)
+                if V_ >= six:
+                    g20 = abs(V_ - lu)
+                    if m20 is None or g20 < m20[0]:
+                        m20 = (g20, V_, est_, e["a"], e["b"], cls, r_)
+        if not e["forced"]:
+            idx_nf.setdefault(k, []).append(i)
+    # each non-forced Clenshaw-Curtis solve (is_direct) with a visited non-forced half that went to the Levin solve; and
+    # the same on 07's estimate from the same nodes (the half's first build's estimate >= SIX_PI, the parent's < SIX_PI)
+    cc_lh, cc_lh_07 = [], 0
+    for i, e in enumerate(entries):
+        if e["forced"] or not e["builds"]:
+            continue
+        a_, b_ = e["a"], e["b"]
+        c_ = (b_ + a_) / 2.0
+        halves = idx_nf.get(_r07a_key(a_, c_), []) + idx_nf.get(_r07a_key(c_, b_), [])
+        if bool(e.get("is_direct")):
+            for j in halves:
+                if entries[j]["builds"] and not bool(entries[j].get("is_direct")):
+                    cc_lh.append({"parent": [fx(a_), fx(b_)], "parent_V": fx(Vs[i]), "half": [fx(entries[j]["a"]), fx(entries[j]["b"])],
+                                  "half_V": fx(Vs[j]), "half_desc": entries[j]["desc"]})
+        if Es[i] < six:
+            for j in halves:
+                if entries[j]["builds"] and Es[j] >= six:
+                    cc_lh_07 += 1
+    n_route_own = by_cls["own"]["route"]
+    n_lu_own = by_cls["own"]["lu"]
+    n_route_aux = sum(by_cls[c_]["route"] for c_ in R07A_CLASSES if c_ != "own")
+    n_lu_aux = sum(by_cls[c_]["lu"] for c_ in R07A_CLASSES if c_ != "own")
+
+    def mrec(m):
+        if m is None:
+            return None
+        return {"margin": fx(m[0]), "V": fx(m[1]), "est_07": fx(m[2]), "a": fx(m[3]), "b": fx(m[4]), "class": m[5],
+                "role": R07A_ROLES[m[6]]}
+
+    summary = {
+        "n_solves": n,
+        "n_by_role": dict(zip(R07A_ROLES, n_role)),
+        "n_forced": int(sum(1 for e in entries if e["forced"])),
+        "n_no_build": int(np.sum(dec == -1)),
+        "differs_by_class": by_cls,
+        "n_route_differs": int(np.sum(dec == 1)),
+        "n_lu_differs": int(np.sum(dec == 2)),
+        "n_route_differs_own": n_route_own, "n_lu_differs_own": n_lu_own,
+        "n_route_differs_aux": n_route_aux, "n_lu_differs_aux": n_lu_aux,
+        "own_differs": bool(n_route_own or n_lu_own),
+        "aux_differs": bool(n_route_aux or n_lu_aux),
+        "partition": part,
+        "n_partition_nonforced": int(sum(part["nonforced"].values())),
+        "n_cc_with_levin_half": len(cc_lh),
+        "cc_with_levin_half": cc_lh[:16],
+        "n_cc_with_levin_half_07est": cc_lh_07,
+        "min_margin_6pi_same": mrec(m6),
+        "min_margin_20pi_same_levin": mrec(m20),
+        "theta_end_max_nonforced": fx(th_nf),
+        "theta_end_max_forced": fx(th_f),
+        "changed": changed,
+        "n_changed_kept": len(changed),
+    }
+    record = {"a": A, "b": B, "V": Vs, "est_07": Es, "theta_end_max": TH, "role": role, "decision": dec,
+              "flags": flags, "n_builds": nb, "flag_bits": R07A_FLAG_BITS, "roles": R07A_ROLES,
+              "changed_all": [_r07a_solve_detail(e, int(dec[i]), bool(flags[i] & 2), bool(flags[i] & 4),
+                                                 ret.get(_r07a_key(e["a"], e["b"])))
+                              for i, e in enumerate(entries) if dec[i] in (1, 2)],
+              "cc_with_levin_half": cc_lh}
+    return summary, record
+
+
+def _r07a_post_tally(entries, six, lu):
+    """prompt 07a: the harness's own re-solves of one section (after the timed section), tallied as the call's are."""
+    if entries is None:
+        return None
+    dec = [_r07a_decision(e, six, lu) for e in entries]
+    return {
+        "n_solves": len(entries),
+        "n_route_differs": sum(1 for d_ in dec if d_ == 1),
+        "n_lu_differs": sum(1 for d_ in dec if d_ == 2),
+        "n_partition": sum(1 for e in entries if any(b_[2] for b_ in e["builds"])),
+        "changed": [_r07a_solve_detail(e, d_, False, False, None) for e, d_ in zip(entries, dec) if d_ in (1, 2)][:16],
+    }
+

+                            if INSTR_07A else None,
                         }
                         import io as _io

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07a section 6: write state/predictions.md, after Gate W0 and before the census is launched.
+New in 07a. The prompt's text is copied from the prompt file in the tree h by line range, word for word, and checked to
+begin and end where it should; V4's 33 calls are copied from 07's records (docs/levin-phase-floor/07/census/out/v4/

+Usage: python -B write_predictions_07a.py OUT_MD
+"""
+
+import json
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

+

+OWN = r"""
+## 4. This agent's additions, descriptive and not scored (the dispatch brief)
+
+- **§10.4's item** (part 3), as the census lists it: every call that goes from converged (07) to unconverged (h) with a
+  solve whose route changed and no window at 07 or at h, with its V, 07's estimate, and the abserr under each code. "A
+  region whose route changed" is listed under both readings of part 5's question 1 (an own solve; any solve).

+  |theta(a)|, |theta(b)| over every non-forced solve's builds (section 6's diagnostics, ``theta_end_max_nonforced``),
+  with its ulp.

+  diagnostics' ``min_margin_6pi_same`` and ``min_margin_20pi_same_levin``, the latter among solves on the Levin route),
+  by kind and rtol, with their V and 07's estimate.
+
+## 5. How each rule is read (this agent's, fixed here before the census; the open questions are the user's)
+
+**The diagnostics** (worker.py, ``route_07a``; out/w0/ shows they change nothing). For every subregion solve the driver
+makes in a call (each call of ``_adaptive_levin_subregion``), they record its span, its role, whether it was forced to
+the nested Clenshaw-Curtis pair, and, for each build of its Levin data, V (h's), 07's estimate mean|theta'| * width from
+the same returned theta_prime_Cheb and grid by the parent's expression, and whether V took the node-partition sum. A
+solve's **decision differs** if at some build its C2 route differs (V < 6 pi against the estimate < 6 pi), or both
+routes are Levin and its LU choice differs (> 20 pi). A forced solve (a window piece, a window leaf's halves and
+quarters, a guarded leaf at depth_max) makes no route decision and is never counted as differing. Each solve is
+classed by **role**:
+- ``own``: its span is a region the driver made (a ``used_interval``: returned, or made and later bisected), so its
+  value and error became, at some point, a region's own;
+- otherwise by the description the driver gave it: ``quarter`` (a trust-check quarter: "trust check: ..."),
+  ``comparison_half`` (a Levin leaf's step-(4) halves, "Levin left/right-comparison region", data_L/data_R),
+  ``fallback_half`` (a fallback leaf's halves, solved when it is chosen), ``other``.
+The harness's own re-solves after the timed section (06b's W3, 06c's deep lines, 06e's floor terms, 06h's declared terms
+and its analytic counterfactual) are not the call's and are tallied apart (``route_07a_post``).
+
+**Question 1 (open; the user's): what W1's "region whose route or LU choice differs" and W7's counts.** R30's 49
+abserr-only records each had exactly one changed solve, never a region's own: a trust-check quarter or a comparison half.
+Such a solve's value enters the call's declared error (the trust check's delta1 and delta2, a Levin leaf's residual), not
+its value. Two readings, both scored:
+- **reading A:** every solve the driver makes counts (own and auxiliary): W1 covers only calls in which no solve of any
+  role changed;
+- **reading B:** only a call's own region solves count: W1 also covers calls whose only changes are auxiliary solves,
+  and predicts them bit-identical (R30 says they are not, in abserr).
+The same choice applies to W7's "no region whose route or LU choice differs". The census's comparison classes every call
+without a window as ``untouched`` (no solve changed), ``aux_only`` (only auxiliary solves changed) or ``own`` (an own
+solve changed), and reports W1 and W7 under each reading. This agent does not choose.
+
+**Question 2 (open; the user's): what W1's "equals 07's call bit for bit" compares.** Every call is diffed against 07's on
+everything 07's comparison takes as the call's, and each differing path is put in one category:
+- ``call``: the call's own outputs (value, abserr, converged, regions, leaves, its solve counts, its trust checks' and
+  premise checks' records, guard_07's windows and counts, its scans);
+- ``location``: guard_07's ``location_skipped_phases``, ``unlocated_minima`` and ``n_unlocated_minima``: the location's
+  own report. §1.1 item 1 changes it by construction for every call whose phase Rule A skipped at 07: 07's entry

+  can newly report an unlocated minimum even where it locates nothing and makes no window;

+  06e's deep lines, 06b's W3), which re-solve spans the driver may not have visited, under h's routing.

+choose. (``phase_span`` itself is not a key of any record of the harness at 07 or at h: compare_07a.py counts its
+occurrences in every unit compared, and sets it aside by showing it is absent.)
+
+**W1's rows.** A unit all of whose calls are of W1's classes (under the reading) is scored on ``result.total`` and
+``result.total_abserr``, hex, as written; its whole-unit diff is reported with its categories (the unit's 06h analytic
+counterfactual is ``harness``).
+
+**W2** is 07's V2 on the census's scan_07 (every group call at both rtols, every analytic call at 1e-7), as 07 scored it.
+
+**W3** is 07's V3 on the scanned calls, as 07 scored it, with 07 §10's set-aside replaced by 07a's: a phase is set aside
+only if its entry in ``location_skipped_phases`` says ``reason: zero_rate``; each such call is listed and its rate is
+checked to be exactly zero on the scan (scan_07: no sign change, and min |theta'| / max |theta'| of 0 or no finite
+non-zero sample). A skipped phase of any other kind fails W3.
+
+**W4** is scored by 07's rule (score_v4_07.py's) on 07's readings, with "the before" now 07's census and "the new" h's:
+the tolerance min(abserr_07, abserr_h)/100, the readings' agreement test unchanged in form, honest iff |v_h - ref| <=
+abserr_h. A call whose readings do not agree under the new tolerance is reported, not scored, and its verdict under 07's
+own tolerance is shown beside it. §9's amendment adopted no rule that leaves a call unconverged (§9.3), so W4's escape
+clause "or is unconverged under a rule of §9's amendment" admits no call. The prediction of §9.5 (fails on exactly v08,
+v09, v12, v17, v22 at 3.16, 2.21, 3.08, 2.38, 2.53 to three figures; holds on the other 28) is scored as written: to three
+significant figures.

+**W5:** the three counts are made on every solve of every call (forced included), each broken down by role, and the
+node-partition count is also given for the gated (non-forced) solves alone, which is how Part B's review counted it

+the Levin solve. The harness's own re-solves are not regions of the call and are not counted. ``num_guarded_regions`` is
+guard_07's, as 07 recorded it.
+
+**W6** is the cross-check's r_g at h, r_g = |v_h(1e-7) - v_h(1e-8)| / e_h(1e-7) (state/xcheck_rule_20261109.md's

+**W7** is scored on the calls with a window at 07 and at h (guard_07 ``n_windows`` > 0 in both), no differing decision

+
+**The over-budget pass:** a unit over budget in both passes is counted as not scored for W1 and W7 and listed.
+"""

+def main():

+    out = ["# levin-phase-floor 07a §6 — the census's predictions, fixed before the census", ""]
+    out += ["Provenance (the writer's header):", "", "```"] + md + ["```", ""]
+    out += ["Written after Gate W0 (all four clauses: out/w0/, out/k0/compare_w0b.json, out/w0c3/compare_instr.json, "
+            "out/w0c4/compare_det.json) and before the census is launched. Not to be rewritten: a correction is a new file "
+            "and a deviation.", "",
+            f"Source: `{PROMPT}` (file SHA-256 {sha256(PROMPT)}). Part 1 is lines {RANGES['s6'][0]}-{RANGES['s6'][1]} (§6, the "

+            f"additions: W4, W6 on r_g, W7); part 3 is lines {RANGES['s104'][0]}-{RANGES['s104'][1]} (§10.4's descriptive item). "
+            "Each is copied unchanged, word for word, by line range. Part 4 (descriptive additions) and part 5 (how each rule "
+            "is read, with the two questions left open for the user) are this agent's. Part 6 fixes V4's 33 calls.", ""]
+    out += ["## 1. Prompt §6, the predictions and the descriptive items (verbatim)", "", parts["s6"], ""]
+    out += ["## 2. Prompt §9.5, the additions to §6's predictions (verbatim)", "", parts["s95"], ""]
+    out += ["## 3. Prompt §10.4, the descriptive item (verbatim)", "", parts["s104"], ""]
+    out += OWN.strip("\n").splitlines() + [""]
+    out += ["## 6. V4's 33 calls, fixed by name (07's set, from 07's records; W4 is scored on 07's readings)", "",
+            f"From `{V4}` (SHA-256 {sha256(V4)}), its `calls` (the 24) and `z` (z00, z12 and Z3's seven). The unit is 07's "
+            "name (`g-...`); at h it is `h-...`. Each call is the unit's Levin call of that index (group: `calls`; analytic: "
+            "`other_calls`), matched at h by index, label and span as compare_07a.py matches calls.", "",
+            "| call | unit | rtol | kind | index | 07's basis | 07's ref (30 digits) | 07's |v-ref|/abserr | 07's verdict |",
+            "|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|"]

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07a section 6, the user's references (state/ruling_w5.md): 07's census x1_capture.py (a record,
+not edited), changed only where marked "prompt 07a": the tree is h (prov.TREES["h"]); a selection entry's unit is

+census's harness, unchanged since the census ran; ROWS_JSON is 06g's rows.json, which the census read too. 06g's text
+follows.
+
+levin-phase-floor prompt 06g section 5 (the references): copied from 06h's census
+scripts/x1_capture.py (docs/levin-phase-floor/06h/census/scripts/, a record, not edited; its copy is in
+state/scripts_06h_census_reference/), which is 06c's, changed only where marked "prompt 06g": ROWS_JSON is
+the census agent's state/rows.json (read only, its SHA-256 checked), each unit's budget is its g census
+record's ``budget_s`` (select_refs.py; 300 s in the main pass), and the header names 06g. It runs at g
+(prov.TREES["g"], the code worktree at 1c5336e) with the census agent's harness (worker.py, harness.py,
+rebuild.py, dispatch.py, spscan.py, prov.py), copied unchanged. 06c's text follows.
+
+levin-phase-floor prompt 06c section 5, X1: capture the inputs of the selected calls, after the
+run. New in 06c.
+
+X1's 24 calls are known only once g has run ("moved" compares g with 06b), and capturing every
+candidate's inputs during the run, as 06b's W2 did for its 975, would take about 1.35 MB per call
+over tens of thousands of candidates. So the run captures nothing, and this script re-runs, at g,
+each selected call's unit in a fresh census worker (worker.py, the producer cache, the same 300 s
+budget, the scan and W3 as in the run), with the unit's ``"capture"`` list naming the selected
+calls. The worker pickles each listed call's span, integrand ``f``, phase dict ``theta`` and
+keyword arguments as the call saw them, with its value, abserr, num_regions, max_depth and
+converged. The capture **counts only if** that record equals the census run's call bit for bit
+(value and abserr by hex; num_regions, max_depth, converged), which Gate X0's third clause (the new
+code is deterministic) leads one to expect; a call whose capture does not match is reported and
+its reference is not computed from it.
+
+Input SELECTION_JSON: a list of entries {"unit": "g-<rtol>-<shard>-<serial>", "index", "notify_label",
+"census_value", "census_abserr" (hex), "census_num_regions", "census_max_depth",
+"census_converged", ...}; any other keys are carried. One worker per unit, at most six at once.
+Writes OUT_DIR/<worker>/ (spec, emit, log, inputs pickle) and OUT_JSON: the entries, each with
+its capture's {"dir", "pickle", "offset", "length", "sha256"} and ``capture_matches_census``.
+
+Usage: PYTHONPATH=<tree g> python -B x1_capture.py SELECTION_JSON OUT_DIR OUT_JSON [--workers N]
+"""
+
+import argparse
+import json
+import os
+import pickle
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import time
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+from prov import PYTHON, TREES, WIDER, header, sha256  # noqa: E402
+from prov import LQ_SHA256_07APARENT, PARENT_VARIANT_07A  # noqa: E402  prompt 07a
+import refs_common as RC  # noqa: E402  prompt 06g
+
+BUDGET_S = 300.0
+ROWS_JSON = RC.ROWS  # prompt 06g: the census agent's rows.json, read only (06c: WIDER / "state" / "rows.json")
+
+
+def main():
+    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
+    ap.add_argument("selection")
+    ap.add_argument("out_dir")
+    ap.add_argument("out_json")
+    ap.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=6)
+    ap.add_argument("--variant", default=None, choices=("parent",))  # prompt 07a
+    args = ap.parse_args()
+    if args.workers > 6:
+        raise SystemExit("at most six workers")
+    # the captured records hold functions pickled by reference to g's modules, and are unpickled
+    # here to be matched to the census (out/cmd/11, the first development capture, stopped there)
+    if os.environ.get("PYTHONPATH") != str(TREES["h"][0]):  # prompt 07a
+        raise SystemExit("PYTHONPATH must be the tree h")
+    vspec = None  # prompt 07a
+    if args.variant == "parent":
+        if sha256(PARENT_VARIANT_07A) != LQ_SHA256_07APARENT:
+            raise SystemExit("the parent's module has another SHA-256")
+        vspec = {"name": "parent", "path": str(PARENT_VARIANT_07A.resolve()), "sha256": LQ_SHA256_07APARENT}
+    pre = "census07_" if vspec else "census_"  # prompt 07a: the census record the capture must match
+    for l in header(Path(__file__).resolve(), f"levin-phase-floor 07a: the user's reference calls' inputs, captured at h, variant {args.variant} (06c's X1 capture)", [Path(args.selection), ROWS_JSON]):  # prompt 06g; prompt 07: the title
+        print(l)
+    sel = json.loads(Path(args.selection).read_text())
+    if sha256(ROWS_JSON) != RC.ROWS_SHA256:  # prompt 06g
+        raise SystemExit(f"{ROWS_JSON}: sha256 is not {RC.ROWS_SHA256}")
+    rows = json.loads(ROWS_JSON.read_text())["rows"]
+    by_key = {f"{r['shard']}-{r['serial']}": r for r in rows}
+    by_unit = {}
+    for e in sel:
+        by_unit.setdefault(e["unit"], []).append(e)
+    out_dir = Path(args.out_dir)
+    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
+    pending = []
+    for i, (uid, ents) in enumerate(sorted(by_unit.items())):
+        parts = uid.split("-")  # "g-1e-7-<shard>-<serial>": the rtol holds a "-" (06b's deviation 20)
+        tree, shard, serial, rtol = parts[0], parts[-2], parts[-1], "-".join(parts[1:-2])
+        if tree != "h" or rtol not in ("1e-7", "1e-8"):  # prompt 07a
+            raise SystemExit(f"{uid}: not an h unit")
+        R = by_key[f"{shard}-{serial}"]
+        u = {
+            "uid": uid,
+            "tree": "h",  # prompt 07a
+            "rtol": rtol,
+            "row": R["name"],
+            "names05": R["names05"],
+            "shard": R["shard"],
+            "serial": R["serial"],
+            "k_key": R["k_key"],
+            "budget": ents[0].get("census_unit_budget_s", BUDGET_S),  # prompt 06g: the g census record's budget_s
+            "scan": rtol == "1e-7",
+            "w3": rtol == "1e-7",
+            "capture": [[e["index"], e["notify_label"]] for e in sorted(ents, key=lambda e: e["index"])],
+        }
+        name = f"C{i:02d}"
+        wdir = out_dir / name
+        wdir.mkdir()
+        spec = {
+            "tree": "h",  # prompt 07a
+            "name": name,
+            "units": [u],
+            "out_dir": str(wdir),
+            "emit_fd": None,
+            "emit_file": str(wdir / "emit.jsonl"),

+            "w2_capture_sha256": None,
+            "variant": vspec,  # prompt 07a
+        }
+        (wdir / "spec.json").write_text(json.dumps(spec, indent=1))
+        pending.append((name, wdir))
+    env = dict(os.environ)
+    env["PYTHONPATH"] = str(TREES["h"][0])  # prompt 07a
+    env["PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE"] = "1"
+    env["OMP_NUM_THREADS"] = "1"
+    running = {}
+    rc = {}
+    while pending or running:
+        while pending and len(running) < args.workers:
+            name, wdir = pending.pop(0)
+            log = open(wdir / "worker.log", "ab")
+            running[name] = subprocess.Popen(
+                [str(PYTHON), "-B", str(HERE / "worker.py"), str(wdir / "spec.json")],
+                cwd=str(WIDER), env=env, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
+            )
+            log.close()
+            print(f"{name}: pid {running[name].pid}", flush=True)
+        for name, p in list(running.items()):
+            if p.poll() is not None:
+                rc[name] = p.returncode
+                del running[name]
+        time.sleep(0.5)
+    print(f"exit codes {rc}")
+    # read back
+    import hashlib
+
+    out = []
+    for name in sorted(rc):
+        wdir = out_dir / name
+        units, bye = {}, None
+        for line in open(wdir / "emit.jsonl"):
+            m = json.loads(line)
+            if m["kind"] == "unit":
+                units[m["data"]["unit"]] = m["data"]
+            elif m["kind"] == "bye":
+                bye = m
+        for uid, d in units.items():
+            inp = {e["index"]: e for e in (d.get("x1_inputs") or [])}
+            for e in by_unit[uid]:
+                r = dict(e)
+                r["capture_worker"] = name
+                r["capture_unit_status"] = d["status"]
+                r["capture_ray_initialized"] = None if bye is None else bye["ray_initialized"]
+                ent = inp.get(e["index"])
+                if ent is None or ent.get("error"):
+                    r["capture_error"] = "not captured" if ent is None else ent["error"]
+                    r["capture_matches_census"] = False
+                    out.append(r)
+                    continue
+                with open(wdir / ent["pickle"], "rb") as f:
+                    f.seek(ent["offset"])
+                    blob = f.read(ent["length"])
+                if hashlib.sha256(blob).hexdigest() != ent["sha256"]:
+                    raise RuntimeError(f"{uid} {e['index']}: capture blob sha256 differs")
+                rec = pickle.loads(blob)
+                r.update({"dir": str(wdir), "pickle": ent["pickle"], "offset": ent["offset"], "length": ent["length"], "sha256": ent["sha256"]})
+                got = (rec["value"], rec["abserr"], rec["num_regions"], rec["max_depth"], rec["converged"], rec["notify_label"])
+                want = (e[pre + "value"], e[pre + "abserr"], e[pre + "num_regions"], e[pre + "max_depth"], e[pre + "converged"], e["notify_label"])  # prompt 07a: h's or 07's
+                r["capture_record"] = {"value": rec["value"], "abserr": rec["abserr"], "num_regions": rec["num_regions"],
+                                       "max_depth": rec["max_depth"], "converged": rec["converged"]}
+                r["capture_matches_census"] = got == want
+                r["capture_error"] = None
+                out.append(r)
+    Path(args.out_json).write_text(json.dumps(out, indent=1))
+    n_ok = sum(1 for r in out if r["capture_matches_census"])
+    print(f"captured and matching the census bit for bit: {n_ok} of {len(sel)}")
+    for r in out:
+        print(f"   {r['unit']} {r['index']}: matches {r['capture_matches_census']}; error {r.get('capture_error')}; ray_initialized {r['capture_ray_initialized']}")
+    print(f"wrote {args.out_json} sha256 {sha256(args.out_json)}")
+    return 0 if n_ok == len(sel) and len(out) == len(sel) else 1
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    sys.exit(main())

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07a section 6 (descriptive): the disagreement list re-made at h against 06m. New in 07a.
+06m's census run directory was trimmed on 2026-11-10 (its TRIMMED_2026-11-10.txt; the checkpoint is gone), so 06m's
+values and abserrs are taken from 07's cross-check output (var/levin-phase-floor-07/census/out/xcheck/xcheck.json, its
+``pairs``: m7, m8 per pair, SHA-256 checked against 07's KEPT list), and h's from xcheck_07a.py's h-against-07 run on 07's
+population (out/xcheck/h_vs_07_pop07/xcheck.json, its ``g7``, ``g8``). The classes are 07's rule
+(state/xcheck_rule_20261109.md) with g read as h. Exact, by Fraction. Read only.
+Usage: python -B xcheck_06m_from07_07a.py OUT_JSON
+"""
+import json
+import sys
+from collections import Counter
+from fractions import Fraction
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+from prov import REC07, WIDER, header, sha256  # noqa: E402
+
+X07 = REC07 / "out" / "xcheck" / "xcheck.json"
+X07_SHA256 = "533b2b0789b541aa0e19aa099340c17d90e793866edeecbe86bf924fa5543dab"  # 07's KEPT_ON_MAC_STUDIO.txt
+XH = WIDER / "out" / "xcheck" / "h_vs_07_pop07" / "xcheck.json"
+
+
+def F(h):
+    return Fraction(float.fromhex(h))
+
+
+def main():
+    out_p = Path(sys.argv[1])
+    hdr = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), "levin-phase-floor 07a: the disagreement list at h against 06m (06m from 07's xcheck)", [X07, XH])
+    if sha256(X07) != X07_SHA256:
+        raise SystemExit("07's xcheck.json has another SHA-256")
+    p07 = {(p["row"], p["kind"], p["index"]): p for p in json.loads(X07.read_text())["pairs"]}
+    ph = {(p["row"], p["kind"], p["index"]): p for p in json.loads(XH.read_text())["pairs"]}
+    if set(p07) != set(ph):
+        raise SystemExit(f"pairs differ: {len(p07)} / {len(ph)}")
+    listed = []
+    for k in sorted(p07):
+        a, b = ph[k], p07[k]
+        h7, h8, eh7, eh8 = F(a["values"]["g7"]), F(a["values"]["g8"]), F(a["abserrs"]["g7"]), F(a["abserrs"]["g8"])
+        m7, m8, em7, em8 = F(b["values"]["m7"]), F(b["values"]["m8"]), F(b["abserrs"]["m7"]), F(b["abserrs"]["m8"])
+        g_self, m_self = abs(h7 - h8) <= eh7, abs(m7 - m8) <= em7
+        agree8 = abs(h8 - m8) <= max(eh8, em8)
+        cls = ("a" if g_self and not m_self and agree8 else "b" if m_self and not g_self and agree8 else
+               "c" if g_self and m_self else "d" if not g_self and not m_self else "e")
+        for rt, g, m, eg, em in (("1e-7", h7, m7, eh7, em7), ("1e-8", h8, m8, eh8, em8)):
+            if abs(g - m) > max(eg, em):
+                listed.append({"row": k[0], "kind": k[1], "index": k[2], "rtol": rt, "class": cls,
+                               "class_07": b["class"] if b["listed"][rt] else None, "group": a["group"]})
+    doc = {"header": hdr, "n_pairs": len(p07), "n_listed_calls": len(listed), "by_class": dict(Counter(x["class"] for x in listed)),
+           "by_class_rtol": {f"{c}|{r}": n for (c, r), n in sorted(Counter((x["class"], x["rtol"]) for x in listed).items())},
+           "by_group": dict(Counter(x["group"] for x in listed)),
+           "listed_at_07_too": sum(1 for x in listed if x["class_07"] is not None),
+           "class_h_vs_07": {f"{x['class_07']}->{x['class']}": n for x_ in [None] for x, n in []},
+           "calls": listed}
+    doc["class_07_to_h"] = dict(Counter(f"{x['class_07']}->{x['class']}" for x in listed))
+    out_p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    out_p.write_text(json.dumps(doc, indent=1))
+    print(json.dumps({k: v for k, v in doc.items() if k not in ("calls", "header")}, indent=1))
+    print(f"wrote {out_p} sha256 {sha256(out_p)}")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()
